This vibrant event takes place in the heart of London, Greater London, and offers a unique opportunity for 20,000 runners to traverse both the City of London and the City of Westminster. Starting on the iconic Pall Mall and finishing by the historic Downing Street, participants will enjoy a scenic route that showcases the capital's most famous landmarks while uncovering its quirky and hidden gems.Whether you’re a seasoned runner or just looking to soak in the atmosphere, this half marathon celebrates London’s rich culture and heritage, making it a must-attend event for both runners and spectators alike. Organised by RunThrough Events, this vibrant race day offers a variety of distances to suit every runner's ambition, including a Half Marathon, 10 Miles, 10k, 5k, 1 Mile, and a Junior Race. Set in the heart of London, Greater London, the Lee Valley VeloPark is renowned for its gently undulating Moto GP styled circuit, making it an ideal venue to challenge yourself or achieve a personal best.With race times kicking off at 9:30 am for the Half Marathon and continuing through the morning, every participant will enjoy chip-timed mile splits to track their progress. As a historic Olympic venue from the London 2012 Games, the atmosphere is sure to inspire you as you tackle the tarmac and race towards the finish line. Whether you're a seasoned runner or a newcomer, this event promises to be a memorable experience filled with energy and encouragement. Don’t miss out on the excitement at Lee Valley Velo Park this January!Read moreLee Valley Velo Park - JanuarySat 24th January 2026Organiser's DescriptionJoin us at the Lee Valley Velo Park Half Marathon, 10 Miles, 10k, 5k, 1 Mile & Junior Race on Saturday 24th January 2026The Lee Valley VeloPark series returns on Saturday 24th January 2026, offering you a chance to run a Half Marathon, 10 Mile, 10k, 5k or Mile on this unique and gently undulating Moto GP styled circuit.The Lee Valley VeloPark’s one-mile floodlit road circuit is the perfect course to test yourself over a new distance or check your pacing with each mile split chip timed for you to view after the race. ## Event 3: https://findarace.com/events/the-wacky-run

- **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/the-wacky-run
- **Date**: The Wacky RunFrom Saturn RunningSun 22nd February 2026Time09:00LocationThe Last Hop, Unit 2, Thames Edge Court, Clarence Street, Staines, TW18 4SUPrice£18.00 - £35.00Races8 racesDistances5km, 10km, Half Marathon, Marathon, UltraBook NowShareShare this eventThe Wacky RunSun 22nd February 2026Copy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for a day filled with fun and nostalgia at The Wacky Run, organized by Saturn Running! Join us on Sunday, February 22, 2026, at The Last Hop, located at Unit 2, Thames Edge Court, Clarence Street, Staines, TW18 4SU, for an exhilarating time challenge event set along the stunning River Thames. Whether you’re a seasoned runner or just looking to enjoy a day out, this event welcomes all abilities, including walkers!Participants can choose their distance, with a minimum of 3km up to ultra-marathon distances, including 50km, while enjoying the vibrant atmosphere inspired by the beloved Wacky Races cartoon. With a fantastic aid station offering a variety of snacks and drinks, including vegan options, and exciting medals and distance pins to earn, this is an event you won't want to miss. Register now for just £23 as a member and secure your spot for an unforgettable day of running and community spirit!Read moreOrganiser's DescriptionAbout The EventTime to get nostalgic with our Wacky Run! I'm sure a lot of us can remember this fun cartoon and some of the crazy characters such as Dick Dastardly and Muttley, Penelope Pitstop and The Ant Hill Mob - our medal will be based on the iconic cartoon and will definitely be one which you will want to add to your collection! & time: Sunday 22nd of February 2026Official start time: 9amLocation: The Last Hop, Unit 2, Thames Edge Court, Clarence Street, Staines, TW18 4SUThe £23.00 entry price is available only to members. You can become a member by logging into your profile and purchasing one of the membership schemes. As well as the cheapest entry price, a member is guaranteed entry to the run and gets a mini-me medal pin at every event they attend!Distance & TimeThis is a 7 hour Time Challenge event which allows you full control over the distance you want to run. The minimum distance is 3km, which is part of a lap. A full lap is 4.37 miles or 7km (this is subject to change to a 3.28 mile loop depending on course conditions) and you can run as many, or as few, of these laps as you like subject to UK athletics age restrictions (please see the restrictions below). We will have a 3k turn point and 5k turn point within the lap which is available to allow our younger runners to comply with the UK Athletics distance rules. It is 3 laps for a half marathon, 6 for a marathon and 7 or over for an ultra, we also have a 50km distance available.CourseThe course along a beautiful stretch of the River Thames. It is fully signed so no navigational experience is required. The course is mainly well made paths and tarmac with a very short grass section. Road shoes are suitable all year round.All abilities are welcome at our super-inclusive events and we also welcome walkers too.Medals & PinsAs long as you complete 3km, you will be an official finisher and you will receive your Wacky Races medal!We also have distance pins available for anyone who runs a half-marathon distance or more. Pins available to collect are our Half-Marathon, Mega-Half, Marathon, 50km and Ultra pins. You will receive one pin on the ribbon of your medal to reflect your achievement.Goody BagAbsolutely! You have earned these replacement calories after all, so all our finishers get a great goody bag. You will have a selection of goodies to choose from. As well as our standard goody bag, we have a vegan goody bag too. Please let us know if you are vegan or if you prefer a vegan goody bag when you enter and we can have this ready for you.Aid TableWe have a fantastic aid table and drinks table at the start / finish of each lap. There is an incredible selection of drinks and savoury or sweet snacks such as crisps, cheese, fruit and cookies with vegan options also available.Special EventIf our event is a special event for you, for instance your 50th, 99th, 100th, 150th, 200th, 250th etc. marathon or half marathon then please let us know. We will provide you with a great number with your name and the event date on it.Venue FacilitiesToilets are available at the start/finish area inside the venue HQ. There are changing facilities available at the venue. These facilities will be open from 8am and will be available for use throughout the entire event.Car ParkingThere is parking available in Bridge Street Car Park in Staines, TW18 4TP.Race Day Instructions & Number Pick UpFinal race day instructions will be sent out via email in the week leading up to the event and will include full race day information. Number pick up is on the day at race registration before the start of the event.Other InformationThere will be somewhere to leave a bag at the start / finish area. However, this is not a secure bag and the items cannot be watched at all times so please do not leave anything valuable.Headphones are allowed.Our running events are held on public footpaths so we - **Price**: The Wacky RunFrom Saturn RunningDateSun 22nd February 2026Time09:00LocationThe Last Hop, Unit 2, Thames Edge Court, Clarence Street, Staines, TW18 4SU£18.00 - £35.00Races8 racesDistances5km, 10km, Half Marathon, Marathon, UltraBook NowShareShare this eventThe Wacky RunSun 22nd February 2026Copy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for a day filled with fun and nostalgia at The Wacky Run, organized by Saturn Running! Join us on Sunday, February 22, 2026, at The Last Hop, located at Unit 2, Thames Edge Court, Clarence Street, Staines, TW18 4SU, for an exhilarating time challenge event set along the stunning River Thames. Whether you’re a seasoned runner or just looking to enjoy a day out, this event welcomes all abilities, including walkers!Participants can choose their distance, with a minimum of 3km up to ultra-marathon distances, including 50km, while enjoying the vibrant atmosphere inspired by the beloved Wacky Races cartoon. With a fantastic aid station offering a variety of snacks and drinks, including vegan options, and exciting medals and distance pins to earn, this is an event you won't want to miss. Register now for just £23 as a member and secure your spot for an unforgettable day of running and community spirit!Read more

- **Date**: Richmond Park January 5k,10k and Half Marathon4.5·39 reviewsFrom The Fix Events UKSun 18th January 2026LocationLondon, Greater LondonPrice£25.00 - £30.00Races3 racesDistances5km, 10km, Half MarathonTop ratedThis is a popular eventLast chance to enterRace day is fast approachingPopularBooked 96 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventRichmond Park January 5k,10k and Half MarathonSun 18th January 20264.5·39 reviewsCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryKickstart your New Year with a fitness-focused adventure at the Richmond Park January 5k, 10k, and Half Marathon, taking place on Sunday, January 18th, 2026, in the beautiful surroundings of London, Greater London. Organised by The Fix Events UK, this event offers a fantastic opportunity to set your running goals for the year ahead. Whether you're a seasoned runner or a beginner, you can choose from three exciting race distances: the 5k, which is a single lap; the 10k, featuring two laps; or the challenging half marathon, consisting of four laps around the stunning Richmond Park.With limited spots available, this fully race chip-timed event promises not only a competitive environment but also a fun atmosphere filled with motivation. Participants will enjoy great entry benefits, including a stylish medal to commemorate your achievement, delicious sweets, refreshing drinks, and plenty of excitement along the way. So, lace up those running shoes, embrace the New Year, and join us for an unforgettable day of fitness and camaraderie!Read moreView allRichmond Park January 5k,10k and Half MarathonSun 18th January 2026Organiser's DescriptionStart your NEW YEAR with a fitness focus! Take on our superb Richmond Park 5k, 10k and half marathon around this gorgeous venue. Places are limited to just say yes, get excited and be part of this fully race chip timed event!A chance to lace up those shoes, get motivated, focus on training and run this event with us. If you require additional fluid between these points please bring your own.Please take care when running around the course as this is public land and local residents will be on the Park. Please always stay on the left unless otherwise guided. Be aware that there are deer in the Park and they occasionally cross the walking paths and roads.ZoomWhat's IncludedWhat's IncludedStunning medalFull race chip timingTrophiesDrinksSnacksVoucher pointsPartner BenefitsHow to Get ThereHow to Get ThereTrains either come in to Richmond and from there it's a 20-30 minute walk otherwise Mortlake is the closest station and about a 10 minute walk from Sheen.ParkingParkingPlease note parking is limited and please do your best to park before coming in via East Sheen Gate. You will see our orange gazebos here and come to our registration tents to collect your race pack.Event VideoReviewsVerified PurchaseA 'verified' purchase review means that we've verified that the person writing the review purchased their entry to this race on findarace.com.543214.539 reviewsJBJAKUB·a month ago·VerifiedI signed up for my first running event (It was the 10km version) and I was super nervous about how I was going to do. Luckily the organisers made it really easy with pre-race instructions on where to park, what to bring, the course map, etc... And they were super helpful on the day with good signage and lots of marshalls. I would highly recommend signing up!  It suggests trail shoes but I did it in Brooks Ghost 17's and found them a good fit for the race (It was a little wet from rain the previous days but we had good weather on the day). There's 1 muddy section which I can see becoming a little slippery if it's raining but it's manageable :)Read moreMJMichael·a month ago·VerifiedWell Organised   I spoke to 4 Marshals, all were very friendly, chatty and supportiveNMNick·2 months ago·VerifiedI did the 10k with my niece and had a great day, I have run many races before including half marathons, marathons and countless 10k's. It was my nieces first race and she loved it! A great atmosphere and scenic route made it a perfect run for us to do together.KGKasia·4 months ago·VerifiedFirst race I've done.  Route was lovely and perfect for  10k - i could see how it would get repetitive if I was doing a half marathon but it was great for 5k or a 10k  staff lovely and support great :)Peter·4 months agoI've done this half marathon twice now and its a great little event. The setup is cheap and cheerful with a very nice team running the show. Because of the loose surface and hills its a tough run and great training to get the fitness up.RWRichard·7 months ago·VerifiedThank you to the organiser for a lovely morning! This was my first 10k, and having only done Parkrun before I was a bit nervous doing a "proper" race with a number/timing chip. I really didn't need to be. It was a lovely atmosphere and the organisers and marshalls were so friendly and helpful. The course is a 5k loop, so 10k does two loops (there was a water station at the 5k mark) and half marathon does a 1k out and back at the start, followed by 4 laps. The medals are handed out at the finish line and you get a bottle of water and sweets at the end.  The weather was slightly rainy on the day. The organisers recommended trail shoes, but the course is largely tarmac/gravel with a little bit on grass, which was a little slippery in the weather, but decent road shoes are fine. Most of the course is a gentle uphill, with a steep downhill section.  Richmond Park is lovely, but it is very large! I thought it would be easy to reach from Richmond, but the race starts at Sheen Gate which is not very easy to get to on a Sunday morning.  Results were sent online almost instantly, and I loved being able to get my time as soon as the race finished.  I would recommend this race to anyone running their first 10k!Read moreStewart·7 months ago·VerifiedThis was my first half marathon and it was an awesome experience :)Beata·7 months agoThis was my first ever race. I was pleasantly surprised by how organised this event was. The venue was stunning, helped in part by the weather, and was a good balance of terrain and elevation. It was very smooth to register with clear instructions on where to go. One downside was how busy the course got.  As well as the general public - walkers, horse riders, cyclists, other runners etc. - the course got very busy when the 5K and 10K races got under way, and trying to navigate so many runners/walkers when you're on your final miles was a little tricky. The route was a series of laps, which has its updsides and downsides, but for the most part was OK. Nice medal and good T-shirt to remember the day too (t-shirt cost extra). From my very limited experience this was a good race.Read moreLCLiz·a year ago·VerifiedOasis for a sunday run. easy to park with a 10 minute walk. Dry route. Lovely crowd and organisers. Use it/try it!JPJean-Marie·a year ago·VerifiedThis was my first half marathon and the day was just perfect, sunny an suitably cool at least initially. The location is simply stunning! With my running team mate we had prepared and reviewed the course and practiced a few laps during our training. It helped motivate me past the 17th km which was our longest run The event was very well organised and not too crowded. The events team are nice and enthusiastic people, encouraging along the way and giving clear indications. The water station provided only water (not isotonic water, possibly do avoid adverse reactions). I would suggest they move to paper cups and space the waste buckets further from the pickup table. Getting to the race start by public transport wasn't easy : the start is not near tube or train stations or even bus routes. The wildlife  on display in the park added to the whole experience. There were a few things that could have worked better for me, but I still had an enjoyable experience. The course was very narrow and winding at the beginning which made for a very slow start. I spent my first km running all over the place to try and pass other people and I saw a few runners tripping and falling. Also, the first half of the loop is predominantly uphill, whilst the second half goes downhill. This may be a matter of preference, but I found the course quite hard (my overall time was quite a bit slower than expected). I think ideally a flatter route, or one where up and downs are spread more evenly throughout the loop would allow for quicker times. I must say the wind was very strong on the day, which made the uphills even more challenging. The water was provided in plastic cups rather than bottles, so I spilled most of it rather than drink it. And I would have loved for the medals to say half marathon. But shout out to the volunteers/organisers who tried really hard to get the people around to cheer you on at the end (there weren't too many). The course is undulating with the first half lap being up hill, the second half mostly down hill, with a little kicker before you reach the lap completion. The staff were extremely friendly.  Like others have said, 4 laps for a half marathon isn't ideal given the size of the park available, however, there are roads in use in the park and cyclists, cars, crossings that would be impossible to control for a running race.   I would have liked a medal specific to my race (the half marathon) rather than a generic medal given to competitors of the 5k, 10k, and half-marathon.   I think there should have been a member of staff at all key turning points, however, there were too few along the course in my opinion.   Better recovery foods could have been provided at the end of the race.  However, the day was a success and I would do the event again in future, knowing what it entails now.Read moreASAshley·3 years ago·VerifiedThe race was organised well at the start, and i had an amazing time, my issue was at the finish line you had no marshalls people were not guided to the finish line and ran past it which contributed to extra time on their timings. I ran the 10K and when I finished there was no sweets or drinks left, so all i could get was water, after running 10k i felt like there was nothing left for us, I could see other runners had sweet drinks and chocolates.Read morePierre·3 years ago·VerifiedGood turnout and great fun on a tricky and undulating course. I thought it was well organised, but signalisation could have been more clear with bigger signposting over the latter stages of the race. Richmond Park is a great place to run. For a half marathon it's a 4x 5km lap (and it's not like Richmond wouldn't fit a single lap as most other parks in London), the only food they offered at the end of the race were candy and lollipops (great for recovery -.-), not music or anything (not that I'd care much but hey at least they'd be trying), no bathrooms apart from the public ones which were unusable, and signalization was poor (for the half marathon it just assumed every lap was 5k and therefore there was a 0.3k margin of error by the start of lap 2, 0.6k by lap 3, etc. etc. Given it was just a 5k lap you could expect they would close the area but not the case, so apart from the 21k/10k/5k runners you would frequently encounter dogs, cyclists, running groups, etc. If you're really fond of medals, then sure why not, but if you just like running, I'd just go on any day and run by yourself. The only pros: staff & volunteers were very friendly, and Richmond is a nice park for running. - **Price**: Richmond Park January 5k,10k and Half Marathon4.5·39 reviewsFrom The Fix Events UKDateSun 18th January 2026LocationLondon, Greater London£25.00 - £30.00Races3 racesDistances5km, 10km, Half MarathonTop ratedThis is a popular eventLast chance to enterRace day is fast approachingPopularBooked 96 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventRichmond Park January 5k,10k and Half MarathonSun 18th January 20264.5·39 reviewsCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryKickstart your New Year with a fitness-focused adventure at the Richmond Park January 5k, 10k, and Half Marathon, taking place on Sunday, January 18th, 2026, in the beautiful surroundings of London, Greater London. Organised by The Fix Events UK, this event offers a fantastic opportunity to set your running goals for the year ahead. Whether you're a seasoned runner or a beginner, you can choose from three exciting race distances: the 5k, which is a single lap; the 10k, featuring two laps; or the challenging half marathon, consisting of four laps around the stunning Richmond Park.With limited spots available, this fully race chip-timed event promises not only a competitive environment but also a fun atmosphere filled with motivation. Participants will enjoy great entry benefits, including a stylish medal to commemorate your achievement, delicious sweets, refreshing drinks, and plenty of excitement along the way. So, lace up those running shoes, embrace the New Year, and join us for an unforgettable day of fitness and camaraderie!Read moreView allRichmond Park January 5k,10k and Half MarathonSun 18th January 2026

## Event 5: https://findarace.com/events/surrey-half-5k-and-2k-kids-race

- **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/surrey-half-5k-and-2k-kids-race
- **Date**: SURREY HALF MARATHON & 5K5.0·3 reviewsFrom RunThrough EventsSun 16th March 2025Time08:00LocationWoking, SurreyPrice£17.00 - £192.00Races4 racesDistances5km, Half Marathon, 10kmShareShare this eventSURREY HALF MARATHON & 5KSun 16th March 20255.0·3 reviewsCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an unforgettable day of running at the Surrey Half Marathon & 5K, organized by RunThrough Events! On Sunday, March 16th, 2025, Woking, Surrey will come alive with the energy of runners embracing the scenic closed road routes. Participants can choose from a thrilling half marathon, a vibrant 5K, or the exciting 2K Kids race, making it a perfect event for families and athletes of all levels.The half marathon features a professionally marked and measured single loop that showcases the beautiful countryside, offering unparalleled potential for personal bests. Runners in the 5K will enjoy a taste of the half marathon experience, starting and finishing under the main event gantry. Adding to the festive atmosphere, local bands will perform, and enthusiastic spectators will cheer you on every step of the way. With chip-timed results, a unique themed medal, and post-race goodies, this event promises to be a fantastic celebration of fitness and community. Don't miss out on this exciting opportunity to be part of the award-winning Surrey Half Marathon & 5K!Read moreSURREY HALF MARATHON & 5KSun 16th March 2025Organiser's DescriptionRUN THE SURREY HALF, 5K AND 2K KIDS RACE SUNDAY 16TH MARCH 2025We are excited to announce the return of the Surrey Half Marathon, 5k and 2k Kids race.You’ll enjoy an award-winning atmosphere in an accessible, central location, glorious scenery, and closed road routes.The Half Marathon boasts unparalleled PB potential and follows a professionally marked and measured single loop from central Woking into the surrounding countryside. The 5K features some of the Half Marathon’s best bits – including starting and finishing under the main event gantry.This picturesque race is made even more popular by live performances from local bands, aid stations and communities of cheering spectators, and boasts one of the fastest half marathon finish times on record!*PLEASE NOTE: DUE TO ROAD CLOSURES THERE IS A STRICT 3 HOUR CUT OFF TIME FOR THE HALF MARATHON!YOUR ENTRY WILL INCLUDE:Chip timed resultsUnique themed medal (to be revealed!)Free official event photosPost race goodiesCome and enjoy the day with us; we hope to see you there!Read moreRacesHalf Marathon£42.00CLOSED5k£32.00CLOSEDKids 2k Race£17.00CLOSEDGroup EntryAny Distance Combination£192.00 perCLOSEDTeam6BookHalf Marathon£42.00CLOSED5k£32.00CLOSEDKids 2k Race£17.00CLOSEDGroup EntryAny Distance Combination£192.00 perCLOSEDTeam6LocationLocationWoking Leisure Centre, Kingfield Road, Woking, GU22 9BAstart timesstart times5k: 8amHalf Marathon: 9amKids Race (2k): 9:20amReviewsVerified PurchaseA 'verified' purchase review means that we've verified that the person writing the review purchased their entry to this race on findarace.com.543215.03 reviewsAWAndrew·7 months ago·VerifiedWhat a fabulous half marathon! Plenty of amenities at the race village, plenty of helpers at bag drop. I think following the pacers in to the start pen, is a good approach. I would describe the 2025 route as rolling (not hilly). ## Event 6: https://findarace.com/events/cheshire-half-marathon

- **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/cheshire-half-marathon
- **Date**: Cheshire Half Marathon4.8·10 reviewsFrom RunThrough EventsSun 14th September 2025Time09:00LocationMacclesfield, CheshirePrice£42.00 - £182.00Races2 racesDistancesHalf MarathonShareShare this eventCheshire Half MarathonSun 14th September 20254.8·10 reviewsCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an exhilarating day of running at the Cheshire Half Marathon, set to take place on Sunday, September 14th, 2025, in the picturesque Macclesfield, Cheshire. Organised by RunThrough Events, this exciting race will be hosted at the beautiful Capesthorne Hall, providing a stunning backdrop for runners aiming to achieve their personal bests. The notoriously flat and fast course runs through the charming rural roads of Cheshire, making it perfect for participants of all abilities.Whether you’re a seasoned runner or just starting out, the Cheshire Half Marathon promises an unforgettable experience with chip-timed results, free parking, post-race refreshments, and complimentary official event photos. Don't miss out on this fantastic opportunity to be part of a vibrant running community and challenge yourself on a fully licensed and officially measured half marathon route. Mark your calendars and join us for a memorable race day at Capesthorne Hall!Read moreCheshire Half MarathonSun 14th September 2025Organiser's DescriptionTHE CHESHIRE HALF MARATHON RETURNS TO CAPESTHORNE HALL ON SUNDAY 14TH SEPTEMBER 2025Returning to Capesthorne Hall, this notoriously flat and fast course provides the perfect opportunity to a personal best race over the half marathon distance in 2025. The Cheshire Half starts and finishes outside the stunning Capesthorne Hall and runs around the flat and rural closed roads of Cheshire, this Run Britain/UKA licensed, officially measured Cheshire Half Marathon distance road race is open to runners of all abilities.(Please note there’s a 3hr cut off on this race due to road closures in place)Entry includes:Chip timed resultsFree parkingPost race refreshmentsFree official event photosRacesHalf Marathon£42.00SOLD OUTGroup Booking (6 People)£182.00 perSOLD OUTTeam6BookHalf Marathon£42.00SOLD OUTGroup Booking (6 People)£182.00 perSOLD OUTTeam6Route and elevationRoute and elevationWe will have a lead bike and plenty of marshals as well as mile signs and arrows all around the course .The course is 1 lap.Please familiarise yourself with the course map before you start.ZoomLocationLocationCongleton Rd, Siddington, Macclesfield SK11 9JYon the dayon the dayToiletsPortable toilets are available in the event village. If you think you’re going to be using the loos then please give yourself plenty of time.RefreshmentsThere will be water provided at 5k, 10k, 15k and at the finish.ReviewsVerified PurchaseA 'verified' purchase review means that we've verified that the person writing the review purchased their entry to this race on findarace.com.543214.810 reviewsJade·4 months ago·VerifiedGreat race, good course. Mainly flat but some very very light inclines in the last few miles.  Well organised, staff and marshals all really friendly.  Good amount of refuelling stations.  Not many spectators as running through country roads so if you need moral support may not be for you!MAMohammad·2 years ago·VerifiedEnjoyed this race! Definitely one of my favourite runsLGLuke·2 years ago·VerifiedIt was a nice run with no extreme uphills or downhills. The heat of the day made it more challenging but overall a nice run.JBJacob·3 years ago·VerifiedGreat race and very well organised! For my first half marathon everything went very smoothly and was very well organisedPHPaul·3 years ago·VerifiedGreat organisation, lots of friendly marshals and the course was grand to boot! Highly recommend as a flat n’ fast halfGeoff·5 years agoReally well organised. With all the challenges that Covid presented them, the organisers of this event did a first class job. The course was flat & fast on closed roads. There were lots of clearly visible marshals and plenty of water on offer.  I loved the whole event.Lucy·6 years ago·VerifiedGreat Event. Having never run in an organised event before I found it was great.  Every aspect really. Only bad thing really was the walk to and from the car park field which was so wet everyones trainers were soaked before you even started the race!DFDavid·6 years ago·VerifiedDef 1 for a pb or any one new to half’s. Good well thought out race ,friendly marshals ,good T and goodies  Course a few undulating bits no hills so good for new half marathon runners or pb chasers  Down side field where parked got bit muddy at entrance exit when leaving  Could do with being plastic freeSOSarah·6 years ago·VerifiedCountryside Delight. The course for the Cheshire half was stunning. Lots of beautiful houses and greenery edging windy country lanes. A few 'undulations' rather than hills and a relatively smooth running surface makes for a potential PB race time. It was my first half marathon and I will definitely be back next year to improve my time.Congrats on your first half!findarace.com (UK)JCJoanna·6 years ago·VerifiedBeautiful surroundings. My first official half marathon. Was very impressed with the location and organisation. The Marshall were very encouraging and the medal/t-shirt was spot on. Taking place from Saturday, October 10th to Sunday, October 11th, 2026, this vibrant event is set against the stunning backdrop of Bournemouth, Dorset. Participants can look forward to an exciting selection of races, including the fast and flat Bournemouth Half Marathon, the exhilarating Supersonic 10K, and fun-filled junior races of 2K and 1.5K for the younger runners.For those just starting their running journey, the Kids’ Kilometre offers a fantastic introduction to the sport, while the popular Supernova 5K invites runners to light up the night with head torches and brilliant fluorescent outfits as they traverse the iconic promenade and piers. ## Event 8: https://findarace.com/events/sex-and-scandal-10k-running-tour

- **Date**: Sex and Scandal 10k running tourFrom Secret London RunsSat 14th February 2026Time13:00LocationLondonPrice£24.00Races1 raceDistances10kmLast chance to enterRace day is fast approachingBook NowShareShare this eventSex and Scandal 10k running tourSat 14th February 2026Copy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryJoin Secret London Runs for a unique and exhilarating experience at the Sex and Scandal 10k running tour on Saturday, February 14th, 2026, in the vibrant heart of London. This isn't just any run; it's a captivating 10km journey through the city's intriguing and cheeky history, where you'll learn about some of London's most scandalous figures and moments. From the infamous King known as "too randy to rule" to the fascinating origins of the condom, this guided tour will take you to the city's most notorious destinations.No matter your pace, every runner is welcome to join in on the fun, ensuring that no one gets left behind. Whether you're flying solo, bringing friends, or sharing the experience with a partner, you'll enjoy an unforgettable adventure through London's erotic past. The Victoria Park half was extremely well organised with plenty of helpful volunteers. Unlike some venues there was plenty of portable toilet facilities and the PA provided clear instructions on the day. Only downside was no toilets on the route not so much of an issue for me doing 10k but for people doing half marathon some were struggling.RMRob·a year ago·VerifiedA very well organised race, great course to PB a half marathon. Easy to follow and well marked to keep the route clear of public.DNDonn·a year ago·VerifiedVery well set up, no frills. 10K pacers for 40, 45, 50, 55, 60, 65 mins!SSSeb·a year ago·VerifiedWas very good. Organised by RunThrough Events, this exciting race offers the perfect opportunity to enjoy a fast and flat course set in the beautiful surroundings of London’s Victoria Park. Whether you're a seasoned runner looking to achieve a personal best or a beginner eager to tackle a new distance, this event has something for everyone.The race schedule kicks off at 9:30 am with the Half Marathon, followed by the 10k at 10:00 am, and rounding off with the 5k starting at 11:15 am. Participants will receive a unique medal, free race photos, and post-race refreshments to celebrate their achievements. ## Event 10: https://findarace.com/events/the-racoon-rocket-run

- **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/the-racoon-rocket-run
- **Date**: The Racoon Rocket RunFrom Saturn RunningSat 14th February 2026Time09:00LocationThe Last Hop, Unit 2, Thames Edge Court, Clarence Street, Staines, TW18 4SUPrice£18.00 - £35.00Races8 racesDistances5km, 10km, 10 Mile, Half Marathon, Marathon, UltraLast chance to enterRace day is fast approachingPopularBooked 5 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventThe Racoon Rocket RunSat 14th February 2026Copy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an unforgettable running experience at The Racoon Rocket Run, organized by Saturn Running! This exciting event takes place on Saturday, February 14, 2026, at The Last Hop in Staines, TW18 4SU, along the picturesque stretch of the River Thames. Participants can choose their own adventure with a 7-hour Time Challenge, offering distances starting from a minimum of 3km to ultra distances of 50km. With laps measuring 4.37 miles (or potentially 3.28 miles depending on conditions), there's something for everyone, whether you're a seasoned marathoner or a casual walker.Join us for a day filled with fun, camaraderie, and the chance to earn a charming raccoon medal! Enjoy a fantastic aid table stocked with refreshments and snacks, including vegan options. Plus, finishers who complete at least 3km will receive a goody bag filled with treats to celebrate their achievement. With distance pins available for those who conquer half-marathon distances and beyond, The Racoon Rocket Run promises to be a memorable event for runners of all abilities. Don’t miss out on this special occasion – register now and make it a day to remember!Read moreOrganiser's DescriptionAbout The EventCome and join us for The Racoon Rocket Run and earn yourself a beautiful medal with an adorable picture of a fluffy racoon on! What more could you ask for?? Well we also include an amazing aid table which you can help yourselves to and distance pins for anyone who runs a half marathon or more! & time: Saturday 14th of February 2026Official start time 9amLocation: The Last Hop, Unit 2, Thames Edge Court, Clarence Street, Staines, TW18 4SUThe £23.00 entry price is available only to members. You can become a member by logging into your profile and purchasing one of the membership schemes. As well as the cheapest entry price, a member is guaranteed entry to the run and gets a mini-me medal pin at every event they attend!Distance & TimeThis is a 7 hour Time Challenge event which allows you full control over the distance you want to run. The minimum distance is 3km, which is part of a lap. A full lap is 4.37 miles or 7km (this is subject to change to a 3.28 mile loop depending on course conditions) and you can run as many, or as few, of these laps as you like subject to UK athletics age restrictions (please see the restrictions below). We will have a 3k turn point and 5k turn point within the lap which is available to allow our younger runners to comply with the UK Athletics distance rules. It is 3 laps for a half marathon, 6 for a marathon and 7 or over for an ultra, we also have a 50km distance available.CourseThe course along a beautiful stretch of the River Thames. It is fully signed so no navigational experience is required. The course is mainly well made paths and tarmac with a very short grass section. Road shoes are suitable all year round.All abilities are welcome at our super-inclusive events and we also welcome walkers too.Medals & PinsAs long as you complete 3km, you will be an official finisher and you will receive your racoon medal!We also have distance pins available for anyone who runs a half-marathon distance or more. Pins available to collect are our Half-Marathon, Mega-Half, Marathon, 50km and Ultra pins. You will receive one pin on the ribbon of your medal to reflect your achievement.Goody BagAbsolutely! You have earned these replacement calories after all, so all our finishers get a great goody bag. You will have a selection of goodies to choose from. As well as our standard goody bag, we have a vegan goody bag too. Please let us know if you are vegan or if you prefer a vegan goody bag when you enter and we can have this ready for you.Aid TableWe have a fantastic aid table and drinks table at the start / finish of each lap. There is an incredible selection of drinks and savoury or sweet snacks such as crisps, cheese, fruit and cookies with vegan options also available.Special EventIf our event is a special event for you, for instance your 50th, 99th, 100th, 150th, 200th, 250th etc. marathon or half marathon then please let us know. We will provide you with a great number with your name and the event date on it.Venue FacilitiesToilets are available at the start/finish area inside the venue HQ. There are changing facilities available at the venue. These facilities will be open from 8am and will be available for use throughout the entire event.Car ParkingThere is parking available in Bridge Street Car Park in Staines, TW18 4TP.Race Day Instructions & Number Pick UpFinal race day instructions will be sent out via email in the week leading up to the event and will include full race day information. Number pick up is on the day at race registration before the start of the event.Other InformationThere will be somewhere to leave a bag at the start / finish area. However, this is not a secure bag and the items cannot be watched at all times so please do not leave anything valuable.Headphones are allowed.Our running events are held on public footpaths so we would be grateful if all runners were considerate of other path users.Read moreRaces10kmSat 14th February 2026 (09:00)£27.00Trail Running Association (TRA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than ## Event 11: https://findarace.com/events/runthrough-queen-elizabeth-olympic-park-5k-10k

- **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/runthrough-queen-elizabeth-olympic-park-5k-10k
- **Date**: QE Olympic Park 5k & 10k4.4·28 reviewsFrom RunThrough EventsSat 7th February 2026Time09:30LocationLondonPrice£32.00Races2 racesDistances5km, 10kmTop ratedThis is a popular eventLast chance to enterRace day is fast approachingPopularBooked 37 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventQE Olympic Park 5k & 10kSat 7th February 20264.4·28 reviewsCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an exhilarating day of running at the QE Olympic Park 5k & 10k, organized by RunThrough Events! On Saturday, February 7th, 2026, join fellow running enthusiasts at the stunning Queen Elizabeth Olympic Park in London for a chip-timed race that promises both excitement and achievement. Whether you’re a seasoned runner or just starting out, you can choose between the fast and flat 5k or the invigorating 10k course, both of which are designed to inspire and motivate.Starting at 9:30 am, the event features well-marked kilometer flags, enthusiastic volunteers to cheer you on, and a unique themed medal waiting for you at the finish line. With a terrain of smooth tarmac and minimal elevation, this is the perfect opportunity to chase your personal best while enjoying the beautiful surroundings of South Park. Don’t miss out on this fantastic event filled with community spirit, fun, and a chance to earn some great goodies. Mark your calendars and prepare for a memorable experience at the QE Olympic Park 5k & 10k! We can't wait to see you there!Read moreQE Olympic Park 5k & 10kSat 7th February 2026Organiser's DescriptionJoin us for the Queen Elizabeth Olympic Park 5k & 10k on Saturday 7th February 2026Come and run a chip timed 5k or 10k through the incredible grounds of the South Park in London’s Queen Elizabeth Olympic Park. This course is not only flat and fast, but also inspiring. The RunThrough Queen Elizabeth Olympic Park 5k & 10k event is a great opportunity for a quick time and a fun morning out with a unique themed medal waiting for you at the finish!Included is a themed medal, water and goodies! Each kilometre is accurately marked out with distance flags and we have plenty of helpers to keep you on track and to encourage you on your way to the finish. Come and enjoy the day with us; we hope to see you there!Race Distance & Start Times09:30 am - 5k & 10k5k | Elevation - 3m | Laps - 2 | Terrain - Tarmac10k | Elevation - 3m | Laps - 4 | Terrain - TarmacRace End11:00 amRead moreRaces5kSat 7th February 2026 (09:30)£32.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basket10kSat 7th February 2026 (09:30)£32.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketBook5kSat 7th February 2026 (09:30)£32.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basket10kSat 7th February 2026 (09:30)£32.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketRoute and elevationRoute and elevationThere will be marshals all around the course as well as km signs and arrows. The course is two laps for the 5k and four laps for the 10k on the roads/paths in the park.Please be aware that there will be spectators and members of the public using the park.What's IncludedWhat's IncludedUnique Themed MedalFree Race PhotosChip-timed resultsPost Race GoodiesHow to Get ThereHow to Get TherePARKING/TRAVELWe strongly recommend getting public transport for this event. The nearest available train stations are Stratford, Stratford International, Hackney Wick, and Pudding Mill Lane.LocationLocationTimber Lodge Cafe, 1A Honour Lea Avenue, London, E20 1DYfacilitiesfacilitiesBaggageWe will have a secure area where you can leave your baggage. We recommend you bring as little baggage as possible. Your baggage tag is attached to your race number, you simply tear it off and attach to your bag. To collect, all you have to do show you number to the baggage tent staff and they will help you find your baggage.RefreshmentsThere will be water provided at the 5k point of the race, and water at the finish. There will also be goodies & flapjacks waiting for you at the finish.Event VideoReviewsVerified PurchaseA 'verified' purchase review means that we've verified that the person writing the review purchased their entry to this race on findarace.com.543214.428 reviewsEREugene·a month ago·VerifiedGood raceJTJamila·7 months ago·VerifiedLoved it!KHKerry·7 months ago·VerifiedLoved every second of it. After a horrific ankle surgery last year (not related to running), I started intensive rehabilitative physiotherapy. Working with the therapists, I went from total immobility to a knee scooter to crutches to limping to walking. I was warned that I might never run again. It was a holistic process (joint / muscle problems are never isolated). Now I do my physio exercises every day as habits, I recover / rest (so important!) and I can now do 3 ‘sad-looking’ (but great-feeling!) physio-walk-jogs per week, with lots of resting and physio exercises in between. I was very nervous about being ‘too slow’ or ‘too limpy’ for a 5k, too awkward because I used to be ‘fast’ and fully able. Long story short (too late, sorrrry) if you feel you can’t because you’re too slow/awkward/disabled — just be vulnerable and tell a physiotherapist. Go in tiny steps. Then sign up to a RunThrough 5k as your first 5k post-disablement!!! I was so embarrassed and nervous at first, then the race started and I truly didn’t care that I was the last person of all the runners. I’m disabled but just took it very slowly and steady exactly like in physiotherapy and as advised — and listened to my ankle and my body. The organisers were SO amazing, at a few points I cried (so cringey!) and the organisers were so kind and let me get on with things — they obvs didn’t know that (a) I’m a crier anyway and (b) these are like tears of immense relief that I can even move and (c) it’s very very powerful and emotional as a woman to finally achieve this kind of freedom of movement. I was treated equally. If / when they saw me doing my jog-physio-walk they cheered me on, just like everyone else. When you’re disabled, you don’t want special treatment you just want to be treated equally and that’s what happened for me. If you (and your doctor / physiotherapist / trainer) agree that you’re ready — as long as you ‘go slow’ — then you won’t find a more supportive 5k. Even if you’re disabled like me, you get that medal at the end and it’s not about the time or the distance, it’s that you feel seen for your abilities instead of pitied for your disabilities.Read moreMichael·10 months ago·VerifiedNice flat route and great if you are aiming for a PB. However, if you are on the faster side the course doesn’t work well as after the first lap you are overtaking packs of people which meant it was hard to get into a rhythm and race properly! It was previously 2 laps in a different location which seemed to work better.GSGrace·10 months ago·VerifiedSuch a fun morning! The event was organised very well and all the staff were friendly and helpful. A real supportive atmosphere. The only thing I could suggest is having signage from Stratford Station (via Westfield) to make it easier to find the meeting point.JMJodie·10 months ago·VerifiedHad a great time but got confused with the route in the 10k. I was alone at a few points too which was a little uncomfortable. Would also help for some signage to show where the start is.LMLisa·a year ago·VerifiedWell organized pavement run with a little bit of incline. There was no signage to find the venue (start).JBJeff·a year ago·VerifiedVery well organised and a really good race if you are looking for a PB or coming along to join in the fun and keep your fitness going. Just go for it.James·3 years ago·VerifiedGreat location but confusing course layout (the water station also pulls you to the wrong side)  Ran out of medals towards the endAPAna·3 years ago·Verifiedwell organised, could have organised one more water station as it was quite hot weather.  unfortunately, by the time I finished all the jubilee medals had gone and was promised one would be sent to those who didn't get it.KDKlaudiusz·3 years ago·VerifiedWas a my first run  I really enjoyed the atmosphereSGSarah·3 years ago·VerifiedSmall, well organised race in an amazing place. Not flat though - I clocked 72 metres of ascent, with a short tough one at the end of each lap.MTMichael·4 years agoGreat course for a PB, pretty flat and laps so feels like a short course.  Staff are super organised and highly recommend to anyoneCCCatherine·4 years ago·VerifiedWell organised, really relaxed atmosphere, a bit like a Park Run event.  All of the event staff were super friendly and helpful.  Suitable for more and less experienced runners. Great support from the Marshalls on the way round the course. Nice medal and tasty snacks at the end. Really enjoyed it.JWJulie·5 years ago·VerifiedGreat return to racing. My first run since lockdown. Super well organised. Felt very safe. Staggered starts were a bonus - was nice to have so much space. Great venue. Would highly recommend.Henry·6 years ago·VerifiedEnergised simple fun. Was a fun course in a beautiful part of London but a short lap IMO. All staff motivational and positive which was nice, medal and snacks after. Not sure I would do this race again but fun to do!FAFelix·6 years ago·Verifiedexpensive for what's provided. On Saturday, February 7th, 2026, join fellow running enthusiasts at the stunning Queen Elizabeth Olympic Park in London for a chip-timed race that promises both excitement and achievement. Whether you’re a seasoned runner or just starting out, you can choose between the fast and flat 5k or the invigorating 10k course, both of which are designed to inspire and motivate.Starting at 9:30 am, the event features well-marked kilometer flags, enthusiastic volunteers to cheer you on, and a unique themed medal waiting for you at the finish line. ## Event 12: https://findarace.com/events/the-rubber-ducky-dash

- **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/the-rubber-ducky-dash
- **Date**: The Rubber Ducky DashFrom Saturn RunningSat 21st February 2026Time09:00LocationThe Last Hop, Unit 2, Thames Edge Court, Clarence Street, Staines, TW18 4SUPrice£18.00 - £35.00Races8 racesDistances5km, 10km, 10 Mile, Half Marathon, Marathon, UltraPopularBooked 16 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventThe Rubber Ducky DashSat 21st February 2026Copy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for a quacking good time at The Rubber Ducky Dash, organized by Saturn Running! Mark your calendars for Saturday, February 21, 2026, for an exciting day of fun and fitness set along the picturesque banks of the River Thames in Staines. This unique event invites participants to take part in a 7-hour Time Challenge, where you can run a minimum of 3km or as many laps as you desire, with the option for distances up to 50km. Whether you’re a seasoned runner or just starting out, everyone is welcome to join in on the fun!Each participant will earn a delightful rubber duck-themed medal and receive their very own rubber ducky to take home. With a fantastic aid station, goody bags filled with treats, and special recognition for milestone achievements, The Rubber Ducky Dash promises an unforgettable experience for all. Plus, members enjoy exclusive entry discounts and perks! Don't miss out on this joyous celebration of running—register today and get ready to waddle your way to victory!Read moreOrganiser's DescriptionAbout The EventDue to the success of our Rebellious Rubber Ducky Run in 2025 we are holding our second event inspired by the adorable bath toys! At this event, not only will you earn a super cute rubber duck themed medal but also, your very own rubber ducky to take home with you!! & time: Saturday 21st of February 2026Official start time: 9amLocation: The Last Hop, Unit 2, Thames Edge Court, Clarence Street, Staines, TW18 4SUThe £23.00 entry price is available only to members. You can become a member by logging into your profile and purchasing one of the membership schemes. As well as the cheapest entry price, a member is guaranteed entry to the run and gets a mini-me medal pin at every event they attend!Distance & TimeThis is a 7 hour Time Challenge event which allows you full control over the distance you want to run. The minimum distance is 3km, which is part of a lap. A full lap is 4.37 miles or 7km (this is subject to change to a 3.28 mile loop depending on course conditions) and you can run as many, or as few, of these laps as you like subject to UK athletics age restrictions (please see the restrictions below). We will have a 3k turn point and 5k turn point within the lap which is available to allow our younger runners to comply with the UK Athletics distance rules. It is 3 laps for a half marathon, 6 for a marathon and 7 or over for an ultra, we also have a 50km distance available.CourseThe course along a beautiful stretch of the River Thames. It is fully signed so no navigational experience is required. The course is mainly well made paths and tarmac with a very short grass section. Road shoes are suitable all year round.All abilities are welcome at our super-inclusive events and we also welcome walkers too.Medals & PinsAs long as you complete 3km, you will be an official finisher and you will receive your rubber ducky medal!We also have distance pins available for anyone who runs a half-marathon distance or more. Pins available to collect are our Half-Marathon, Mega-Half, Marathon, 50km and Ultra pins. You will receive one pin on the ribbon of your medal to reflect your achievement.Goody BagAbsolutely! You have earned these replacement calories after all, so all our finishers get a great goody bag. You will have a selection of goodies to choose from. As well as our standard goody bag, we have a vegan goody bag too. Please let us know if you are vegan or if you prefer a vegan goody bag when you enter and we can have this ready for you.Aid TableWe have a fantastic aid table and drinks table at the start / finish of each lap. There is an incredible selection of drinks and savoury or sweet snacks such as crisps, cheese, fruit and cookies with vegan options also available.Special EventIf our event is a special event for you, for instance your 50th, 99th, 100th, 150th, 200th, 250th etc. marathon or half marathon then please let us know. We will provide you with a great number with your name and the event date on it.Venue FacilitiesToilets are available at the start/finish area inside the venue HQ. There are changing facilities available at the venue. These facilities will be open from 8am and will be available for use throughout the entire event.Car ParkingThere is parking available in Bridge Street Car Park in Staines, TW18 4TP.Race Day Instructions & Number Pick UpFinal race day instructions will be sent out via email in the week leading up to the event and will include full race day information. Number pick up is on the day at race registration before the start of the event.Other InformationThere will be somewhere to leave a bag at the start / finish area. However, this is not a secure bag and the items cannot be watched at all times so please do not leave anything valuable.Headphones are allowed.Our running events are held on public footpaths so we would be grateful if all runners were considerate of other path users.Read moreRaces10kmSat 21st February 2026 (09:00)£27.00Trail Running Association (TRA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than Mark your calendars for Saturday, February 21, 2026, for an exciting day of fun and fitness set along the picturesque banks of the River Thames in Staines. This unique event invites participants to take part in a 7-hour Time Challenge, where you can run a minimum of 3km or as many laps as you desire, with the option for distances up to 50km. Whether you’re a seasoned runner or just starting out, everyone is welcome to join in on the fun!Each participant will earn a delightful rubber duck-themed medal and receive their very own rubber ducky to take home. With a fantastic aid station, goody bags filled with treats, and special recognition for milestone achievements, The Rubber Ducky Dash promises an unforgettable experience for all. Plus, members enjoy exclusive entry discounts and perks! Don't miss out on this joyous celebration of running—register today and get ready to waddle your way to victory!Read more

- **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/magna-carta
- **Date**: Magna Carta Winter4.3·3 reviewsFrom HermesRunningSat 21st February 2026Time08:30LocationWraysbury Skiff & Punting Club  Riverside  Egham  Surrey TW20 0AAPrice£37.36 - £58.59Races3 racesDistances10km, Half Marathon, MarathonTop ratedThis is a popular eventPopularBooked 16 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventMagna Carta WinterSat 21st February 20264.3·3 reviewsCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an unforgettable running experience at the Magna Carta Winter event, organized by HermesRunning! Set against the stunning backdrop of Runnymede, Surrey, this event will take place on Saturday, February 21, 2026. Participants can choose from three exciting race distances: the 10km, Half Marathon, and Marathon, all of which start near the historic site where the Magna Carta was signed in 1215.The 10km race kicks off at 9:15 AM and features a scenic out-and-back course along the picturesque River Thames, while the Half Marathon begins at 9:00 AM, offering a mix of loops and out-and-back sections. For the marathon enthusiasts, the race starts at 8:30 AM and comprises five laps along the same beautiful route. Each distance promises a flat and fast experience, making it perfect for both seasoned runners and newcomers alike. With well-stocked checkpoints and a supportive atmosphere, the Magna Carta Winter event is sure to be a highlight of the running calendar! Sign up today and be part of this historic race!Read moreMagna Carta WinterSat 21st February 2026Organiser's DescriptionThe Magna Cartaracesarebased in scenic Runnymede (Surrey), and they all start right near the site where the treaty of Magna Carta was agreed by King John of England and signed on 15th June 1215.All the races will start on the river Thames next to the Wraysbury Skiff & Punting Club ((Race HQ). The routesare out / back lapped courses on one of the most scenic parts of the river Thames and it's a flat and fast course.10KM Route - starts at 9.15am will be x1 -1.0 mile out / back loop towards Staines Bridge (turnaround point by Runnymede Hotel) followed by x1 out / back 5.27-mile section to Old Windsor Lock (turnaround point) and back to the start at the Wraysbury Skiff Club HQ. Distance for the 10km will be 6.27 miles, 10.09 kms (allowing for variances in GPS devices).Half Marathon Route - starts at 9.00am will be x2 -1.3 mile out / back loops towards Staines Bridge (turnaround point by Runnymede Hotel) followed by 2 out / back 5.27-mile sections turning around at the Old Windsor Lock then back to the start at the Wraysbury Skiff Club HQ. Distance for the half marathon will be13.14 miles (allowing for variances in GPS devices.Marathon Route - starts at 8.30am it will be x5 -5.27-mile sections, to Old Windsor Lock (turnaround point) and back to the start at the Wraysbury Skiff Club HQ.Distance for the marathon will be 26.35 miles (allowing for variances in GPS devices).The turnaround points for the Marathon, Half and 10Km will all be fully marked and marshalledand theCheck Points at the CP1 Start / Finish and CP2 Old Windsor Lock will be very well stocked with a selection of energy drinks and food.Runners World rated this course - **Date**: CRYSTAL PALACE 5K 10K, HALF MARATHON & JUNIORS4.7·10 reviewsFrom RunThrough EventsSun 25th January 2026Time09:15LocationLondonPrice£12.00 - £36.00Races4 racesDistances5km, 10km, Half MarathonTop ratedThis is a popular eventLast chance to enterRace day is fast approachingPopularBooked 13 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventCRYSTAL PALACE 5K 10K, HALF MARATHON & JUNIORSSun 25th January 20264.7·10 reviewsCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an exhilarating running experience at the CRYSTAL PALACE 5K, 10K, HALF MARATHON & JUNIORS, organized by RunThrough Events! On Sunday, January 25th, 2026, join fellow runners in the picturesque Crystal Palace Park, located in the heart of London, Greater London. This vibrant event features races for all ages and abilities, including a 5K, 10K, Half Marathon, and exciting junior runs. The course winds through the scenic park on Norwood Ridge, providing stunning views of Central London, Greenwich, and the North Downs. Participants will enjoy a unique themed medal, complimentary water, sponsored goodies, and free race photos to capture the fun. Don't miss this fantastic opportunity to kickstart your running journey and create unforgettable memories amidst the iconic backdrop of Crystal Palace Park!Read moreCRYSTAL PALACE 5K 10K, HALF MARATHON & JUNIORSSun 25th January 2026Organiser's DescriptionCRYSTAL PALACE PARK 5K, 10K, HALF MARATHON & JUNIOR RUN ON 25TH JANUARY 2026Get into the running spirit and come and join us for a 5k,10k, Half Marathon or junior race at Crystal Palace Park located in the South-East London suburb of Crystal Palace on Sunday 25TH JANUARY 2026The park is situated on the Norwood Ridge, offering views of Central London, Greenwich and the North Downs. This undulating route in the beautiful park is the perfect opportunity for a fun morning run, even passing a few dinosaurs along the course!Included in your entry:Unique themed medalWaterSponsored goodiesFree race photosRaces5k£30.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basket10k£32.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketHalf Marathon£36.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketJunior Race£12.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketBook5k£30.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basket10k£32.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketHalf Marathon£36.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketJunior Race£12.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketRoute and elevationRoute and elevationThere will be marshals all around the course as well as km signs and arrows. The course is one lap for the 5k, two laps for the 10k and 4 laps for the Half Marathon on the roads/paths in the park.There WILL be pedestrians on the paths. Headphones are not permitted as we ask you to pay close attention to everything around you. Please be aware of people, cyclists and dogs.SpectatorsYou are free to wander around the park so you can see where the competitors will be running. It is lapped so you will see them on numerous occasions.LocationLocationCrystal Palace Parade, London SE19 2GAevent logisticsevent logisticsStart Times10:00am - Half Marathon10:30am - 10k10:30am - 5k12:30pm - JuniorsBaggageThere will be an informal bag drop area where you can leave your belongings whilst you run.ToiletsThere will be portaloos within the event village for runners and spectators to use.RefreshmentsThere will be water provided at the 5k point of the race, and there will also be water available at the finish along with plenty of goodies for when you finish 🙂During the raceThere will be marshals all around the course as well as km signs and arrows. Please be aware that there will be spectators and members of the public using the park and dogs are not permitted within the athletics track.ReviewsVerified PurchaseA 'verified' purchase review means that we've verified that the person writing the review purchased their entry to this race on findarace.com.543214.710 reviewsMark·a month ago·VerifiedGreat atmosphere, really well organised. The guy who was emcee-ing was brilliant.CWCyrita·a month ago·VerifiedCouldn’t have asked for a better first race!Rodrigo·a year ago·VerifiedThat day was awesome!!  I did half marathon!!! 🔥🔥Matt·a year ago·VerifiedI did the Half Marathon on the day. I was a bit trepidatious on arrival as I had not had any further communications since booking (despite emailing and asking). Things like car parking and start times were still up in the air. When I arrived they did not have mine or my wife's details on file, so I had to show my booking reference and then they gave my wife a half marathon number instead of a 10K number. Sounds all very negative and it was a bit but the race was nice, tougher than I imagined and I bought a nice hat from a vendor so all good in the end.Read moreMichael·4 years agoWell organized. Arrived and easy to see where to go to get my bib and drop off my bag.  Arrived a little early and no wait for porta potties.   Organized warm up and announcements for starting based on estimated finish. Volunteers at about 4 key spots on the 3ish mile loop of the park to cheer you on and make sure you didn't eat it turning a slippery corner. Water only in one place, but you run by it 3/4 times in the half marathon, so it's all you need. The course was a combination of road, hard gravel, and mud in December weather, but only a few places where big puddles were inevitable. My phone Strava said it was 13.51 miles, so a little bigger discrepancy than I normally see.  Also good to note it was 797 feet of vertical gain, so be ready to be running either up or down hills.  Only issue I saw was lack of mileage signs. There is also a 5k and 10k going on, so lots of signs, but I was checking distance and the only sign(s) I saw that even came close to what we could be at was a 2 mile sign, and a 12 mile sign. Best to have GPS on you or you would have no idea how far you have gone or your pacing. Finishing medal and snacks were great.Read moreLHLauren·5 years ago·VerifiedAbsolutely amazing. You must come and try it I'm not much of a jogged but I jogged most of the time it was so much funLWLizzie·5 years ago·VerifiedA very pleasant run. I had never run a 10k before and was on my own so felt a bit nervous about running through a notoriously hilly terrain on a cold January day. However as I made my way over to the event village and saw the buzz and hear the enthusiasm and support from the girl with the microphone I felt at home. The race was so well supported from the sponsors, the group warm up to all the volunteers taking time out to cheer on a group of people going for a park run. The route was brilliant - a mixture of light hills, slopes and flat terrain both on pavement and grass. Periodically throughout the course there were motivational signs from Run Through and hearing the sounds of the guy with the steel drums was encouraging. I really enjoyed the run, even for a novice like me, and as I crossed the finish line and heard my name called out over the tannoy, and later on enjoying a free flapjack, I felt really glad I did this. I wore my medal for the rest of the day!Read moreBMBen·5 years ago·VerifiedA great run. The run was so much fun. The team there were really supportive and helpful, with marshals cheering you on at every corner!  For my first experience of a race event, I could not have hoped for more.DTDan·5 years ago·VerifiedCrystal Palace half marathon. The course was tough although the weather didn’t help it been the beginning of January 😕. Due to the nature of the course there was varied hills and terrain(grass and pavement) which was challenging but worth while.  Great way to start the new year although my legs are hurting now. The course was well run and staff were helpful. I would recommend for anyone looking for a challenge.Read moreLHLauren·5 years ago·VerifiedMust try. What an amazing experience definitely going to do it again it was so much fun I usually just brisk walk these kinds of events but I drank the most the way so much funAdd a reviewShow all reviewsLocationLondon SE19 2BB, UKWho's InSee who else is doing this eventBe the firstNo-one has marked this event as 'Doing' or 'Maybe' yet. Participants will enjoy a unique themed medal, complimentary water, sponsored goodies, and free race photos to capture the fun. Don't miss this fantastic opportunity to kickstart your running journey and create unforgettable memories amidst the iconic backdrop of Crystal Palace Park!Read moreCRYSTAL PALACE 5K 10K, HALF MARATHON & JUNIORSSun 25th January 2026

Set in the beautiful Cassiobury Park, this exciting event promises a thrilling experience for runners of all levels. With over 40 years of tradition, the day kicks off with a fun 1.8km run for kids, followed by the adrenaline-pumping 10K and the challenging Half Marathon. Participants will enjoy scenic routes through tranquil countryside and the serene Grand Union Canal, all while being cheered on by enthusiastic supporters.Organized by ATW, a leading name in UK events, the Watford Half Marathon also features incredible incentives, including a chance to win a £2000 cash spot prize! Runners can also look forward to a vibrant event village, live entertainment, and the opportunity to earn a unique medal and finish line goodies. With pacers available to help you achieve your personal best and professional photographers capturing every moment, this celebration of fitness and community is not to be missed. Whether you're training for the London Marathon or simply looking for a fun challenge, the Watford Half Marathon & 10K is the perfect event for you! Join us forRead moreATW Watford Half Marathon & 10KSun 1st February 2026Organiser's DescriptionWelcome to the Watford Half Marathon, 10K & Fun Run, a thrilling event set to take place on Sunday, February 1st, 2026, starting in the picturesque Cassiobury Park!Join us for a day of running, community spirit, and exciting experiences that has been taking place for over 40 years!IF YOU WANT 2000 REASONS TO ENTER THE WATFORD HALF MARATHON THEN YOU'LL BE PLEASED TO READ THAT ONE LUCKY COMPETITOR CHOSEN AT RANDOM WILL WIN A £2000 CASH SPOT PRIZE!The day kicks off with a 1.8km Fun Run around the Park, giving parents and supporters a great chance to cheer on their little athletes.The 10K, traffic-free run starts with the Half Marathon runners, heading out of the park. You'll experience the energy and adrenaline of running with over 2,000 runners, before splitting from the Half Marathon route and dropping down on to the Grand Union Canal. After a peaceful run along the tow path, you'll enjoy the solitude of a woodland path before tackling the final section past the event HQ to the applause of your cheer squad.The Half Marathon will start in Cassiobury Park and follows fully closed roads, venturing into serene countryside lanes behind the Grove Hotel and Golf Course. Runners will pass through Bucks Hill, Commonwood Common, and Chandlers Cross before looping back to the stunning Cassiobury Park.This Half Marathon features the Hertfordshire Vets half marathon championships.  To be eligible, you must have been born in Hertfordshire or been a resident for 9 months. Please state in your eligibility during the registration process.Lead Car Escort: The event will be led by a lead car, ensuring a smooth and safe journey for all participants throughout the course.Racecheck Gold Award: In 2023, the Watford Half Marathon proudly earned the prestigious Racecheck Gold Award.Musical Entertainment: Enjoy the rhythm of the course with music, including the beats of our amazing drummer, adding excitement and motivation to your run!Ideal for London Marathon Training: Whether you're gearing up for the London Marathon or seeking a challenging yet scenic route, the Watford Half Marathon is the perfect opportunity to test your skills and stamina.Spot Prize: One lucky Half Marathon runner stands a chance to win an incredible spot prize of £2000! Will it be you?Vibrant Event Village: Embrace the lively atmosphere at our event village, filled with activities, stalls, and a fantastic community spirit.PB Pacers: We will have pacers in both the 10K and half marathon. - **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/run-nation-victoria-park-10k-5k
- **Date**: Run London Victoria Park Half/10k/5k1.1·7 reviewsFrom Run NationSun 22nd February 2026LocationLondonPrice£15.00 - £30.00Races3 racesDistances10km, 5km, Half MarathonPopularBooked 109 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventRun London Victoria Park Half/10k/5kSun 22nd February 20261.1·7 reviewsCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an exhilarating day of running at the Run London Victoria Park Half/10k/5k event, hosted by Run Nation on Sunday, February 22nd, 2026! Set against the picturesque backdrop of London’s Victoria Park, this exciting running event promises something for every fitness enthusiast. Whether you’re aiming to conquer the half marathon with its unique course of one small lap followed by six larger laps, tackle the 10k with three laps of the eastern section of the park, or breeze through the 5k featuring one small lap and one larger lap, you’ll find the perfect challenge to suit your abilities.With all three courses being flat, participants have a fantastic opportunity to chase their personal bests while enjoying the vibrant atmosphere of this popular event. Race HQ will provide a smooth experience, with race number collection, toilets, and bag storage all conveniently located on-site. Plus, every finisher will take home a medal to commemorate their achievement, with an optional t-shirt available for purchase. Don’t miss out on this exciting winter edition of the Victoria Park running series – mark your calendars for February 22nd and join the thrill of the run!Read moreRun London Victoria Park Half/10k/5kSun 22nd February 2026Organiser's DescriptionThe Autumn 2025 edition is now the 'Winter' edition on February 22nd 2026. Run Nation's ever-popular Victoria Park running events will be merged and take place on the new dateAll three race distances start and finish on the park's Northern Drive near the Burdett-Coutts drinking fountain.The half marathon is made up of one small lap followed by six larger laps. The 10k comprises three laps of the eastern half of Victoria park, while the 5k has one small lap followed by one larger lap. All three courses are flat, offering good potential for a PB!Race Numbers will be collected from Race HQ on the day of the event. Toilets will be located at Race HQ along with bag storage.All finishers will receive a medal and have the chance to purchase an optional t shirtRaces10kMinimum Age 15yrs£24.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basket5kMinimum Age 11 Yrs£15.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketHalf MarathonMinimum Age 17yrs£30.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketBook10kMinimum Age 15yrs£24.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basket5kMinimum Age 11 Yrs£15.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketHalf MarathonMinimum Age 17yrs£30.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketCourse DetailsCourse DetailsFlat and fast courses in the park with no road traffic around.What's IncludedWhat's IncludedAll finishers will receive a medal.How to Get ThereHow to Get ThereThe Run London Victoria Park Half Marathon, 10k and 5k will all start on the Northern Drive opposite the Burdett-Coutts drinking fountain. There is limited parking nearby, so participants are advised to travel via public transport.ParkingParkingThere is limited parking nearby, so participants are advised to travel via public transport.LocationLocationNorthern Drive, London E9 7DDFAQsFAQsWill I receive race information in the lead up to my event?You will receive information and instructions by email approximately 3 days before the race. It is important that your email account is accessible to you and that you check for the email before the race.  Please be aware we do not post out race numbers, these are to be collected on the day from registration, we will provide safety pins.Will I be chipped timed?Run Nation will always endeavour to produce your results with chip timing.  Sometimes chips are not picked up for reasons outside our control (the chip didn't work or was covered up by clothing or was wet/damaged), in these cases we may have to refer to our video (GoPro) at the finish to manually identify runners. This allows us to guarantee a finish time   When the race is chip timed, your race/bib number will have a foam strip with the chip underneath - Do not remove or damage the foam or chip.   Your race number should be attached to your running top at all four corners and be visible throughout the race (including start and finish).  The race results/positions are always based on the GUN time NOT the chip time - this is standard across all official running events across the world. The sport has rules that are consistent and used in all countries/races that follow the World governing body rules.  The chip time is only used for personal use. The position the runners cross the finishing line is how ALL results are calculated.  WHERE RUNNERS DO NOT WEAR THEIR NUMBER CORRECTLY - THEY MAY NOT GET A TIME. PLEASE DISPLAY YOUR NUMBER ON THE FRONT OF YOUR RUNNING TOP AT ALL TIMES.I am injured and cannot take part. What can I do?Unfortunately we cannot offer refunds if you are ill, injured or change your plans.  Up to 14 days before race we can allow you to change the event or defer to next years race. See full instructions here  With 14 days of the event NO changes can be madeCan I register on the day of the event?We cannot guarantee that entries on the day will be available. They will be subject to an additional late entry fee.  Please enter at least 14 days before the race.When do I receive my race pack?We do not send anything in the post.  You will get an email within 7 days of the race with start times, location and how to collect your number on the day  All race packs (numbers and chips) can be collected on the day of the race from the race HQWhere can I find my results?Results are posted on the Run Nation website after the event at www.runnation.co.uk/resultsTime of registration8:00am - 10:00amprizesprizesIndividual prizes - Half & 10k1st Male/Female Overall £502nd Male/Female Overall £303rd Male/Female Overall £20First Veteran in each age group (L35+ and M40+) (minimum of 5 in an age group for 1st to qualify for a prize) £10Team prizesFirst team for men and women (3 team members to score plus minimum of 3 teams to qualify. A UKA registered club vest must be worn)Individual prizes - 5k1st Male/Female Overall £302nd Male/Female Overall £203rd Male/Female Overall £10Team prizesFirst team for men and women (3 team members to score plus minimum of 3teams to qualify. - **Price**: Run London Victoria Park Half/10k/5k1.1·7 reviewsFrom Run NationDateSun 22nd February 2026LocationLondon£15.00 - £30.00Races3 racesDistances10km, 5km, Half MarathonPopularBooked 109 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventRun London Victoria Park Half/10k/5kSun 22nd February 20261.1·7 reviewsCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an exhilarating day of running at the Run London Victoria Park Half/10k/5k event, hosted by Run Nation on Sunday, February 22nd, 2026! Set against the picturesque backdrop of London’s Victoria Park, this exciting running event promises something for every fitness enthusiast. Whether you’re aiming to conquer the half marathon with its unique course of one small lap followed by six larger laps, tackle the 10k with three laps of the eastern section of the park, or breeze through the 5k featuring one small lap and one larger lap, you’ll find the perfect challenge to suit your abilities.With all three courses being flat, participants have a fantastic opportunity to chase their personal bests while enjoying the vibrant atmosphere of this popular event. Race HQ will provide a smooth experience, with race number collection, toilets, and bag storage all conveniently located on-site. Plus, every finisher will take home a medal to commemorate their achievement, with an optional t-shirt available for purchase. Don’t miss out on this exciting winter edition of the Victoria Park running series – mark your calendars for February 22nd and join the thrill of the run!Read moreRun London Victoria Park Half/10k/5kSun 22nd February 2026
