- **Date**: Dorney Lake Triathlon - May4.6·11 reviewsFrom RunThrough EventsSat 2nd May 2026Time09:10LocationWindsor, BerkshirePrice£60.00 - £122.00Races7 racesDistancesSprint, Olympic, Super SprintTop ratedThis is a popular eventPopularBooked 79 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventDorney Lake Triathlon - MaySat 2nd May 20264.6·11 reviewsCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an exhilarating day at the Dorney Lake Triathlon, taking place on Saturday, May 2nd, 2026, in the picturesque Windsor, Berkshire! Organized by RunThrough Events, this exciting triathlon offers participants the chance to race at a world-class Olympic venue. With options for a Super Sprint, Standard, and even an Aquabike, there’s something for everyone, whether you’re a seasoned athlete or a newcomer looking to challenge yourself.The Dorney Lake Triathlon features a stunning 400m swim, a flat and fast 9.5km bike ride, and a 2.5km run for the Super Sprint; or take on the Standard distance with a 1500m swim, 38km bike, and a 10km run. Each course is meticulously marked with distance flags, ensuring a smooth and enjoyable experience for all participants. Rally your friends and family to cheer you on, or team up for the relay option to share the excitement! Join us for a day filled with camaraderie, achievement, and unforgettable memories at Dorney Lake!Read moreView allDorney Lake Triathlon - MaySat 2nd May 2026Organiser's DescriptionJoin us for the Dorney Lake Triathlon on Saturday 2nd May 2026Take on a Super Sprint or Standard triathlon around Dorney Lake on Saturday 2nd May 2026 and take the opportunity to race around this unique and inspiring Olympic venue!The purpose built lake is perfect for the swim and the flat grounds lend themselves to a totally flat bike and run course, the ideal venue for a chip timed personal best. Each discipline will be accurately marked out with distance flags and we will have plenty of helpers to keep you on track and to encourage you all the way to the finish.For those who don’t want to take part in the run, there is also an Aquabike (swim-bike) option, and for those who would like to compete as a team, there is a relay option, which allows 2 or 3 individuals to share the disciplines between them.COURSE DETAILS:Super Sprint Triathlon400m Swim | 9.5km Bike - Organized by RunThrough Events, this vibrant event invites runners of all levels to experience the stunning scenery of Finsbury Park in North London. - **Date**: Chase the Moon Battersea Park 5k & 10k4.8·4 reviewsFrom RunThrough EventsWed 25th February 2026Time19:00LocationLondonPrice£32.00 - £132.00Races3 racesDistances5km, 10kmTop ratedThis is a popular eventPopularBooked 14 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventChase the Moon Battersea Park 5k & 10kWed 25th February 20264.8·4 reviewsCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an unforgettable evening of running at the Chase the Moon Battersea Park 5k & 10k, hosted by RunThrough Events on Wednesday, February 25th, 2026. This exciting race takes place in the picturesque Battersea Park, London, where you'll enjoy smooth tarmac paths and breathtaking views of the Thames under the enchanting winter night sky. Whether you're a seasoned runner or just starting out, this pancake-flat course is perfect for chasing that elusive personal best.The event kicks off with the 5k race at 7:00 PM, followed by the 10k at 7:04 PM, allowing participants to experience the thrill of night racing with a supportive crowd cheering you on from the central Bandstand start/finish area. The lapped layout makes it easy for spectators to cheer you on from the central Bandstand start/finish area.Race Distance & Start Times07:00 pm - 5kTerrain - Tarmac | Laps - 207:04 pm - 10kTerrain - Tarmac | Laps - 4Races5kWed 25th February 2026 (19:00)£32.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basket10kWed 25th February 2026 (19:04)£32.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketGroup Booking (6 Places)£132.00 perTeam6Book5kWed 25th February 2026 (19:00)£32.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basket10kWed 25th February 2026 (19:04)£32.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketGroup Booking (6 Places)£132.00 perTeam6Route and elevationRoute and elevationBattersea Park is one of London’s most picturesque parks and has been a favourite course for those wanting to run a PB! ## Event 21: https://findarace.com/events/run-the-thames-bridges-10k

- **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/run-the-thames-bridges-10k
- **Date**: Run the Thames Bridges - 10KFrom Secret London RunsFri 8th March 2024 - Thu 31st December 2026LocationLondonPrice£15.00 - £168.00Races3 racesDistances10kmPopularBooked 185 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventRun the Thames Bridges - 10KFri 8th March 2024 - Thu 31st December 2026Copy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an unforgettable running experience with the "Run the Thames Bridges - 10K" event, organized by Secret London Runs! This exciting self-guided audio tour invites you to lace up your trainers and embark on a 10K journey across the iconic bridges of the River Thames. Kicking off on Friday, March 8, 2024, and available until December 31, 2026, participants will have the opportunity to explore the stunning sights of London at their own pace.As you traverse a scenic route from Tower Bridge to Vauxhall, you'll cross 11 picturesque bridges, including London Bridge and Waterloo Bridge, while soaking in panoramic views of major landmarks like the Tower of London and St Paul's Cathedral. With GPS-triggered audio commentary, you'll dive into the rich history of the Thames and its surrounding attractions, making every step both fun and educational. Whether you're a seasoned runner or just looking for a unique way to stay active, this event promises an exhilarating and enriching experience. Don't miss your chance to take on this challenge and earn an optional finisher medal! Sign up today and discover the beauty of London like never before.Read moreRun the Thames Bridges - 10KFri 8th March 2024 - Thu 31st December 2026Organiser's DescriptionWe challenge you to run a 10k route over the Thames bridges in a unique self-guided audio tour that takes you from Tower Bridge to Vauxhall.​With 11 bridges in total, your GPS location will trigger directions, along with audio commentary that celebrates the history of the glorious River Thames, its bridges and the development of our capital along its banks.​As you cross over every bridge possible along the route, you'll enjoy some of the best views of London, discover their history and get a unique training run in the bag.​Sign up today and take on the challenge at any time until 31st December 2026 with an optional finisher medal upon completion.The exact route of the tour will be revealed when you strap in and begin your audio tour. But over it's 10km duration you will see:​Tower Bridge, London Bridge, Millenium Bridge, Waterloo Bridge (+7 more Thames Bridges)Panoramic views of LondonMany of London's biggest attractions (the Tower of London, St Paul's Cathedral, Big Ben, Battersea Power Station, the London Peace Pagoda and loads more)Hidden sights off the beaten trackWe've been leading running tours of London for 9 years and can't wait to show you some of the city's best bits along our famous river.Read moreRacesRun the Thames Bridges£15.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketRun the Thames Bridges - With Medal£21.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketBene PLCWith Medal£168.00 perSOLD OUTTeam8BookRun the Thames Bridges£15.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketRun the Thames Bridges - With Medal£21.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketBene PLCWith Medal£168.00 perSOLD OUTTeam8Course DetailsCourse DetailsOnce you've received your code, download the route (we advise doing this as close to the date of your run as possible). Then travel to the start of the route (the sundial outside Tower Hill Station) put your headphones on and press 'Go' on the app. Your audio instructions will begin, and you will be able to follow them at your own pace. Along the way, you will be guided through some of the most striking sights along the River Thames.What's IncludedWhat's IncludedAccess to our fantastic 10K route, using VoiceMap's app, including some of Central London's finest sights.Online finishers galleryHow to Get ThereHow to Get ThereThe start point is just outside Tower Hill Station, which is accessible on the Circle and District lines, or by walking from London Bridge or Liverpool street.LocationLocationThe half marathon starts at The Sundial by Tower Hill Station, and finishes in Putney - near to Putney Station.FAQsFAQsHow does it work?We've teamed up with Voicemap to give you a state of the art, GPS triggered audio tour. Once you've signed up, you'll receive a code that will enable you to access the tour. As soon as you're on the route, the audio guide will direct you through the route  with plenty of commentary along the way about the places of interest (big sights and hidden gems) that you see along the way. Take part on a date and time of your choice.Is there a medal?Optional medals are available when you sign up. You can choose to just buy the self-guided tour without the medal if they're not your jam.When can I do it?You can complete the audio tour at a time of your choice, between the dates advertised.What if I get lost?The audio is reliant on the GPS on your device. If the cue does fail, you can use the map in the app to follow the route until the next cue. There are also options to replay any audio that you've missed.Can I run with friends?You absolutely can! You will however need to make sure that you have all purchased your own individual access to the tour using VoiceMap, so you can all use the technology. There will be a lot of audio commentary on the tour to tell you about the things that you see on the route and their history.Can one of us buy entry and lead a larger group?You won't have a positive experience - you'll be plugged into the audio (which has lots of commentary about the things you see along the way, as well as the directions) whilst the rest of your group wouldn't. This would be very difficult to manage.   You'll damage our small, family-run business. We have spent a considerable amount of time and money on creating this tour from scratch. The fees for individual entry are fair and help support our operations.If I sign up now, when will I get access to the app?If you sign up before the tour goes live, you'll get the code the day before.   If you sign up afterwards, you'll get the code within 24 hours of signing up.   Please email[email protected]with any queries on this.Can I walk or cycle the route?Walking - you will find longer gaps between the commentary, but that will be the only difference.   Cycling - the route is designed to be completed on foot - we reference pavements and road crossings which aren't suitable for cycling. It's also not safe to be plugged into our audio whilst cycling. Plus - it won't properly as you'll travel too quickly, causing the audio to overlapHow does it work?How does it work?GET READYSign up now and you'll get a code to download the Run the Thames Bridges 10K tour on VoiceMap, an award winning audio tour app.ON THE DAYHead to the start location (the Sundial at Tower Bridge) at a time of your choice on any day between 8th March and 31st October 2024.  Simply open the app, put your headphones on and hit "go" - you'll hear your first set of instructions.ALONG THE WAYTriggered by your GPS location, you'll receive more directions and commentary about the Thames, bridges and sites you're seeing along the way.WHEN YOU FINISHThe 10K finishes near Vauxhall Station. Send us your finisher pics for our online Finisher's Gallery. You can also choose to receive a fantastic medal that will be sent to you upon completion.ReviewsDone this event?First-hand event reviews from people who have been there, done that and got the breathable T-shirt, really help everyone choose their next event.Add a reviewLocationTower Hill, London, United KingdomWho's InSee who else is doing this eventDoingGNGrace NewtonViewEAElizabeth AkinViewMaybeAGAline Gordiano CassoliViewAtabak SViewBDBradley DunnViewKarl HebblewhiteViewDarby LambertViewShow allDavid BrownViewEHEmma HuntViewErin BunchViewEWEve WatersViewHBHannah BeauchampViewHSHollie Salter-HendryViewIPIlinca PopescuViewJWJessica WaglandViewKSKeira SmithViewLCLiam CarrollViewPAPete AndersonViewShannon BakerViewSESophie ElloyViewZahra ArshadViewRun the Thames Bridges - 10KDescription·Races·Reviews·Who's In↑Back to topTopBook NowWhy book with us?Official Booking PartnerLowest Price GuaranteedSecure PaymentsAdd to your eventsLoadingLoadingPsst… mark an event as Maybe and we’ll remind you when it’s coming up.Event OrganiserSecret London RunsThousands of people join our themed running tours every year to uncover London's hidden past, catch up with friends, meet new people and work up a sweat.More from Secret London RunsOfficial PartnerOfficial Booking PartnerWe’ve teamed up with Secret London Runs to allow you to enter this event directly on findarace.com.Complete the entry form and secure card payment, we'll then instantly pass on your entry without you having to leave the site.Convenient huh. 👍

This exciting self-guided audio tour invites you to lace up your trainers and embark on a 10K journey across the iconic bridges of the River Thames. Kicking off on Friday, March 8, 2024, and available until December 31, 2026, participants will have the opportunity to explore the stunning sights of London at their own pace.As you traverse a scenic route from Tower Bridge to Vauxhall, you'll cross 11 picturesque bridges, including London Bridge and Waterloo Bridge, while soaking in panoramic views of major landmarks like the Tower of London and St Paul's Cathedral. With GPS-triggered audio commentary, you'll dive into the rich history of the Thames and its surrounding attractions, making every step both fun and educational. Whether you're a seasoned runner or just looking for a unique way to stay active, this event promises an exhilarating and enriching experience. Mark your calendars for Sunday, February 15, 2026, and join us in the scenic Crystal Palace Park, London, for a memorable running experience. This timed event caters to all abilities, offering flexible race distances that include 5km, 10k, half marathon, marathon, and ultra options. Runners will navigate a well-marked 2.65km (1.65 mile) lap, with the chance to complete as many laps as desired within a generous 7-hour time limit.Whether you’re looking to challenge yourself with a full marathon or simply enjoy a couple of laps, every participant is celebrated as a winner. Come join us and get this lovely medal. This is timed event is where you choose - from 5km to 10k, half to marathon to ultra, you can complete as many, or as few, laps as you like of 2.65km (1.65 mile) along Crystal Palace Park. We welcome all abilities of runner - complete two lap and you're both a finisher and a winner in our eyes, or keep going and see how far you can get in the time limit of 7 hours and everyone gets the fabulous bespoke finishers' medal.It's 2 laps for 3.30mi (meaning you complete at least 5km) with 302ft elevation gainIt's 4 laps for 6.60mi (meaning you complete at least 10km) with 604ft elevation gainIt's 8 laps for 13.20mi (meaning you complete a half marathon) with 1208ft elevation gainIt's 16 laps for 26.40mi (meaning you complete a marathon) with 2416ft elevation gainIt's 17 plus laps for 28.05mi (meaning you complete an ultra-marathon or 45.14km plus) with 2567ft plus elevation gainNot sure? Well, the beauty of this format is that you don't even need to decide in advance, simply come and run 2 laps and see how you feel on the day and keep on going if it feels right.The course will be well marked, minimum distance is 3.30 miles or as far as you can go in the time limit. Off road trail. Organised by the passionate Rory Macpherson, this iconic event celebrates 20 years of being the toughest and most thrilling winter trail run in the South East. Runners will be treated to breathtaking views right from the start as they navigate a challenging course that features meadows, steep muddy descents, and deep sandy climbs through stunning beech and pine forests. With a race distance that promises to test your limits, participants will find themselves facing an exciting return journey that culminates in a demanding climb to the finish line. Don’t miss the chance to be part of this incredible trail running experience—mark your calendars and prepare for an unforgettable day of adventure!Read moreView allG3 - no 2Sat 31st January 2026Organiser's Description20 years on and the G3 off road race is still the best and toughest winter trail run in the South East. - **Price**: G3 - no 24.8·2 reviewsFrom Rory MacphersonDateSat 31st January 2026LocationGuildford, Surrey£27.00 - £31.00Races2 racesDistances10kmTop ratedThis is a popular eventLast chance to enterRace day is fast approachingPopularBooked 10 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventG3 - no 2Sat 31st January 20264.8·2 reviewsCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an exhilarating outdoor adventure at the G3 - no 2, taking place on Saturday, January 31, 2026, in the picturesque Guildford, Surrey! Organised by the passionate Rory Macpherson, this iconic event celebrates 20 years of being the toughest and most thrilling winter trail run in the South East. Runners will be treated to breathtaking views right from the start as they navigate a challenging course that features meadows, steep muddy descents, and deep sandy climbs through stunning beech and pine forests. With a race distance that promises to test your limits, participants will find themselves facing an exciting return journey that culminates in a demanding climb to the finish line. Don’t miss the chance to be part of this incredible trail running experience—mark your calendars and prepare for an unforgettable day of adventure!Read moreView allG3 - no 2Sat 31st January 2026

## Event 24: https://findarace.com/events/run-battersea-park-10k/29th-march-2026

- **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/run-battersea-park-10k/29th-march-2026
- **Date**: RUN Battersea Park 10K Spring5.0·1 reviewFrom Run UKSun 29th March 2026Time10:30LocationBattersea Park (next to the Bandstand)- Post Code SW11 4NGPrice£27.80Races1 raceDistances10kmTop ratedThis is a five star eventPopularBooked 93 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventRUN Battersea Park 10K SpringSun 29th March 20265.0·1 reviewCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an exhilarating day of running at the RUN Battersea Park 10K Spring, organized by Run UK! This exciting event takes place on Sunday, March 29, 2026, at the picturesque Battersea Park in London. Runners of all levels will enjoy the pancake-flat, well-paved paths that make this scenic course perfect for both beginners and seasoned athletes looking to achieve their personal bests.As you loop around the park, you’ll be surrounded by stunning views of lush trees, vibrant gardens, and the serene River Thames, creating an inspiring and motivating atmosphere. The supportive environment, filled with fellow runners and enthusiastic spectators, ensures a memorable experience. Participants will be rewarded with a medal upon finishing, along with an assortment of goodies including water, juices, fruit, and a delicious chocolate bar! With advanced Chip Timing technology, you’ll receive your accurate finishing time instantly after crossing the line. Plus, enjoy the convenience of a free baggage drop, public toilets, and a pre-race warm-up to get those muscles ready. Join us at the RUN Battersea Park 10K Spring for a fantastic day of community spirit and fitness—are you ready to take on the challenge?Read moreOrganiser's DescriptionLace up your trainers and RUN Battersea Park 10K which will be a scenic and energizing experience. The park offers pancake flat, well-paved paths, making it ideal for both beginners and experienced runners. Soak in the beautiful views, surrounded by trees, gardens, and views of the River Thames, the route is both peaceful and motivating. The race will loop around the park, creating a supportive atmosphere with spectators and fellow runners nearby. It’s a great blend of nature, city vibes, and community spirit—perfect for chasing a personal best or simply enjoying the run.All runners will receive a medal for finishing as well as loads of goodies at the finish including water, juices, fruit and a chocolate bar! We use the latest Chip Timing technology so you will get an accurate finishing time. The results are posted as soon as you cross the finish line!We will have a free baggage drop facility for you to use. This area will be cordoned off but we do advise against storing any valuables as we can’t be held responsible. Public Toilets will also be available near the start/finish area. An official race photographer will be in attendance around the course so make sure they catch you when you are looking your very best!A Medical team will also be situated at the start area and around the course. We will also have a pre-race warm up around 10 minutes before the race start to warm up those tight muscles so you will be ready for the run. ## Event 25: https://findarace.com/events/phoenix-roses-are-red-valentines-run

- **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/phoenix-roses-are-red-valentines-run
- **Date**: PHOENIX - Roses Are Red Valentines RunFrom Phoenix RunningSat 14th February 2026LocationWalton-on-Thames, SurreyPrice£22.00 - £40.00Races6 racesDistances5km, 10km, Half Marathon, Marathon, UltraLast chance to enterRace day is fast approachingPopularBooked 9 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventPHOENIX - Roses Are Red Valentines RunSat 14th February 2026Copy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready to celebrate love and fitness at the PHOENIX - Roses Are Red Valentines Run, hosted by Phoenix Running, on Saturday, February 14, 2026, in the picturesque Walton-on-Thames, Surrey. This unique running event is designed to bring together runners of all abilities for a delightful day filled with love and camaraderie. Participants can choose their distance, with options ranging from 5km to a marathon, completing laps on a scenic 5.3km (3.28 mile) out-and-back course along the enchanting river Thames.The highlight of this event is the stunning 'love spoon' medal, a perfect keepsake to commemorate your achievement. With a flexible 'rock up and run' start time within a one-hour window, there’s no need to stress about arriving on time—just come and enjoy the run at your own pace! Whether you’re aiming for a single lap or pushing for the full 7-hour limit, everyone is welcomed as a finisher and a winner. Don't forget to mark your calendars for a weekend of love, as the fun continues with the 'Violets Are Blue Valentines Run' in West Sussex the following day. With limited places available, be sure to secure your spotRead moreView allPHOENIX - Roses Are Red Valentines RunSat 14th February 2026Organiser's DescriptionIntroducing our 'Roses Are Red Valentines Run' and you are just going to love the medal for this one. We've designed an epic 'love spoon' medal with our Valentines run in Walton on Thames on Saturday 14th February presented in red.  Then, if you fancy making it a weekend of love-running, why not join us on Sunday 15th February in West Sussex for the 'Violets Are Blue Valentines Run'. That's right - there is a pair of medals on off and they are the perfect match.Absolutely everyone is welcome and this is one of our timed events where you choose - from 5km to 10k, half to marathon to ultra, you can complete as many, or as few, laps as you like of a 5.3km, 3.28 mile, out and back, course along the beautiful river Thames.  We welcome all abilities of runner - complete one lap and you're both a finisher and a winner in our eyes, or keep going and see how far you can get in the full 7 hour time limit and everyone gets the fabulous bespoke finishers' medal.We also operate our uniquely flexible 'rock up and run' start times within a 1 hour start window, so no waiting around if you arrive early and no added pressure if you're running a bit late.  If you have any special start time requirements then just get in touch because depending on the distance you are planning to run we can pretty much accommodate all start time requests.Not sure?  Well the beauty of this format is that you don't even need to decide in advance - simply come and run a lap and see how you feel on the day and keep on going if it feels right.Places will be strictly limited so don't leave it too late to enter.Read moreRacesPhoenix - 5k£27.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - 10k£29.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Half Marathon£37.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Marathon£39.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Ultra Marathon / 7-Hour Timed Event£40.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketNo Medal - Phoenix - 7-Hour Timed Event£22.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketBookPhoenix - 5k£27.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - 10k£29.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Half Marathon£37.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Marathon£39.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Ultra Marathon / 7-Hour Timed Event£40.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketNo Medal - Phoenix - 7-Hour Timed Event£22.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketCourse DetailsCourse DetailsPhoenix Running offer two different courses from the Weir in Walton.  The 'Blue Bridge' route takes you west along the Thames, passing over the small 'Blue Bridge' before passing under Walton Bridge before returning.  The 'Hampton Court' route takes you east along the times in the direction of 'Hampton Court', passing Sunbury Locke on the way before returning.  Both courses are along scenic towpaths and have no elevation gain / loss making them both fast and flat routes.  The course will be confirmed one week prior to the event and dependent on factors such as planned local events and the weather.ZoomWhat's IncludedWhat's IncludedPhoenix Running operate welcoming and inclusive events for those for all ability levels.Fabulous Bespoke Finishers’ Medal!Epic Aid Station!Flexible Start Times!Chip Timing with online live resultsResults e-mailed to participants and uploaded to website same dayDigital Finishers certificate available on Phoenix World siteAthlete profile available on Phoenix World siteHow to Get ThereHow to Get ThereThe race is around 6 miles from the closest M25 junction.Walton Station is around a 2.5 mile walk away.ParkingParkingFree parking is available a short 5 minute walk from the start line.  Details are provided in the final event information which is emailed a week prior to the event.LocationLocationThe event will start in the small field just before The Weir Pub on Waterside Drive or on the patio of the Weir Pub depending on the weather.  Just look for the Phoenix Running marquee, you can't miss it!FAQsFAQsWhat do you stock at the Aid Station?Selection of jelly sweets, crisps and pretzels along with Freddos.  There is a good chance of cake and, if the weather is hot, ice lollies as well.  Vegan options are clearly marked.  Water, variety of squash and cola will be available.Are spectators welcome?Spectators are more than welcome!  The Weir Pub is located right next to the start and finish line so offers an extremely convenient cheering on point for spectators.  The Weir offers cold drinks, hot drinks and food from 11am.  There is also a café located at the nearby Xcel Leisure Centre.  The Weir offers hotel rooms for those wishing to stay over but please book early as it always sells out.How many laps for my distance?Complete 1 lap and you’re both a finisher and a winner in our eyes! Complete 4 laps for a half marathon distance. Complete 8 laps for a full marathon distance, recognised by the 100 Marathon Club. Complete 9+ laps for an ultra marathon distance.Flexible Start times?We offer flexible start times, simply come to the start area ready to run so we can check you in and set you off as soon as you’re ready.Toilets and Facilities?There are toilets in the Weir Pub, which will be open for our use from 8:00am.  There are also toilets in the Xcel Leisure Centre that are available from 7:00am.Can I leave my bag at the aid station?We have no ‘official’ baggage check in facility; however you can leave small bags and your own items in the grassy area nearby at your own risk – these will be easily accessible to you during and throughout the event.What is the event cut-off?The cut-off time is 3:30pm by which time you should be finished running.Who are Phoenix Running?Phoenix Running are now in their 11th year of organising events in the South-East of England.  We’re all about being inclusive; it’s about great routes, great medals and above else, great people.  We believe it’s all about encouraging and enabling runners to achieve things that perhaps they once thought were impossible, whether that’s your first 5km, 5 mile or 50 km run, we’ll support you to success.  Small steps lead to bigger things and everyone starts at the beginning. Who knows where the journey may take you?ReviewsDoing this event?First-hand event reviews from people who have been there, done that and got the breathable T-shirt, really help everyone choose their next event. You'll be able to review this event once it's taken place.LocationWaterside Drive, Walton-on-Thames, KT12 2, United KingdomWho's InSee who else is doing this eventMaybeRobert DevineViewPHOENIX - Roses Are Red Valentines RunDescription·Races·Reviews·Who's In↑Back to topTopBook NowWhy book with us?Official Booking PartnerLowest Price GuaranteedSecure PaymentsAdd to your eventsLoadingLoadingPsst… mark an event as Maybe and we’ll remind you when it’s coming up.Event OrganiserPhoenix RunningPhoenix Running - At Phoenix Running we’re all about being inclusive; it’s about great routes, great medals and above else, great people. We believe it’s all about encouraging and enabling runners to achieve things that perhaps they once thought were impossible, whether that’s your first 5km, 5 mile or 50 km run, we’ll support you to success. Small steps lead to bigger things and everyone starts at the beginning. Who knows where the journey may take you?More from Phoenix RunningOfficial PartnerOfficial Booking PartnerWe’ve teamed up with Phoenix Running to allow you to enter this event directly on findarace.com.Complete the entry form and secure card payment, we'll then instantly pass on your entry without you having to leave the site.Convenient huh. 👍

- **Price**: PHOENIX - Roses Are Red Valentines RunFrom Phoenix RunningDateSat 14th February 2026LocationWalton-on-Thames, Surrey£22.00 - £40.00Races6 racesDistances5km, 10km, Half Marathon, Marathon, UltraLast chance to enterRace day is fast approachingPopularBooked 9 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventPHOENIX - Roses Are Red Valentines RunSat 14th February 2026Copy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready to celebrate love and fitness at the PHOENIX - Roses Are Red Valentines Run, hosted by Phoenix Running, on Saturday, February 14, 2026, in the picturesque Walton-on-Thames, Surrey. This unique running event is designed to bring together runners of all abilities for a delightful day filled with love and camaraderie. Participants can choose their distance, with options ranging from 5km to a marathon, completing laps on a scenic 5.3km (3.28 mile) out-and-back course along the enchanting river Thames.The highlight of this event is the stunning 'love spoon' medal, a perfect keepsake to commemorate your achievement. With a flexible 'rock up and run' start time within a one-hour window, there’s no need to stress about arriving on time—just come and enjoy the run at your own pace! Whether you’re aiming for a single lap or pushing for the full 7-hour limit, everyone is welcomed as a finisher and a winner. Don't forget to mark your calendars for a weekend of love, as the fun continues with the 'Violets Are Blue Valentines Run' in West Sussex the following day. With limited places available, be sure to secure your spotRead moreView allPHOENIX - Roses Are Red Valentines RunSat 14th February 2026

## Event 26: https://findarace.com/events/run-the-river-5k-10k

- **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/run-the-river-5k-10k
- **Date**: Run the River. Along the Thames. 20255.0·1 reviewFrom Teach FirstWed 10th September 2025Time18:00LocationLondonPrice£40.00Races2 racesDistances5km, 10kmShareShare this eventRun the River. Along the Thames. 2025Wed 10th September 20255.0·1 reviewCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready to lace up your running shoes for an unforgettable evening at Run the River, taking place on Wednesday, September 10, 2025, along the stunning banks of the Thames in London! Organised by Teach First, this exciting event invites runners of all levels to participate in either a 5k or 10k race, all while supporting a vital cause. As you navigate through iconic London landmarks, you’ll join over 2,500 fellow supporters in a twilight run that not only celebrates fitness but also champions educational equity.By participating in Run the River, you’re not just racing; you’re making a difference. Every step you take and every pound you raise will help Teach First provide essential resources to disadvantaged schools and communities across England, ensuring that every child has the chance to fulfil their potential. Join us for this inspiring event, proudly supported by headline sponsor IG Group, and be part of a movement that empowers young minds and transforms lives. Don't miss out on this incredible opportunity to run for a brighter future!Read moreView allRun the River. Along the Thames. 2025Wed 10th September 2025Organiser's DescriptionRun the River. Along the Thames. 2025Run the River is back this September. Sign up for Run the River 2025 and help ensure every young person gets the opportunity to fulfil their potential.This year, our twilight run returns where hundreds of supporters take in famous London landmarks along the banks of the Thames on Wednesday 10 September.Take part in either 5k or 10k with over 2,500 runners at the Thames and across the world as we help every child fulfil their potential.You can help ensure every child, whatever their background, gets the education they deserve, and the opportunity to fulfil their potential.Every mile covered, and every pound raised, will help fund our vital work in some of the most disadvantaged schools and communities in EnglandStep up for schools.Run the River, along the Thames at our in-person event proudly supported by headline sponsor IG Group.Read moreRaces5k£40.00CLOSED10k£40.00CLOSEDBook5k£40.00CLOSED10k£40.00CLOSEDRoute and elevationRoute and elevationA fantastic route, great for a fun run, setting a quick time. Please note Run the River. Along the Thames. is not a closed route and you will be running amongst the public.What's IncludedWhat's IncludedSoak up the atmosphere of our event village and experience iconic London landmarks as you run along the Thames at twilight.High quality technical running t-shirtFinisher medalChip timingDrinks and snacks at the event villagePrizes for top fundraisersPre-run group warm up at the event villageHow to Get ThereHow to Get TherePlease be aware that tube strikes are expected in London on the day, which may affect travel across the city. While this is outside of our control, we encourage you to plan your journey in advance and allow extra time to arrive.To help you plan your journey, here are some useful details:📍 Event Location: Potters Fields Park🔎 Best postcode for Google Maps: SE1 2AA🚶 Accessible via:· Tooley Street· Tower Bridge Road· The Queen’s Walk (from More London)· Shad Thames🚆 Nearest Rail Station: London Bridge🚌 Bus Routes:· Tooley Street: 47, 343, 381· Tower Bridge Road: 42, 78, 188We recommend allowing extra time for your journey and checking travel updates before you set off. We understand travel may be more challenging than usual, but we hope you’ll still be able to join us.LocationLocationPotters Fields ParkLondonSE1 2UDon the dayon the dayIf you are planning to walk, you must ensure you return to Potters Fields by 9.15pm. This means if you are planning on walking the 10k you must be able to complete within 2 hours and the 5k you must be able to complete within 1hour 30 minutes.Bag drop will close at 9.30pm. Please ensure you collect your bag before heading offsite.Please note, those who sign up after 31 of August will need to collect their race pack (including their running bib and t-shirt) from the event village on the day. Please allow extra time and come to the 'Info tent' when you arrive.your supportyour supportYour support has never been more vitalOne in four children in the UK live in poverty.All children have big dreams. But not every child gets the opportunities they need to thrive in school. Children experiencing poverty only have half the chance of fulfilling their potential at school than their wealthier classmates. You can change this by signing up and showing your support with Run the River. Help power the potential in our young people by fundraising or donating to support the children who need it the most.With your help, we can ensure all children can have the future of their choice.ReviewsVerified PurchaseA 'verified' purchase review means that we've verified that the person writing the review purchased their entry to this race on findarace.com.543215.01 reviewMTMaria·a year ago·VerifiedThis was such a nice event! ## Event 27: https://findarace.com/events/phoenix-alpaca-wacka-run

- **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/phoenix-alpaca-wacka-run
- **Date**: PHOENIX - Alpaca Wacka RunFrom Phoenix RunningSun 8th February 2026LocationWalton-on-Thames, SurreyPrice£22.00 - £40.00Races6 racesDistances5km, 10km, Half Marathon, Marathon, UltraLast chance to enterRace day is fast approachingBook NowShareShare this eventPHOENIX - Alpaca Wacka RunSun 8th February 2026Copy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an unforgettable running experience at the PHOENIX - Alpaca Wacka Run, hosted by Phoenix Running on Sunday, February 8, 2026, in the picturesque Walton-on-Thames, Surrey! This exciting event invites runners of all abilities to take part in a unique and flexible race format, offering distances ranging from 5km to ultra-marathon. Whether you choose to complete just one lap of the stunning 5.3km (3.28 miles) course along the scenic River Thames or push your limits for the full 7-hour duration, every participant will receive a fabulous bespoke finisher's medal.The Alpaca Wacka Run features a convenient 'rock up and run' start window, allowing you to begin your race within an hour of your arrival, making it perfect for those who prefer flexibility. With a focus on inclusivity, Phoenix Running is dedicated to making this event accessible for everyone, including a special 'no medal' entry option at a flat £20 to help keep costs down during tough times. Don't miss your chance to join in on the fun and excitement—secure your spot today, as places are limited! Whether you're a seasoned runner or just starting out, the Alpaca WackRead moreView allPHOENIX - Alpaca Wacka RunSun 8th February 2026Organiser's DescriptionIntroducing our 'Alpaca Wacka Run' the next medal in our brand new supersized spinning medal series - the medal for this event is just epic and you are not going to want to miss it.This is one of our timed events where you choose - from 5km to 10k, half to marathon to ultra, you can complete as many, or as few, laps as you like of a 5.3km, 3.28 mile, out and back, course along the beautiful river Thames.  We welcome all abilities of runner - complete one lap and you're both a finisher and a winner in our eyes, or keep going and see how far you can get in the full 7 hour time limit and everyone gets the fabulous bespoke finishers' medal.We also operate our uniquely flexible 'rock up and run' start times within a 1 hour start window, so no waiting around if you arrive early and no added pressure if you're running a bit late.  If you have any special start time requirements then just get in touch because depending on the distance you are planning to run we can pretty much accommodate all start time requests.Whilst Phoenix Running is very much about the bling and our medals are second to none, we're also acutely conscious that events can be expensive to enter and times are tough right now.  We're now in our 12th year of fixed prices for our standard themed events, but to try and do our bit we're trailing a 'no medal' entry option at a flat £20.  We cover our base costs, you get a run with all the usual Phoenix support, epic aid station, same day results published, athletes profiles, Phoenix World, digital BIBs, digital finishers certificates, etc, etc but without a medal we can keep the price low for this entry option.Not sure?  Well the beauty of this format is that you don't even need to decide in advance - simply come and run a lap and see how you feel on the day and keep on going if it feels right.Places will be strictly limited so don't leave it too late to enter.Minimum distance is 3.28 miles or as far as you can go in 7 hours. Off road trail, the terrain is riverside towpath. The overall course has no elevation gain / loss making this a flat and fast route.Read moreRacesPhoenix - 5k£27.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - 10k£29.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Half Marathon£37.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Marathon£39.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Ultra Marathon / 7-Hour Timed Event£40.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketNo Medal - Phoenix - 7-Hour Timed Event£22.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketBookPhoenix - 5k£27.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - 10k£29.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Half Marathon£37.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Marathon£39.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Ultra Marathon / 7-Hour Timed Event£40.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketNo Medal - Phoenix - 7-Hour Timed Event£22.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketCourse DetailsCourse DetailsPhoenix Running offer two different courses from the Weir in Walton.  The 'Blue Bridge' route takes you west along the Thames, passing over the small 'Blue Bridge' before passing under Walton Bridge before returning.  The 'Hampton Court' route takes you east along the times in the direction of 'Hampton Court', passing Sunbury Locke on the way before returning.  Both courses are along scenic towpaths and have no elevation gain / loss making them both fast and flat routes.  The course will be confirmed one week prior to the event and dependent on factors such as planned local events and the weather.ZoomWhat's IncludedWhat's IncludedPhoenix Running operate welcoming and inclusive events for those for all ability levels.Fabulous Bespoke Finishers’ Medal!Epic Aid Station!Flexible Start Times!Chip Timing with online live resultsResults e-mailed to participants and uploaded to website same dayDigital Finishers certificate available on Phoenix World siteAthlete profile available on Phoenix World siteHow to Get ThereHow to Get ThereThe race is around 6 miles from the closest M25 junction.Walton Station is around a 2.5 mile walk away.ParkingParkingFree parking is available a short 5 minute walk from the start line.  Details are provided in the final event information which is emailed a week prior to the event.LocationLocationThe event will start in the small field just before The Weir Pub on Waterside Drive or on the patio of the Weir Pub depending on the weather.  Just look for the Phoenix Running marquee, you can't miss it!FAQsFAQsWhat do you stock at the Aid Station?Selection of jelly sweets, crisps and pretzels along with Freddos.  There is a good chance of cake and, if the weather is hot, ice lollies as well.  Vegan options are clearly marked.  Water, variety of squash and cola will be available.Are spectators welcome?Spectators are more than welcome!  The Weir Pub is located right next to the start and finish line so offers an extremely convenient cheering on point for spectators.  The Weir offers cold drinks, hot drinks and food from 11am.  There is also a café located at the nearby Xcel Leisure Centre.  The Weir offers hotel rooms for those wishing to stay over but please book early as it always sells out.How many laps for my distance?Complete 1 lap and you’re both a finisher and a winner in our eyes! Complete 4 laps for a half marathon distance. Complete 8 laps for a full marathon distance, recognised by the 100 Marathon Club. Complete 9+ laps for an ultra marathon distance.Flexible Start times?We offer flexible start times, simply come to the start area ready to run so we can check you in and set you off as soon as you’re ready.Toilets and Facilities?There are toilets in the Weir Pub, which will be open for our use from 8:00am.  There are also toilets in the Xcel Leisure Centre that are available from 7:00am.Can I leave my bag at the aid station?We have no ‘official’ baggage check in facility; however you can leave small bags and your own items in the grassy area nearby at your own risk – these will be easily accessible to you during and throughout the event.What is the event cut-off?The cut-off time is 3:30pm by which time you should be finished running.Who are Phoenix Running?Phoenix Running are now in their 11th year of organising events in the South-East of England.  We’re all about being inclusive; it’s about great routes, great medals and above else, great people.  We believe it’s all about encouraging and enabling runners to achieve things that perhaps they once thought were impossible, whether that’s your first 5km, 5 mile or 50 km run, we’ll support you to success.  Small steps lead to bigger things and everyone starts at the beginning. Who knows where the journey may take you?race day inforace day infoCut-offsHere at Phoenix Running, all of our cut-offs are linear and stated in the final event information emails so you do not need to push harder in the first instance, keep to a steady pace & you will finish.Toilets and changing facilitiesWhere possible we have separate female & male toilets at all our venues and access to suitable facilities to change your clothes / running kit.Event UPDATESPlease join our Facebook chat groups. We recommend all entrants to our events join these groups.For any specific event changes we will always EMAIL all entrants as the primary communication channel.However, please do join our Facebook chat groups as we post news / interim updates / weather updates and much more.PHOENIX RUNNING COMMUNITYTREATING CUSTOMERS FAIRLY (TCF) OR IN THIS CASE TREATING RUNNERS FAIRLY (TRF)TREATING CUSTOMERS FAIRLY (TCF) OR IN THIS CASE TREATING RUNNERS FAIRLY (TRF)On entering Phoenix Running Ltd events you are entitled to the following;FREE DEFERALS - You can transfer or defer any race entry (once) to a future event up to 1 week (7 days) prior to the original event date (subject to availability). This is our ‘no questions asked’ commitment to our runners. There are no deferral fees, no online forms to fill out and there is no onus on you to find another runner to take your place (unless, of course, you want to). You can defer an event once, just email us and we’ll do the rest. Simple, easy and fair.FREE TRANSFERS - You can, if you prefer, transfer your entry to another runner up to 1 week (7 days) prior to the event date (subject to availability). Simply email us the new runner’s full contact details prior to the transfer deadline and include any club affiliation and medical conditions.PREGNANT DEFERALS – We allow pregnant & postpartum mums to defer their event for up to three years. This also applies to adopting parents.1 WEEK (7 DAY) DEADLINE - Race numbers are not transferable to another event or another runner within 1 week (7 days) of the race date.DEADLINE EXCEPTIONS – There are no exceptions to shortening the 7 day deadline. From time to time Phoenix Running hold special events or events with a larger than usual capacity. We reserve the right to extend the 1 week deadline for transfers and deferrals for such events. This will be made clear in the specific terms and conditions for those events.REFUNDS - The entry fee is non-refundable.ReviewsDoing this event?First-hand event reviews from people who have been there, done that and got the breathable T-shirt, really help everyone choose their next event. You'll be able to review this event once it's taken place.LocationWaterside Drive, Walton-on-Thames, KT12 2, United KingdomWho's InSee who else is doing this eventBe the firstNo-one has marked this event as 'Doing' or 'Maybe' yet. We believe it’s all about encouraging and enabling runners to achieve things that perhaps they once thought were impossible, whether that’s your first 5km, 5 mile or 50 km run, we’ll support you to success. Small steps lead to bigger things and everyone starts at the beginning. Who knows where the journey may take you?More from Phoenix RunningOfficial PartnerOfficial Booking PartnerWe’ve teamed up with Phoenix Running to allow you to enter this event directly on findarace.com.Complete the entry form and secure card payment, we'll then instantly pass on your entry without you having to leave the site.Convenient huh. 👍

This exciting event invites runners of all abilities to take part in a unique and flexible race format, offering distances ranging from 5km to ultra-marathon. Whether you choose to complete just one lap of the stunning 5.3km (3.28 miles) course along the scenic River Thames or push your limits for the full 7-hour duration, every participant will receive a fabulous bespoke finisher's medal.The Alpaca Wacka Run features a convenient 'rock up and run' start window, allowing you to begin your race within an hour of your arrival, making it perfect for those who prefer flexibility. With a focus on inclusivity, Phoenix Running is dedicated to making this event accessible for everyone, including a special 'no medal' entry option at a flat £20 to help keep costs down during tough times. Don't miss your chance to join in on the fun and excitement—secure your spot today, as places are limited! ## Event 28: https://findarace.com/events/royal-parks-half-marathon

- **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/royal-parks-half-marathon
- **Date**: ROYAL PARKS HALF MARATHONSun 11th October 2026LocationLondon, Greater LondonPrice£25.00Races1 charity placeCharity PlacesShareShare this eventROYAL PARKS HALF MARATHONSun 11th October 2026Copy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an exhilarating day at the Royal Parks Half Marathon on Sunday, October 11, 2026, in the heart of London, Greater London! This iconic event not only offers participants the chance to tackle a scenic 13.1-mile course through some of the most beautiful parks in the city but also plays a crucial role in supporting the vital work of The Royal Parks charity. Since its inception, the Royal Parks Half Marathon has raised an impressive £60 million for over 1,200 UK charities, including renowned organizations like Great Ormond Street Hospital and Mind.Join thousands of runners and supporters as they come together to celebrate health, community, and the preservation of London’s stunning green spaces. Each step taken on the course helps to keep the magic of the Royal Parks alive, promoting environmental education and wildlife conservation while maintaining historic landmarks. Don’t miss out on this remarkable opportunity to be part of a meaningful event that makes a real difference!Read moreROYAL PARKS HALF MARATHONSun 11th October 2026Organiser's DescriptionThe Royal Parks Half Marathon, presented by Royal Bank of Canada, was set up by The Royal Parks charity as an annual fundraising initiative to help conserve and enhance London’s eight Royal Parks for everyone, while enabling charities of all sizes to be involved in a major challenge event.Since then the event has exceeded all expectations, raising £60 million for over 1,200 UK charities – including The Royal Parks, GOSH, and Mind. It’s a key part of raising awareness of The Royal Parks as a charity, funded by donations and events like the Royal Parks Half. Find out more about our charity partners hereThe money raised by this event for The Royal Parks helps to keep the magic of eight vital green spaces alive, teach children and adults about the natural world, care for wildlife and conserve historic monuments.Charity placesAlzheimer's SocietyJoin Team Alzheimer's SocietyRoyal Parks Half Marathon£25.00Minimum Pledge£400.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketReviewsDone this event?First-hand event reviews from people who have been there, done that and got the breathable T-shirt, really help everyone choose their next event.Add a reviewLocationThe Mall, London, UKWho's InSee who else is doing this eventBe the firstNo-one has marked this event as 'Doing' or 'Maybe' yet. ## Event 29: https://findarace.com/events/castle-howard-triathlon

- **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/castle-howard-triathlon
- **Date**: The Castle Race Series - Howard Castle Triathlon, Duathlon, Aquathlon, Aquabike & Junior RacesFrom Castle Race SeriesSat 22nd July 2023 - Sun 23rd July 2023LocationYork, North YorkshirePrice£35.00 - £295.00Races30 racesDistancesSuper Sprint, Sprint, Middle Distance / 70.3, OlympicShareShare this eventThe Castle Race Series - Howard Castle Triathlon, Duathlon, Aquathlon, Aquabike & Junior RacesSat 22nd July 2023 - Sun 23rd July 2023Copy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an unforgettable weekend at The Castle Race Series - Howard Castle Triathlon, Duathlon, Aquathlon, Aquabike & Junior Races, taking place from Saturday, July 22nd to Sunday, July 23rd, 2023, in the stunning York, North Yorkshire. Organised by Castle Race Series, this exciting event invites participants of all ages and skill levels to experience the breathtaking beauty of the Howardian Hills and the enchanting grounds of Castle Howard.Choose your challenge from a variety of race distances in triathlon, duathlon, aquabike, aquathlon, swim, and run, catering to everyone from beginners to elite athletes. Imagine swimming in Castle Howard's Great Lake, cycling through picturesque landscapes, and running through the undulating countryside, culminating in a triumphant sprint finish right at the iconic castle doors! The event promises a vibrant festival village filled with live music, shopping, delicious food and drinks, children's entertainment, and even yoga sessions. Whether you're 9 or 90, gather your friends and family to celebrate your achievements and create cherished memories at this fantastic weekend of sport and community. Join us for an adventure you won't want to miss!Read moreThe Castle Race Series - Howard Castle Triathlon, Duathlon, Aquathlon, Aquabike & Junior RacesSat 22nd July 2023 - Sun 23rd July 2023Organiser's DescriptionJoin us to race through the glorious rural idyll of Yorkshire and the Howardian Hills. Pick your discipline - triathlon, duathlon, aquabike, aquathlon, swim and run - and choose a distance from starter to elite. Recruit your friends and celebrate completing your challenge in our festival village.Swim through Castle Howard’s Great Lake, cycle through the Howardian Hills, run through the undulating countryside of the estate before a sprint finish tops everything off in style right in front of the main doors of the house.Throughout the weekend, athletes and spectators will be able to enjoy a fun-packed festival village featuring live music, shopping, food and drink, children’s entertainment, yoga and more.The weekend will host a huge range of races for all ages and abilities. Whether you’re 9 or 90 years old, a first-timer or an elite, you can pick from our triathlon, duathlon, aquabike, aquathlon, run and swim races. - **Price**: The Castle Race Series - Howard Castle Triathlon, Duathlon, Aquathlon, Aquabike & Junior RacesFrom Castle Race SeriesDateSat 22nd July 2023 - Sun 23rd July 2023LocationYork, North Yorkshire£35.00 - £295.00Races30 racesDistancesSuper Sprint, Sprint, Middle Distance / 70.3, OlympicShareShare this eventThe Castle Race Series - Howard Castle Triathlon, Duathlon, Aquathlon, Aquabike & Junior RacesSat 22nd July 2023 - Sun 23rd July 2023Copy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an unforgettable weekend at The Castle Race Series - Howard Castle Triathlon, Duathlon, Aquathlon, Aquabike & Junior Races, taking place from Saturday, July 22nd to Sunday, July 23rd, 2023, in the stunning York, North Yorkshire. Organised by Castle Race Series, this exciting event invites participants of all ages and skill levels to experience the breathtaking beauty of the Howardian Hills and the enchanting grounds of Castle Howard.Choose your challenge from a variety of race distances in triathlon, duathlon, aquabike, aquathlon, swim, and run, catering to everyone from beginners to elite athletes. Imagine swimming in Castle Howard's Great Lake, cycling through picturesque landscapes, and running through the undulating countryside, culminating in a triumphant sprint finish right at the iconic castle doors! The event promises a vibrant festival village filled with live music, shopping, delicious food and drinks, children's entertainment, and even yoga sessions. Whether you're 9 or 90, gather your friends and family to celebrate your achievements and create cherished memories at this fantastic weekend of sport and community. Join us for an adventure you won't want to miss!Read moreThe Castle Race Series - Howard Castle Triathlon, Duathlon, Aquathlon, Aquabike & Junior RacesSat 22nd July 2023 - Sun 23rd July 2023

- **Date**: PHOENIX - Drama Llama RunFrom Phoenix RunningSat 7th February 2026LocationWalton-on-Thames, SurreyPrice£22.00 - £40.00Races6 racesDistances5km, 10km, Half Marathon, Marathon, UltraLast chance to enterRace day is fast approachingPopularBooked 12 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventPHOENIX - Drama Llama RunSat 7th February 2026Copy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryJoin us for the exhilarating PHOENIX - Drama Llama Run on Saturday, February 7, 2026, in the picturesque Walton-on-Thames, Surrey! Organized by the renowned Phoenix Running, this exciting event features a flexible running format on a beautiful 5.3km (3.28 miles) out-and-back course along the stunning river Thames. Whether you're a seasoned marathoner or a casual jogger, you can choose from distances of 5km, 10k, half marathon, marathon, or ultra, completing as many laps as you desire within a generous 7-hour time limit.What makes this event truly unique is the emphasis on inclusivity and enjoyment—every participant is a winner, receiving a fabulous bespoke finishers’ medal upon completion of their first lap. With a flexible 'rock up and run' approach, you can start your race within a one-hour window, eliminating the stress of waiting for a start time. Plus, in an effort to keep participation affordable, Phoenix Running offers a 'no medal' entry option at a flat £20, allowing you to enjoy all the event perks without the extra cost. Don't miss out on this fantastic opportunity to run, unwind, and celebrate the spirit of communityRead moreView allPHOENIX - Drama Llama RunSat 7th February 2026Organiser's DescriptionIntroducing our 'Drama Llama Run' the next medal in our brand new supersized spinning medal series - and you are just gonna love this one!  Ain't no drama, Llama.This is one of our timed events where you choose - from 5km to 10k, half to marathon to ultra, you can complete as many, or as few, laps as you like of a 5.3km, 3.28 mile, out and back, course along the beautiful river Thames.  We welcome all abilities of runner - complete one lap and you're both a finisher and a winner in our eyes, or keep going and see how far you can get in the full 7 hour time limit and everyone gets the fabulous bespoke finishers' medal.We also operate our uniquely flexible 'rock up and run' start times within a 1 hour start window, so no waiting around if you arrive early and no added pressure if you're running a bit late.  If you have any special start time requirements then just get in touch because depending on the distance you are planning to run we can pretty much accommodate all start time requests.Whilst Phoenix Running is very much about the bling and our medals are second to none, we're also acutely conscious that events can be expensive to enter and times are tough right now.  We're now in our 12th year of fixed prices for our standard themed events, but to try and do our bit we're trailing a 'no medal' entry option at a flat £20.  We cover our base costs, you get a run with all the usual Phoenix support, epic aid station, same day results published, athletes profiles, Phoenix World, digital BIBs, digital finishers certificates, etc, etc but without a medal we can keep the price low for this entry option.Not sure?  Well the beauty of this format is that you don't even need to decide in advance - simply come and run a lap and see how you feel on the day and keep on going if it feels right.Places will be strictly limited so don't leave it too late to enter.Minimum distance is 3.28 miles or as far as you can go in 7 hours. Off road trail, the terrain is riverside towpath. The overall course has no elevation gain / loss making this a flat and fast route.Read moreRacesPhoenix - 5k£27.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - 10k£29.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Half Marathon£37.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Marathon£39.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Ultra Marathon / 7-Hour Timed Event£40.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketNo Medal - Phoenix - 7-Hour Timed Event£22.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketBookPhoenix - 5k£27.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - 10k£29.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Half Marathon£37.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Marathon£39.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Ultra Marathon / 7-Hour Timed Event£40.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketNo Medal - Phoenix - 7-Hour Timed Event£22.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketCourse DetailsCourse DetailsPhoenix Running offer two different courses from the Weir in Walton.  The 'Blue Bridge' route takes you west along the Thames, passing over the small 'Blue Bridge' before passing under Walton Bridge before returning.  The 'Hampton Court' route takes you east along the times in the direction of 'Hampton Court', passing Sunbury Locke on the way before returning.  Both courses are along scenic towpaths and have no elevation gain / loss making them both fast and flat routes.  The course will be confirmed one week prior to the event and dependent on factors such as planned local events and the weather.ZoomWhat's IncludedWhat's IncludedPhoenix Running operate welcoming and inclusive events for those for all ability levels.Fabulous Bespoke Finishers’ Medal!Epic Aid Station!Flexible Start Times!Chip Timing with online live resultsResults e-mailed to participants and uploaded to website same dayDigital Finishers certificate available on Phoenix World siteAthlete profile available on Phoenix World siteHow to Get ThereHow to Get ThereThe race is around 6 miles from the closest M25 junction.Walton Station is around a 2.5 mile walk away.ParkingParkingFree parking is available a short 5 minute walk from the start line.  Details are provided in the final event information which is emailed a week prior to the event.LocationLocationThe event will start in the small field just before The Weir Pub on Waterside Drive or on the patio of the Weir Pub depending on the weather.  Just look for the Phoenix Running marquee, you can't miss it!FAQsFAQsWhat do you stock at the Aid Station?Selection of jelly sweets, crisps and pretzels along with Freddos.  There is a good chance of cake and, if the weather is hot, ice lollies as well.  Vegan options are clearly marked.  Water, variety of squash and cola will be available.Are spectators welcome?Spectators are more than welcome!  The Weir Pub is located right next to the start and finish line so offers an extremely convenient cheering on point for spectators.  The Weir offers cold drinks, hot drinks and food from 11am.  There is also a café located at the nearby Xcel Leisure Centre.  The Weir offers hotel rooms for those wishing to stay over but please book early as it always sells out.How many laps for my distance?Complete 1 lap and you’re both a finisher and a winner in our eyes! Complete 4 laps for a half marathon distance. Complete 8 laps for a full marathon distance, recognised by the 100 Marathon Club. Complete 9+ laps for an ultra marathon distance.Flexible Start times?We offer flexible start times, simply come to the start area ready to run so we can check you in and set you off as soon as you’re ready.Toilets and Facilities?There are toilets in the Weir Pub, which will be open for our use from 8:00am.  There are also toilets in the Xcel Leisure Centre that are available from 7:00am.Can I leave my bag at the aid station?We have no ‘official’ baggage check in facility; however you can leave small bags and your own items in the grassy area nearby at your own risk – these will be easily accessible to you during and throughout the event.What is the event cut-off?The cut-off time is 3:30pm by which time you should be finished running.Who are Phoenix Running?Phoenix Running are now in their 11th year of organising events in the South-East of England.  We’re all about being inclusive; it’s about great routes, great medals and above else, great people.  We believe it’s all about encouraging and enabling runners to achieve things that perhaps they once thought were impossible, whether that’s your first 5km, 5 mile or 50 km run, we’ll support you to success.  Small steps lead to bigger things and everyone starts at the beginning. Who knows where the journey may take you?race day inforace day infoCut-offsHere at Phoenix Running, all of our cut-offs are linear and stated in the final event information emails so you do not need to push harder in the first instance, keep to a steady pace & you will finish.Toilets and changing facilitiesWhere possible we have separate female & male toilets at all our venues and access to suitable facilities to change your clothes / running kit.Event UPDATESPlease join our Facebook chat groups. We recommend all entrants to our events join these groups.For any specific event changes we will always EMAIL all entrants as the primary communication channel.However, please do join our Facebook chat groups as we post news / interim updates / weather updates and much more.PHOENIX RUNNING COMMUNITYTREATING CUSTOMERS FAIRLY (TCF) OR IN THIS CASE TREATING RUNNERS FAIRLY (TRF)TREATING CUSTOMERS FAIRLY (TCF) OR IN THIS CASE TREATING RUNNERS FAIRLY (TRF)On entering Phoenix Running Ltd events you are entitled to the following;FREE DEFERALS - You can transfer or defer any race entry (once) to a future event up to 1 week (7 days) prior to the original event date (subject to availability). This is our ‘no questions asked’ commitment to our runners. There are no deferral fees, no online forms to fill out and there is no onus on you to find another runner to take your place (unless, of course, you want to). You can defer an event once, just email us and we’ll do the rest. Simple, easy and fair.FREE TRANSFERS - You can, if you prefer, transfer your entry to another runner up to 1 week (7 days) prior to the event date (subject to availability). Simply email us the new runner’s full contact details prior to the transfer deadline and include any club affiliation and medical conditions.PREGNANT DEFERALS – We allow pregnant & postpartum mums to defer their event for up to three years. This also applies to adopting parents.1 WEEK (7 DAY) DEADLINE - Race numbers are not transferable to another event or another runner within 1 week (7 days) of the race date.DEADLINE EXCEPTIONS – There are no exceptions to shortening the 7 day deadline. From time to time Phoenix Running hold special events or events with a larger than usual capacity. We reserve the right to extend the 1 week deadline for transfers and deferrals for such events. This will be made clear in the specific terms and conditions for those events.REFUNDS - The entry fee is non-refundable.ReviewsDoing this event?First-hand event reviews from people who have been there, done that and got the breathable T-shirt, really help everyone choose their next event. You'll be able to review this event once it's taken place.LocationWaterside Drive, Walton-on-Thames, KT12 2, United KingdomWho's InSee who else is doing this eventBe the firstNo-one has marked this event as 'Doing' or 'Maybe' yet. Small steps lead to bigger things and everyone starts at the beginning. Simple, easy and fair.FREE TRANSFERS - You can, if you prefer, transfer your entry to another runner up to 1 week (7 days) prior to the event date (subject to availability). Simply email us the new runner’s full contact details prior to the transfer deadline and include any club affiliation and medical conditions.PREGNANT DEFERALS – We allow pregnant & postpartum mums to defer their event for up to three years. This also applies to adopting parents.1 WEEK (7 DAY) DEADLINE - Race numbers are not transferable to another event or another runner within 1 week (7 days) of the race date.DEADLINE EXCEPTIONS – There are no exceptions to shortening the 7 day deadline. From time to time Phoenix Running hold special events or events with a larger than usual capacity. We reserve the right to extend the 1 week deadline for transfers and deferrals for such events. This will be made clear in the specific terms and conditions for those events.REFUNDS - The entry fee is non-refundable.ReviewsDoing this event?First-hand event reviews from people who have been there, done that and got the breathable T-shirt, really help everyone choose their next event. You'll be able to review this event once it's taken place.LocationWaterside Drive, Walton-on-Thames, KT12 2, United KingdomWho's InSee who else is doing this eventMaybeSaaren ThornViewPHOENIX - Huffin Puffin RunDescription·Races·Reviews·Who's In↑Back to topTopBook NowWhy book with us?Official Booking PartnerLowest Price GuaranteedSecure PaymentsAdd to your eventsLoadingLoadingPsst… mark an event as Maybe and we’ll remind you when it’s coming up.Event OrganiserPhoenix RunningPhoenix Running - At Phoenix Running we’re all about being inclusive; it’s about great routes, great medals and above else, great people. We believe it’s all about encouraging and enabling runners to achieve things that perhaps they once thought were impossible, whether that’s your first 5km, 5 mile or 50 km run, we’ll support you to success. Small steps lead to bigger things and everyone starts at the beginning. Who knows where the journey may take you?More from Phoenix RunningOfficial PartnerOfficial Booking PartnerWe’ve teamed up with Phoenix Running to allow you to enter this event directly on findarace.com.Complete the entry form and secure card payment, we'll then instantly pass on your entry without you having to leave the site.Convenient huh. 👍

- **Price**: PHOENIX - Huffin Puffin RunFrom Phoenix RunningDateSat 24th January 2026LocationWalton-on-Thames, Surrey£22.00 - £40.00Races6 racesDistances5km, 10km, Half Marathon, Marathon, UltraLast chance to enterRace day is fast approachingPopularBooked 25 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventPHOENIX - Huffin Puffin RunSat 24th January 2026Copy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryJoin us for the exhilarating PHOENIX - Huffin Puffin Run, organized by Phoenix Running, taking place on Saturday, January 24, 2026, in the picturesque Walton-on-Thames, Surrey. This exciting event offers a unique opportunity for runners of all abilities to choose their challenge, with distances ranging from a fun 5km to the full marathon and ultra distances. The scenic 5.3km (3.28 mile) out-and-back course along the stunning river Thames is perfect for anyone looking to enjoy a leisurely run or push their limits within a generous 7-hour time limit.What makes the Huffin Puffin Run truly special is its flexible 'rock up and run' start times, allowing participants to begin at their convenience within a one-hour window. Whether you're aiming to complete just one lap or striving for multiple laps, everyone is celebrated as a finisher, receiving a fabulous bespoke medal! In addition to the traditional medal entry, a budget-friendly 'no medal' option is available, ensuring that everyone can join in without breaking the bank. With a flat and fast terrain, fantastic support, and a vibrant community atmosphere, the Huffin Puffin Run promises to be an unforgettable day of fun and fitness! BeRead moreView allPHOENIX - Huffin Puffin RunSat 24th January 2026

## Event 32: https://findarace.com/events/bromley-10k

- **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/bromley-10k
- **Date**: Bromley 10K2.5·10 reviewsFrom MCC PromotionsSun 25th January 2026Time10:00LocationNorman Park Athletics Track, off Hayes Lane, Bromley, Kent BR2 9EJPrice£18.24Races1 raceDistances10kmLast chance to enterRace day is fast approachingPopularBooked 15 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventBromley 10KSun 25th January 20262.5·10 reviewsCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an exhilarating day of running at the Bromley 10K on Sunday, January 25, 2026! Organized by MCC Promotions, this exciting event will take place at the Norman Park Athletics Track in Bromley, offering participants a well-measured course that includes 270 meters on the track, followed by four laps around the park, and a final 300 meters back on the track. Runners of all levels can look forward to competing for the prestigious bespoke medal and trophies for the top three finishers, as well as a team award.With a history of impressive course records set by elite runners like Adam Clarke and Debbie Marsden, the Bromley 10K promises a vibrant atmosphere filled with motivation and camaraderie. Participants can expect excellent facilities, including changing rooms, toilets, and baggage storage, alongside first aid support and hydration stations throughout the course. Whether you're a seasoned athlete or a first-time participant, this event is perfect for everyone! Make sure to register between 9:00 and 9:45 am for an unforgettable day of competition and community spirit. Join us and run for a good cause, as a portion of the proceeds will benefit Poppyspath.com. - **Date**: Chase the Moon Battersea Park 5k & 10k4.8·4 reviewsFrom RunThrough EventsWed 21st January 2026Time19:00LocationLondonPrice£32.00 - £132.00Races3 racesDistances5km, 10kmTop ratedThis is a popular eventLast chance to enterRace day is fast approachingPopularBooked 58 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventChase the Moon Battersea Park 5k & 10kWed 21st January 20264.8·4 reviewsCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an exhilarating evening of running at the Chase the Moon Battersea Park 5k & 10k, organized by RunThrough Events! On Wednesday, January 21st, 2026, join fellow running enthusiasts in one of London's most picturesque parks for a midweek motivator that promises both fun and fitness. The event kicks off at the iconic Battersea Park Bandstand, where participants can choose between a speedy 5k or a challenging 10k route.The 5k race starts at 7:00 PM, featuring a flat, tarmac course made up of two laps, while the 10k follows shortly after at 7:04 PM, covering four laps of the same scenic terrain. Whether you're aiming to set a personal best or simply enjoy an atmospheric evening run beneath the stars, Chase the Moon is the perfect opportunity to elevate your winter training. - **Price**: Chase the Moon Battersea Park 5k & 10k4.8·4 reviewsFrom RunThrough EventsDateWed 21st January 2026Time19:00LocationLondon£32.00 - £132.00Races3 racesDistances5km, 10kmTop ratedThis is a popular eventLast chance to enterRace day is fast approachingPopularBooked 58 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventChase the Moon Battersea Park 5k & 10kWed 21st January 20264.8·4 reviewsCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an exhilarating evening of running at the Chase the Moon Battersea Park 5k & 10k, organized by RunThrough Events! On Wednesday, January 21st, 2026, join fellow running enthusiasts in one of London's most picturesque parks for a midweek motivator that promises both fun and fitness. The event kicks off at the iconic Battersea Park Bandstand, where participants can choose between a speedy 5k or a challenging 10k route.The 5k race starts at 7:00 PM, featuring a flat, tarmac course made up of two laps, while the 10k follows shortly after at 7:04 PM, covering four laps of the same scenic terrain. Whether you're aiming to set a personal best or simply enjoy an atmospheric evening run beneath the stars, Chase the Moon is the perfect opportunity to elevate your winter training. Don’t miss out on this unique experience to light up your January in Battersea Park!Read moreChase the Moon Battersea Park 5k & 10kWed 21st January 2026
