## Event 34: https://findarace.com/events/phoenix-all-stars-run

- **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/phoenix-all-stars-run
- **Date**: PHOENIX - Winter All Stars RunFrom Phoenix RunningSun 18th January 2026LocationWalton-on-Thames, SurreyPrice£22.00 - £40.00Races6 racesDistances5km, 10km, Half Marathon, Marathon, UltraLast chance to enterRace day is fast approachingPopularBooked 19 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventPHOENIX - Winter All Stars RunSun 18th January 2026Copy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an unforgettable day of running at the PHOENIX - Winter All Stars Run, hosted by Phoenix Running on Sunday, January 18, 2026, in the scenic Walton-on-Thames, Surrey! This exciting event offers a range of race distances, allowing participants to choose from 5km, 10k, half marathon, marathon, or ultra distances. Runners will navigate a beautiful out-and-back course along the picturesque river Thames, completing laps on a flat and fast 5.3km route.Whether you're a seasoned marathoner or a casual jogger, the Winter All Stars Run welcomes all abilities. With a unique 'rock up and run' start time system, there’s no need to stress about arriving on time; you can simply join in within a one-hour window. Plus, every participant will earn a stunning bespoke medal in one of three colors—red, white, or blue! For those looking for affordable entry, Phoenix Running is trialing a 'no medal' option at a flat £20, ensuring everyone can enjoy the thrill of the race without breaking the bank. Don’t miss your chance to be part of this fantastic event; spots are limited, so sign up today and experience the joyRead moreView allPHOENIX - Winter All Stars RunSun 18th January 2026Organiser's DescriptionPhoenix Running is inviting you to join us for our Winter All Stars Run with a truly epic medal in a choice of 3 different colours - red, white and blue.This is one of our timed events where you choose - from 5km to 10k, half to marathon to ultra, you can complete as many, or as few, laps as you like of a 5.3km, 3.28 mile, out and back, course along the beautiful river Thames.  We welcome all abilities of runner - complete one lap and you're both a finisher and a winner in our eyes, or keep going and see how far you can get in the full 7 hour time limit and everyone gets the fabulous bespoke finishers' medal.We also operate our uniquely flexible 'rock up and run' start times within a 1 hour start window, so no waiting around if you arrive early and no added pressure if you're running a bit late.  If you have any special start time requirements then just get in touch because depending on the distance you are planning to run we can pretty much accommodate all start time requests.Whilst Phoenix Running is very much about the bling and our medals are second to none, we're also acutely conscious that events can be expensive to enter and times are tough right now.  We're now in our 12th year of fixed prices for our standard themed events, but to try and do our bit we're trailing a 'no medal' entry option at a flat £20.  We cover our base costs, you get a run with all the usual Phoenix support, epic aid station, same day results published, athletes profiles, Phoenix World, digital BIBs, digital finishers certificates, etc, etc but without a medal we can keep the price low for this entry option.Not sure?  Well the beauty of this format is that you don't even need to decide in advance - simply come and run a lap and see how you feel on the day and keep on going if it feels right.Places will be strictly limited so don't leave it too late to enter.Minimum distance is 3.28 miles or as far as you can go in 7 hours. Off road trail, the terrain is riverside towpath. The overall course has no elevation gain / loss making this a flat and fast route.Read moreRacesPhoenix - 5k£27.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - 10k£29.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Half Marathon£37.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Marathon£39.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Ultra Marathon / 7-Hour Timed Event£40.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketNo Medal - Phoenix - 7-Hour Timed Event£22.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketBookPhoenix - 5k£27.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - 10k£29.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Half Marathon£37.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Marathon£39.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Ultra Marathon / 7-Hour Timed Event£40.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketNo Medal - Phoenix - 7-Hour Timed Event£22.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketCourse DetailsCourse DetailsPhoenix Running offer two different courses from the Weir in Walton.  The 'Blue Bridge' route takes you west along the Thames, passing over the small 'Blue Bridge' before passing under Walton Bridge before returning.  The 'Hampton Court' route takes you east along the times in the direction of 'Hampton Court', passing Sunbury Locke on the way before returning.  Both courses are along scenic towpaths and have no elevation gain / loss making them both fast and flat routes.  The course will be confirmed one week prior to the event and dependent on factors such as planned local events and the weather.ZoomWhat's IncludedWhat's IncludedPhoenix Running operate welcoming and inclusive events for those for all ability levels.Fabulous Bespoke Finishers’ Medal!Epic Aid Station!Flexible Start Times!Chip Timing with online live resultsResults e-mailed to participants and uploaded to website same dayDigital Finishers certificate available on Phoenix World siteAthlete profile available on Phoenix World siteHow to Get ThereHow to Get ThereThe race is around 6 miles from the closest M25 junction.Walton Station is around a 2.5 mile walk away.ParkingParkingFree parking is available a short 5 minute walk from the start line.  Details are provided in the final event information which is emailed a week prior to the event.LocationLocationThe event will start in the small field just before The Weir Pub on Waterside Drive or on the patio of the Weir Pub depending on the weather.  Just look for the Phoenix Running marquee, you can't miss it!FAQsFAQsWhat do you stock at the Aid Station?Selection of jelly sweets, crisps and pretzels along with Freddos.  There is a good chance of cake and, if the weather is hot, ice lollies as well.  Vegan options are clearly marked.  Water, variety of squash and cola will be available.Are spectators welcome?Spectators are more than welcome!  The Weir Pub is located right next to the start and finish line so offers an extremely convenient cheering on point for spectators.  The Weir offers cold drinks, hot drinks and food from 11am.  There is also a café located at the nearby Xcel Leisure Centre.  The Weir offers hotel rooms for those wishing to stay over but please book early as it always sells out.How many laps for my distance?Complete 1 lap and you’re both a finisher and a winner in our eyes! Complete 4 laps for a half marathon distance. Complete 8 laps for a full marathon distance, recognised by the 100 Marathon Club. Complete 9+ laps for an ultra marathon distance.Flexible Start times?We offer flexible start times, simply come to the start area ready to run so we can check you in and set you off as soon as you’re ready.Toilets and Facilities?There are toilets in the Weir Pub, which will be open for our use from 8:00am.  There are also toilets in the Xcel Leisure Centre that are available from 7:00am.Can I leave my bag at the aid station?We have no ‘official’ baggage check in facility; however you can leave small bags and your own items in the grassy area nearby at your own risk – these will be easily accessible to you during and throughout the event.What is the event cut-off?The cut-off time is 3:30pm by which time you should be finished running.Who are Phoenix Running?Phoenix Running are now in their 11th year of organising events in the South-East of England.  We’re all about being inclusive; it’s about great routes, great medals and above else, great people.  We believe it’s all about encouraging and enabling runners to achieve things that perhaps they once thought were impossible, whether that’s your first 5km, 5 mile or 50 km run, we’ll support you to success.  Small steps lead to bigger things and everyone starts at the beginning. Who knows where the journey may take you?race day inforace day infoCut-offsHere at Phoenix Running, all of our cut-offs are linear and stated in the final event information emails so you do not need to push harder in the first instance, keep to a steady pace & you will finish.Toilets and changing facilitiesWhere possible we have separate female & male toilets at all our venues and access to suitable facilities to change your clothes / running kit.Event UPDATESPlease join our Facebook chat groups. We recommend all entrants to our events join these groups.For any specific event changes we will always EMAIL all entrants as the primary communication channel.However, please do join our Facebook chat groups as we post news / interim updates / weather updates and much more.PHOENIX RUNNING COMMUNITYTREATING CUSTOMERS FAIRLY (TCF) OR IN THIS CASE TREATING RUNNERS FAIRLY (TRF)TREATING CUSTOMERS FAIRLY (TCF) OR IN THIS CASE TREATING RUNNERS FAIRLY (TRF)On entering Phoenix Running Ltd events you are entitled to the following;FREE DEFERALS - You can transfer or defer any race entry (once) to a future event up to 1 week (7 days) prior to the original event date (subject to availability). This is our ‘no questions asked’ commitment to our runners. There are no deferral fees, no online forms to fill out and there is no onus on you to find another runner to take your place (unless, of course, you want to). You can defer an event once, just email us and we’ll do the rest. Simple, easy and fair.FREE TRANSFERS - You can, if you prefer, transfer your entry to another runner up to 1 week (7 days) prior to the event date (subject to availability). Simply email us the new runner’s full contact details prior to the transfer deadline and include any club affiliation and medical conditions.PREGNANT DEFERALS – We allow pregnant & postpartum mums to defer their event for up to three years. This also applies to adopting parents.1 WEEK (7 DAY) DEADLINE - Race numbers are not transferable to another event or another runner within 1 week (7 days) of the race date.DEADLINE EXCEPTIONS – There are no exceptions to shortening the 7 day deadline. From time to time Phoenix Running hold special events or events with a larger than usual capacity. We reserve the right to extend the 1 week deadline for transfers and deferrals for such events. This will be made clear in the specific terms and conditions for those events.REFUNDS - The entry fee is non-refundable.ReviewsDone this event?First-hand event reviews from people who have been there, done that and got the breathable T-shirt, really help everyone choose their next event.Add a reviewLocationWaterside Drive, Walton-on-Thames, KT12 2, United KingdomWho's InSee who else is doing this eventBe the firstNo-one has marked this event as 'Doing' or 'Maybe' yet. Will you be the first?PHOENIX - Winter All Stars RunDescription·Races·Reviews·Who's In↑Back to topTopBook NowWhy book with us?Official Booking PartnerLowest Price GuaranteedSecure PaymentsAdd to your eventsLoadingLoadingPsst… mark an event as Maybe and we’ll remind you when it’s coming up.Event OrganiserPhoenix RunningPhoenix Running - At Phoenix Running we’re all about being inclusive; it’s about great routes, great medals and above else, great people. We believe it’s all about encouraging and enabling runners to achieve things that perhaps they once thought were impossible, whether that’s your first 5km, 5 mile or 50 km run, we’ll support you to success. Small steps lead to bigger things and everyone starts at the beginning. Who knows where the journey may take you?More from Phoenix RunningOfficial PartnerOfficial Booking PartnerWe’ve teamed up with Phoenix Running to allow you to enter this event directly on findarace.com.Complete the entry form and secure card payment, we'll then instantly pass on your entry without you having to leave the site.Convenient huh. 👍

Nestled in the stunning surroundings of Gatton Park in Reigate, Surrey, this event promises to deliver an unforgettable experience for all participants. After the success of last year's event, we're thrilled to bring back the same fantastic format that you loved.Choose your adventure with a gorgeous 5K multi-terrain loop, and decide whether to take on one or two loops for a 5K or 10K challenge. ## Event 36: https://findarace.com/events/phoenix-runificent

- **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/phoenix-runificent
- **Date**: PHOENIX - Runificent RainbowFrom Phoenix RunningSun 25th January 2026LocationWalton-on-Thames, SurreyPrice£22.00 - £40.00Races6 racesDistances5km, 10km, Half Marathon, Marathon, UltraLast chance to enterRace day is fast approachingPopularBooked 6 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventPHOENIX - Runificent RainbowSun 25th January 2026Copy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryJoin Phoenix Running for the vibrant PHOENIX - Runificent Rainbow event on Sunday, January 25th, 2026, in beautiful Walton-on-Thames, Surrey! This exciting day promises a RUNIFICENT experience, celebrating the joy of running with a stunning rainbow-themed medal and the opportunity to dress up in fancy attire. Whether you’re a seasoned runner or just starting out, this event welcomes everyone with open arms.Participants can choose from a variety of race distances, including 5km, 10k, half marathon, marathon, or ultra, completing as many laps as they desire around a scenic 5.3km course along the picturesque river Thames. With a unique 'rock up and run' start time option, you can enjoy a stress-free race day without the long waits. Plus, everyone who crosses the finish line will receive a fabulous bespoke medal, as well as the chance to opt for a lower-cost entry without a medal if desired. Don’t miss out on this incredible opportunity to run, celebrate, and create unforgettable memories with Phoenix Running! Secure your spot today, as places are limited!Read moreView allPHOENIX - Runificent RainbowSun 25th January 2026Organiser's DescriptionPhoenix Running are inviting you to join us once again for a simply RUNIFICENT run and this time the medal is presented in rainbow colours.After the huge success and the amount of fun we had at our first five Runificent events, we're back for more with Runificent - Rainbow Edition.  The event is open to absolutely everyone and anyone and the bling really is magnificent with a whole new 'rainbow' colour scheme, and, of course, fancy dress is the optional order of the day.This is one of our timed events where you choose - from 5km to 10k, half to marathon to ultra, you can complete as many, or as few, laps as you like of a 5.3km, 3.28 mile, out and back, course along the beautiful river Thames.  We welcome all abilities of runner - complete one lap and you're both a finisher and a winner in our eyes, or keep going and see how far you can get in the full 7 hour time limit and everyone gets the fabulous bespoke finishers' medal.We also operate our uniquely flexible 'rock up and run' start times within a 1 hour start window, so no waiting around if you arrive early and no added pressure if you're running a bit late.  If you have any special start time requirements then just get in touch because depending on the distance you are planning to run we can pretty much accommodate all start time requests.Whilst Phoenix Running is very much about the bling and our medals are second to none, we're also acutely conscious that events can be expensive to enter and times are tough right now.  We're now in our 12th year of fixed prices for our standard themed events, but to try and do our bit we're trailing a 'no medal' entry option at a flat £20.  We cover our base costs, you get a run with all the usual Phoenix support, epic aid station, same day results published, athletes profiles, Phoenix World, digital BIBs, digital finishers certificates, etc, etc but without a medal we can keep the price low for this entry option.Not sure?  Well the beauty of this format is that you don't even need to decide in advance - simply come and run a lap and see how you feel on the day and keep on going if it feels right.Places will be strictly limited so don't leave it too late to enter.Minimum distance is 3.28 miles or as far as you can go in 7 hours. Off road trail, the terrain is riverside towpath. The overall course has no elevation gain / loss making this a flat and fast route.Read moreRacesPhoenix - 5k£27.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - 10k£29.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Half Marathon£37.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Marathon£39.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Ultra Marathon / 7-Hour Timed Event£40.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketNo Medal - Phoenix - 7-Hour Timed Event£22.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketBookPhoenix - 5k£27.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - 10k£29.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Half Marathon£37.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Marathon£39.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Ultra Marathon / 7-Hour Timed Event£40.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketNo Medal - Phoenix - 7-Hour Timed Event£22.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketCourse DetailsCourse DetailsPhoenix Running offer two different courses from the Weir in Walton.  The 'Blue Bridge' route takes you west along the Thames, passing over the small 'Blue Bridge' before passing under Walton Bridge before returning.  The 'Hampton Court' route takes you east along the times in the direction of 'Hampton Court', passing Sunbury Locke on the way before returning.  Both courses are along scenic towpaths and have no elevation gain / loss making them both fast and flat routes.  The course will be confirmed one week prior to the event and dependent on factors such as planned local events and the weather.ZoomWhat's IncludedWhat's IncludedPhoenix Running operate welcoming and inclusive events for those for all ability levels.Fabulous Bespoke Finishers’ Medal!Epic Aid Station!Flexible Start Times!Chip Timing with online live resultsResults e-mailed to participants and uploaded to website same dayDigital Finishers certificate available on Phoenix World siteAthlete profile available on Phoenix World siteHow to Get ThereHow to Get ThereThe race is around 6 miles from the closest M25 junction.Walton Station is around a 2.5 mile walk away.ParkingParkingFree parking is available a short 5 minute walk from the start line.  Details are provided in the final event information which is emailed a week prior to the event.LocationLocationThe event will start in the small field just before The Weir Pub on Waterside Drive or on the patio of the Weir Pub depending on the weather.  Just look for the Phoenix Running marquee, you can't miss it!FAQsFAQsWhat do you stock at the Aid Station?Selection of jelly sweets, crisps and pretzels along with Freddos.  There is a good chance of cake and, if the weather is hot, ice lollies as well.  Vegan options are clearly marked.  Water, variety of squash and cola will be available.Are spectators welcome?Spectators are more than welcome!  The Weir Pub is located right next to the start and finish line so offers an extremely convenient cheering on point for spectators.  The Weir offers cold drinks, hot drinks and food from 11am.  There is also a café located at the nearby Xcel Leisure Centre.  The Weir offers hotel rooms for those wishing to stay over but please book early as it always sells out.How many laps for my distance?Complete 1 lap and you’re both a finisher and a winner in our eyes! Complete 4 laps for a half marathon distance. Complete 8 laps for a full marathon distance, recognised by the 100 Marathon Club. Complete 9+ laps for an ultra marathon distance.Flexible Start times?We offer flexible start times, simply come to the start area ready to run so we can check you in and set you off as soon as you’re ready.Toilets and Facilities?There are toilets in the Weir Pub, which will be open for our use from 8:00am.  There are also toilets in the Xcel Leisure Centre that are available from 7:00am.Can I leave my bag at the aid station?We have no ‘official’ baggage check in facility; however you can leave small bags and your own items in the grassy area nearby at your own risk – these will be easily accessible to you during and throughout the event.What is the event cut-off?The cut-off time is 3:30pm by which time you should be finished running.Who are Phoenix Running?Phoenix Running are now in their 11th year of organising events in the South-East of England.  We’re all about being inclusive; it’s about great routes, great medals and above else, great people.  We believe it’s all about encouraging and enabling runners to achieve things that perhaps they once thought were impossible, whether that’s your first 5km, 5 mile or 50 km run, we’ll support you to success.  Small steps lead to bigger things and everyone starts at the beginning. Who knows where the journey may take you?race day inforace day infoCut-offsHere at Phoenix Running, all of our cut-offs are linear and stated in the final event information emails so you do not need to push harder in the first instance, keep to a steady pace & you will finish.Toilets and changing facilitiesWhere possible we have separate female & male toilets at all our venues and access to suitable facilities to change your clothes / running kit.Event UPDATESPlease join our Facebook chat groups. We recommend all entrants to our events join these groups.For any specific event changes we will always EMAIL all entrants as the primary communication channel.However, please do join our Facebook chat groups as we post news / interim updates / weather updates and much more.PHOENIX RUNNING COMMUNITYTREATING CUSTOMERS FAIRLY (TCF) OR IN THIS CASE TREATING RUNNERS FAIRLY (TRF)TREATING CUSTOMERS FAIRLY (TCF) OR IN THIS CASE TREATING RUNNERS FAIRLY (TRF)On entering Phoenix Running Ltd events you are entitled to the following;FREE DEFERALS - You can transfer or defer any race entry (once) to a future event up to 1 week (7 days) prior to the original event date (subject to availability). This is our ‘no questions asked’ commitment to our runners. There are no deferral fees, no online forms to fill out and there is no onus on you to find another runner to take your place (unless, of course, you want to). You can defer an event once, just email us and we’ll do the rest. Simple, easy and fair.FREE TRANSFERS - You can, if you prefer, transfer your entry to another runner up to 1 week (7 days) prior to the event date (subject to availability). Simply email us the new runner’s full contact details prior to the transfer deadline and include any club affiliation and medical conditions.PREGNANT DEFERALS – We allow pregnant & postpartum mums to defer their event for up to three years. This also applies to adopting parents.1 WEEK (7 DAY) DEADLINE - Race numbers are not transferable to another event or another runner within 1 week (7 days) of the race date.DEADLINE EXCEPTIONS – There are no exceptions to shortening the 7 day deadline. From time to time Phoenix Running hold special events or events with a larger than usual capacity. We reserve the right to extend the 1 week deadline for transfers and deferrals for such events. This will be made clear in the specific terms and conditions for those events.REFUNDS - The entry fee is non-refundable.ReviewsDone this event?First-hand event reviews from people who have been there, done that and got the breathable T-shirt, really help everyone choose their next event.Add a reviewLocationWaterside Drive, Walton-on-Thames, KT12 2, United KingdomWho's InSee who else is doing this eventBe the firstNo-one has marked this event as 'Doing' or 'Maybe' yet. Will you be the first?PHOENIX - Runificent RainbowDescription·Races·Reviews·Who's In↑Back to topTopBook NowWhy book with us?Official Booking PartnerLowest Price GuaranteedSecure PaymentsAdd to your eventsLoadingLoadingPsst… mark an event as Maybe and we’ll remind you when it’s coming up.Event OrganiserPhoenix RunningPhoenix Running - At Phoenix Running we’re all about being inclusive; it’s about great routes, great medals and above else, great people. We believe it’s all about encouraging and enabling runners to achieve things that perhaps they once thought were impossible, whether that’s your first 5km, 5 mile or 50 km run, we’ll support you to success. Small steps lead to bigger things and everyone starts at the beginning. Who knows where the journey may take you?More from Phoenix RunningOfficial PartnerOfficial Booking PartnerWe’ve teamed up with Phoenix Running to allow you to enter this event directly on findarace.com.Complete the entry form and secure card payment, we'll then instantly pass on your entry without you having to leave the site.Convenient huh. 👍

- **Date**: G3 - no 34.8·2 reviewsFrom Rory MacphersonSat 28th February 2026Time08:00LocationGuildford, SurreyPrice£27.00 - £31.00Races2 racesDistances10kmTop ratedThis is a popular eventPopularBooked 6 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventG3 - no 3Sat 28th February 20264.8·2 reviewsCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an exhilarating adventure at the G3 - no 3, organized by Rory Macpherson! This thrilling off-road race takes place on Saturday, February 28, 2026, in the picturesque Guildford, Surrey. Celebrating its 20th anniversary, the G3 has established itself as the toughest and most exciting winter trail run in the South East. Runners will be treated to stunning views as they traverse through meadows, tackle steep muddy descents, and conquer deep sandy trails, all while weaving through beautiful beech and pine forests. With challenging race distances that promise to test your limits, the return route features more of the same exhilarating terrain, culminating in a demanding climb to the finish line. Whether you’re a seasoned runner or looking to embrace the challenge, the G3 - no 3 is an event you won't want to miss!Read moreView allG3 - no 3Sat 28th February 2026Organiser's Description20 years on and the G3 off road race is still the best and toughest winter trail run in the South East. With cracking views from the start, the route crosses meadows, descends steep, muddy hills, climbs on deep sandy trails and passes through beech and pine forests. And that's just the half of it! The return is more of the same, ending with a tough climb to the finish line!Races15k£31.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basket10k£27.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketBook15k£31.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basket10k£27.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketCourse DetailsCourse DetailsThere will be hills, mud, rocks, sand, forest and outstanding views. - **Price**: G3 - no 34.8·2 reviewsFrom Rory MacphersonDateSat 28th February 2026Time08:00LocationGuildford, Surrey£27.00 - £31.00Races2 racesDistances10kmTop ratedThis is a popular eventPopularBooked 6 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventG3 - no 3Sat 28th February 20264.8·2 reviewsCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an exhilarating adventure at the G3 - no 3, organized by Rory Macpherson! This thrilling off-road race takes place on Saturday, February 28, 2026, in the picturesque Guildford, Surrey. Celebrating its 20th anniversary, the G3 has established itself as the toughest and most exciting winter trail run in the South East. Runners will be treated to stunning views as they traverse through meadows, tackle steep muddy descents, and conquer deep sandy trails, all while weaving through beautiful beech and pine forests. With challenging race distances that promise to test your limits, the return route features more of the same exhilarating terrain, culminating in a demanding climb to the finish line. Whether you’re a seasoned runner or looking to embrace the challenge, the G3 - no 3 is an event you won't want to miss!Read moreView allG3 - no 3Sat 28th February 2026
- **Distances**: 66 Botley RdSouthamptonSO31 1BBUnited Kingdom

- **Date**: Run Dorney Lake Half Marathon, 10k & 5k - February4.3·27 reviewsFrom RunThrough EventsSat 7th February 2026Time09:10LocationWindsor, BerkshirePrice£30.00 - £162.00Races4 racesDistances5km, 10km, Half MarathonTop ratedThis is a popular eventLast chance to enterRace day is fast approachingPopularBooked 55 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventRun Dorney Lake Half Marathon, 10k & 5k - FebruarySat 7th February 20264.3·27 reviewsCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an exhilarating day of running at the Run Dorney Lake Half Marathon, 10k & 5k on Saturday, February 7th, 2026! Organized by RunThrough Events, this fantastic event takes place at the stunning Dorney Lake in Windsor, Berkshire, known for its scenic views and as the venue for the London 2012 Olympic rowing events. Whether you're aiming for a personal best or simply looking to enjoy a day out with friends and family, there’s a distance for everyone — choose from the challenging Half Marathon, the exhilarating 10k, or the fun-filled 5k race.The flat and fast course is perfect for runners of all levels, providing an excellent opportunity for chip-timed records. The Half Marathon kicks off at 9:10 am, followed by the 10k and 5k races. With a picturesque backdrop and a vibrant atmosphere, the Run Dorney Lake event promises to be an unforgettable experience. Dorney Lake is situated near the village of Dorney in Buckinghamshire, is a purpose-built rowing lake with grounds that are a perfect backdrop to running as well as rowing. ## Event 39: https://findarace.com/events/the-winter-lucky-dip-run

- **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/the-winter-lucky-dip-run
- **Date**: The Winter Lucky Dip RunFrom Saturn RunningSat 24th January 2026Time09:00LocationThe Last Hop, Unit 2, Thames Edge Court, Clarence Street, Staines, TW18 4SUPrice£18.00 - £30.00Races8 racesDistances5km, 10km, 10 Mile, Half Marathon, Marathon, UltraLast chance to enterRace day is fast approachingPopularBooked 8 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventThe Winter Lucky Dip RunSat 24th January 2026Copy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryJoin us for the exhilarating Winter Lucky Dip Run, organized by Saturn Running, on Saturday, January 24th, 2026! This fun-filled event will take place in the scenic Staines, Middlesex, along a beautiful stretch of the River Thames. Participants can engage in a unique 7-hour Time Challenge, with the flexibility to choose their own distance starting from a minimum of 3km up to 50km. Whether you're a seasoned runner or just starting out, this inclusive event welcomes all abilities, including walkers!What makes the Winter Lucky Dip Run even more special is the chance to select your own medal from a variety of past events! With an entry fee of just £23.00 for members, you'll also receive a fabulous goody bag and a mini-me medal pin for every event you attend. Don't miss this opportunity to experience a fantastic race day atmosphere, complete with a well-stocked aid table and facilities at The Last Hop venue. Mark your calendars and be part of this unforgettable running adventure!Read moreOrganiser's DescriptionAbout The EventCome and join us for our Winter Lucky Dip Run, there is no set medal for this race, instead, you will get to choose your own from a selection of medals from past events! & time: Saturday 24th of January 2026Official start time 9amLocation: The Last Hop, Unit 2, Thames Edge Court, Clarence Street, Staines, TW18 4SUThe £23.00 entry price is available only to members. You can become a member by logging into your profile and purchasing one of the membership schemes. As well as the cheapest entry price, a member is guaranteed entry to the run and gets a mini-me medal pin at every event they attend!Distance & TimeThis is a 7 hour Time Challenge event which allows you full control over the distance you want to run. The minimum distance is 3km, which is part of a lap. A full lap is 4.37 miles or 7km (this is subject to change to a 3.28 mile loop depending on course conditions) and you can run as many, or as few, of these laps as you like subject to UK athletics age restrictions (please see the restrictions below). We will have a 3k turn point and 5k turn point within the lap which is available to allow our younger runners to comply with the UK Athletics distance rules. It is 3 laps for a half marathon, 6 for a marathon and 7 or over for an ultra, we also have a 50km distance available.CourseThe course along a beautiful stretch of the River Thames. It is fully signed so no navigational experience is required. The course is mainly well made paths and tarmac with a very short grass section. Road shoes are suitable all year round.All abilities are welcome at our super-inclusive events and we also welcome walkers too.Medals & PinsAs long as you complete 3km, you will be an official finisher and you will get to choose your medal from a selection from past events.We also have distance pins available for anyone who runs a half-marathon distance or more. Pins available to collect are our Half-Marathon, Mega-Half, Marathon, 50km and Ultra pins. You will receive one pin on the ribbon of your medal to reflect your achievement.Goody BagAbsolutely! You have earned these replacement calories after all, so all our finishers get a great goody bag. You will have a selection of goodies to choose from. As well as our standard goody bag, we have a vegan goody bag too. Please let us know if you are vegan or if you prefer a vegan goody bag when you enter and we can have this ready for you.Aid TableWe have a fantastic aid table and drinks table at the start / finish of each lap. There is an incredible selection of drinks and savoury or sweet snacks such as crisps, cheese, fruit and cookies with vegan options also available.Special EventIf our event is a special event for you, for instance your 50th, 99th, 100th, 150th, 200th, 250th etc. marathon or half marathon then please let us know. We will provide you with a great number with your name and the event date on it.Venue FacilitiesToilets are available at the start/finish area inside the venue HQ. There are changing facilities available at the venue. These facilities will be open from 8am and will be available for use throughout the entire event.Car ParkingThere is parking available in Bridge Street Car Park in Staines, TW18 4TP.Race Day Instructions & Number Pick UpFinal race day instructions will be sent out via email in the week leading up to the event and will include full race day information. Number pick up is on the day at race registration before the start of the event.Other InformationThere will be somewhere to leave a bag at the start / finish area. However, this is not a secure bag and the items cannot be watched at all times so please do not leave anything valuable.Headphones are allowed.Our running events are held on public footpaths so we would be grateful if all runners were considerate of other path users.Read moreRaces10kmSat 24th January 2026 (09:00)£27.00Trail Running Association (TRA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than - **Price**: The Winter Lucky Dip RunFrom Saturn RunningDateSat 24th January 2026Time09:00LocationThe Last Hop, Unit 2, Thames Edge Court, Clarence Street, Staines, TW18 4SU£18.00 - £30.00Races8 racesDistances5km, 10km, 10 Mile, Half Marathon, Marathon, UltraLast chance to enterRace day is fast approachingPopularBooked 8 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventThe Winter Lucky Dip RunSat 24th January 2026Copy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryJoin us for the exhilarating Winter Lucky Dip Run, organized by Saturn Running, on Saturday, January 24th, 2026! This fun-filled event will take place in the scenic Staines, Middlesex, along a beautiful stretch of the River Thames. Participants can engage in a unique 7-hour Time Challenge, with the flexibility to choose their own distance starting from a minimum of 3km up to 50km. Whether you're a seasoned runner or just starting out, this inclusive event welcomes all abilities, including walkers!What makes the Winter Lucky Dip Run even more special is the chance to select your own medal from a variety of past events! With an entry fee of just £23.00 for members, you'll also receive a fabulous goody bag and a mini-me medal pin for every event you attend. Don't miss this opportunity to experience a fantastic race day atmosphere, complete with a well-stocked aid table and facilities at The Last Hop venue. Mark your calendars and be part of this unforgettable running adventure!Read more

## Event 40: https://findarace.com/events/phoenix-fridays

- **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/phoenix-fridays
- **Date**: PHOENIX Fridays - Frosty Friday5.0·2 reviewsFrom Phoenix RunningFri 23rd January 2026LocationWalton-on-Thames, SurreyPrice£26.00 - £33.00Races5 racesDistances5km, 10km, Half Marathon, Marathon, UltraTop ratedThis is a five star eventLast chance to enterRace day is fast approachingBook NowShareShare this eventPHOENIX Fridays - Frosty FridayFri 23rd January 20265.0·2 reviewsCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an exhilarating running experience at the PHOENIX Fridays - Frosty Friday event, hosted by Phoenix Running on Friday, January 23rd, 2026, in the picturesque Walton-on-Thames, Surrey. This exciting event invites runners of all abilities to enjoy a flexible and friendly atmosphere, with start times that cater to your schedule. Choose your challenge from a variety of distances including 5km, 10k, half marathon, marathon, or ultra, all set on a beautiful 5.3km out-and-back course along the stunning river Thames.At this unique event, you can tackle as many laps as you like within the 7-hour time limit, making it perfect for both seasoned runners and those new to the sport. Plus, you'll be rewarded with one of PHOENIX Running's legendary bespoke finishers' medals, ensuring that every participant leaves with a tangible memory of their achievement. With limited places available, this is an event you won't want to miss—come and celebrate the joy of running with us on Frosty Friday!Read moreView allPHOENIX Fridays - Frosty FridayFri 23rd January 2026Organiser's DescriptionContinuing the 'PHOENIX Fridays'; a series of Friday running events with flexible 'rock up and run' start times to suit you, here's our 'Frosty Friday' - Thank Phoenix it's Friday!These events are a little smaller than our weekend events and the entry fee a little less than our standard themed entry, but with no compromise on our legendary medals. We'll have a selection of our usual big, bold, bright and epic bling for you to choose from at each of the PHOENIX Fridays and if there's a specific medal you've been after for a while or one you missed out on from one of our previous events then just drop us an email and we'll see if we can bring that one along for you.The PHOENIX Fridays are a series of our timed events where you choose - from 5km to 10k, half to marathon to ultra, you can complete as many, or as few, laps as you like of a 5.3km, 3.28 mile, out and back, course along the beautiful river Thames. We welcome all abilities of runner - complete one lap and you're both a finisher and a winner in our eyes, or keep going and see how far you can get in the full time limit and everyone gets a fabulous bespoke finishers' medal from our awesome selection.Not sure? Well the beauty of this format is that you don't even need to decide in advance - simply come and run a lap and see how you feel on the day and keep on going if it feels right.Places will be strictly limited so don't leave it too late to enter.Minimum distance is 3.28 miles or as far as you can go in 7 hours. Off road trail, the terrain is riverside towpath. The overall course has no elevation gain / loss making this a flat and fast route.Read moreRacesPhoenix Friday - 5k Entry£26.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix Friday - 10k Entry£28.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix Friday - Half Marathon£31.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix Friday - Marathon£32.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix Friday - Ultra Marathon / 7-Hour Timed Event£33.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketBookPhoenix Friday - 5k Entry£26.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix Friday - 10k Entry£28.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix Friday - Half Marathon£31.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix Friday - Marathon£32.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix Friday - Ultra Marathon / 7-Hour Timed Event£33.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketCourse DetailsCourse DetailsPhoenix Running offer two different courses from the Weir in Walton.  The 'Blue Bridge' route takes you west along the Thames, passing over the small 'Blue Bridge' before passing under Walton Bridge before returning.  The 'Hampton Court' route takes you east along the times in the direction of 'Hampton Court', passing Sunbury Locke on the way before returning.  Both courses are along scenic towpaths and have no elevation gain / loss making them both fast and flat routes.  The course will be confirmed one week prior to the event and dependent on factors such as planned local events and the weather.ZoomWhat's IncludedWhat's IncludedThe PHOENIX Fridays are a series of our timed events where you choose - from 5km to 10k, half to marathon to ultra, you can complete as many, or as few, laps as you like of a 5.3km, 3.28 mile, out and back, course along the beautiful river Thames. We welcome all abilities of runner - complete one lap and you're both a finisher and a winner in our eyes, or keep going and see how far you can get in the full time limit and everyone gets a fabulous bespoke finishers' medal from our awesome selection.Fabulous Bespoke Finishers’ Medal!Epic Aid Station!Flexible Start Times!Chip Timing with online live resultsResults e-mailed to participants and uploaded to website same dayDigital Finishers certificate available on Phoenix World siteAthlete profile available on Phoenix World siteHow to Get ThereHow to Get ThereThe race is around 6 miles from the closest M25 junction.Walton Station is around a 2.5 mile walk away.ParkingParkingFree parking is available a short 5 minute walk from the start line.  Details are provided in the final event information which is emailed a week prior to the event.LocationLocationThe event will start in the small field just before The Weir Pub on Waterside Drive or on the patio of the Weir Pub depending on the weather.  Just look for the Phoenix Running marquee, you can't miss it!FAQsFAQsWhat do you stock at the Aid Station?Selection of jelly sweets, crisps and pretzels along with Freddos.  There is a good chance of cake and, if the weather is hot, ice lollies as well.  Vegan options are clearly marked.  Water, variety of squash and cola will be available.Are spectators welcome?Spectators are more than welcome!  The Weir Pub is located right next to the start and finish line so offers an extremely convenient cheering on point for spectators.  The Weir offers cold drinks, hot drinks and food from 11am.  There is also a café located at the nearby Xcel Leisure Centre.  The Weir offers hotel rooms for those wishing to stay over but please book early as it always sells out.How many laps for my distance?Complete 1 lap and you’re both a finisher and a winner in our eyes! Complete 4 laps for a half marathon distance. Complete 8 laps for a full marathon distance, recognised by the 100 Marathon Club. Complete 9+ laps for an ultra marathon distance.Flexible Start times?We offer flexible start times, simply come to the start area ready to run so we can check you in and set you off as soon as you’re ready.Toilets and Facilities?There are toilets in the Weir Pub, which will be open for our use from 8:00am.  There are also toilets in the Xcel Leisure Centre that are available from 7:00am.Can I leave my bag at the aid station?We have no ‘official’ baggage check in facility; however you can leave small bags and your own items in the grassy area nearby at your own risk – these will be easily accessible to you during and throughout the event.What is the event cut-off?The cut-off time is 3:30pm by which time you should be finished running.Who are Phoenix Running?Phoenix Running are now in their 12th year of organising events in the South-East of England.  We’re all about being inclusive; it’s about great routes, great medals and above else, great people.  We believe it’s all about encouraging and enabling runners to achieve things that perhaps they once thought were impossible, whether that’s your first 5km, 5 mile or 50 km run, we’ll support you to success.  Small steps lead to bigger things and everyone starts at the beginning. Who knows where the journey may take you?race day inforace day infoCut-offsHere at Phoenix Running, all of our cut-offs are linear and stated in the final event information emails so you do not need to push harder in the first instance, keep to a steady pace & you will finish.Toilets and changing facilitiesWhere possible we have separate female & male toilets at all our venues and access to suitable facilities to change your clothes / running kit.Event UPDATESPlease join our Facebook chat groups. We recommend all entrants to our events join these groups.For any specific event changes we will always EMAIL all entrants as the primary communication channel.However, please do join our Facebook chat groups as we post news / interim updates / weather updates and much more.PHOENIX RUNNING COMMUNITYTREATING CUSTOMERS FAIRLY (TCF) OR IN THIS CASE TREATING RUNNERS FAIRLY (TRF)TREATING CUSTOMERS FAIRLY (TCF) OR IN THIS CASE TREATING RUNNERS FAIRLY (TRF)On entering Phoenix Running Ltd events you are entitled to the following;FREE DEFERALS - You can transfer or defer any race entry (once) to a future event up to 1 week (7 days) prior to the original event date (subject to availability). This is our ‘no questions asked’ commitment to our runners. There are no deferral fees, no online forms to fill out and there is no onus on you to find another runner to take your place (unless, of course, you want to). You can defer an event once, just email us and we’ll do the rest. Simple, easy and fair.FREE TRANSFERS - You can, if you prefer, transfer your entry to another runner up to 1 week (7 days) prior to the event date (subject to availability). Simply email us the new runner’s full contact details prior to the transfer deadline and include any club affiliation and medical conditions.PREGNANT DEFERALS – We allow pregnant & postpartum mums to defer their event for up to three years. This also applies to adopting parents.1 WEEK (7 DAY) DEADLINE - Race numbers are not transferable to another event or another runner within 1 week (7 days) of the race date.DEADLINE EXCEPTIONS – There are no exceptions to shortening the 7 day deadline. From time to time Phoenix Running hold special events or events with a larger than usual capacity. We reserve the right to extend the 1 week deadline for transfers and deferrals for such events. This will be made clear in the specific terms and conditions for those events.REFUNDS - The entry fee is non-refundable.ReviewsVerified PurchaseA 'verified' purchase review means that we've verified that the person writing the review purchased their entry to this race on findarace.com.543215.02 reviewsJTJohn·2 years ago·VerifiedGreat event with a friendly and supportive team!Gareth·2 years agoGreat bunch of people running the event with lots of supportive runners! On Sunday, April 19th, 2026, athletes from all walks of life will gather in the picturesque town of Stratford-upon-Avon, Warwickshire, to take part in this vibrant and welcoming event. Participants will dive into the challenge with a swim in the heated indoor pool at Stratford Leisure Centre, followed by a scenic bike ride through the beautiful countryside, and concluding with a flat towpath run along the stunning River Avon.Now in its 31st year, the Stratford Triathlon has become a beloved tradition, attracting seasoned triathletes as well as first-time competitors. Each entrant will receive an official finishers medal, and with electronic chip timing, you can track your performance throughout the race. - **Price**: Stratford Triathlon 20264.0·2 reviewsFrom UK TriathlonDateSun 19th April 2026Time07:30LocationStratford-upon-Avon, Warwickshire£89.00 - £99.00Races4 racesDistancesSuper Sprint, SprintTop ratedThis is a popular eventPopularBooked 32 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventStratford Triathlon 2026Sun 19th April 20264.0·2 reviewsCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an exhilarating experience at the Stratford Triathlon 2026, organized by UK Triathlon! On Sunday, April 19th, 2026, athletes from all walks of life will gather in the picturesque town of Stratford-upon-Avon, Warwickshire, to take part in this vibrant and welcoming event. Participants will dive into the challenge with a swim in the heated indoor pool at Stratford Leisure Centre, followed by a scenic bike ride through the beautiful countryside, and concluding with a flat towpath run along the stunning River Avon.Now in its 31st year, the Stratford Triathlon has become a beloved tradition, attracting seasoned triathletes as well as first-time competitors. Whether you're a novice or a pro, the event promises a supportive and festive atmosphere, with enthusiastic crowds cheering you on every step of the way. Each entrant will receive an official finishers medal, and with electronic chip timing, you can track your performance throughout the race. Don't miss out on this amazing opportunity to challenge yourself and celebrate the spirit of triathlon in a truly iconic location!Read moreStratford Triathlon 2026Sun 19th April 2026

## Event 43: https://findarace.com/events/the-highland-hotfoot

- **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/the-highland-hotfoot
- **Date**: The Highland HotfootFrom Saturn RunningSat 7th February 2026Time09:00LocationThe Last Hop, Unit 2, Thames Edge Court, Clarence Street, Staines, TW18 4SUPrice£18.00 - £35.00Races8 racesDistances5km, 10km, 10 Mile, Half Marathon, Marathon, UltraLast chance to enterRace day is fast approachingPopularBooked 8 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventThe Highland HotfootSat 7th February 2026Copy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryJoin us for The Highland Hotfoot, an exciting running event organized by Saturn Running, taking place on Saturday, 7th February 2026, in the picturesque town of Staines, Middlesex. This vibrant event is not just about the challenge; it's about celebrating the joy of running with our beloved furry Highland Cows as your inspiration!Kicking off at 9 AM from The Last Hop, this unique 7-hour Time Challenge allows participants to choose their distance, with options ranging from a minimum of 3 km to ultra distances of 50 km. The scenic route meanders along the beautiful River Thames, offering a mix of well-made paths and tarmac. Whether you're a seasoned marathoner or a casual walker, everyone is welcome! Plus, finishers will receive a charming Highland Cow medal and a goody bag full of delightful treats. Mark your calendars for this fun-filled day of fitness, community, and furry friends—The Highland Hotfoot awaits!Read moreOrganiser's DescriptionAbout The EventCome and join us for The Highland Hotfoot because who doesn't love those furry Highland Cows and therefore want a medal of them!! & time: Saturday 7th of February 2026Official start time 9amLocation: The Last Hop, Unit 2, Thames Edge Court, Clarence Street, Staines, TW18 4SUThe £23.00 entry price is available only to members. You can become a member by logging into your profile and purchasing one of the membership schemes. As well as the cheapest entry price, a member is guaranteed entry to the run and gets a mini-me medal pin at every event they attend!Distance & TimeThis is a 7 hour Time Challenge event which allows you full control over the distance you want to run. The minimum distance is 3km, which is part of a lap. A full lap is 4.37 miles or 7km (this is subject to change to a 3.28 mile loop depending on course conditions) and you can run as many, or as few, of these laps as you like subject to UK athletics age restrictions (please see the restrictions below). We will have a 3k turn point and 5k turn point within the lap which is available to allow our younger runners to comply with the UK Athletics distance rules. It is 3 laps for a half marathon, 6 for a marathon and 7 or over for an ultra, we also have a 50km distance available.CourseThe course along a beautiful stretch of the River Thames. It is fully signed so no navigational experience is required. The course is mainly well made paths and tarmac with a very short grass section. Road shoes are suitable all year round.All abilities are welcome at our super-inclusive events and we also welcome walkers too.Medals & PinsAs long as you complete 3km, you will be an official finisher and you will receive your Highland Cow medal!We also have distance pins available for anyone who runs a half-marathon distance or more. Pins available to collect are our Half-Marathon, Mega-Half, Marathon, 50km and Ultra pins. You will receive one pin on the ribbon of your medal to reflect your achievement.Goody BagAbsolutely! You have earned these replacement calories after all, so all our finishers get a great goody bag. You will have a selection of goodies to choose from. As well as our standard goody bag, we have a vegan goody bag too. Please let us know if you are vegan or if you prefer a vegan goody bag when you enter and we can have this ready for you.Aid TableWe have a fantastic aid table and drinks table at the start / finish of each lap. There is an incredible selection of drinks and savoury or sweet snacks such as crisps, cheese, fruit and cookies with vegan options also available.Special EventIf our event is a special event for you, for instance your 50th, 99th, 100th, 150th, 200th, 250th etc. marathon or half marathon then please let us know. We will provide you with a great number with your name and the event date on it.Venue FacilitiesToilets are available at the start/finish area inside the venue HQ. There are changing facilities available at the venue. These facilities will be open from 8am and will be available for use throughout the entire event.Car ParkingThere is parking available in Bridge Street Car Park in Staines, TW18 4TP.Race Day Instructions & Number Pick UpFinal race day instructions will be sent out via email in the week leading up to the event and will include full race day information. Number pick up is on the day at race registration before the start of the event.Other InformationThere will be somewhere to leave a bag at the start / finish area. However, this is not a secure bag and the items cannot be watched at all times so please do not leave anything valuable.Headphones are allowed.Our running events are held on public footpaths so we would be grateful if all runners were considerate of other path users.Read moreRaces10kmSat 7th February 2026 (09:00)£27.00Trail Running Association (TRA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than - **Price**: The Highland HotfootFrom Saturn RunningDateSat 7th February 2026Time09:00LocationThe Last Hop, Unit 2, Thames Edge Court, Clarence Street, Staines, TW18 4SU£18.00 - £35.00Races8 racesDistances5km, 10km, 10 Mile, Half Marathon, Marathon, UltraLast chance to enterRace day is fast approachingPopularBooked 8 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventThe Highland HotfootSat 7th February 2026Copy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryJoin us for The Highland Hotfoot, an exciting running event organized by Saturn Running, taking place on Saturday, 7th February 2026, in the picturesque town of Staines, Middlesex. This vibrant event is not just about the challenge; it's about celebrating the joy of running with our beloved furry Highland Cows as your inspiration!Kicking off at 9 AM from The Last Hop, this unique 7-hour Time Challenge allows participants to choose their distance, with options ranging from a minimum of 3 km to ultra distances of 50 km. The scenic route meanders along the beautiful River Thames, offering a mix of well-made paths and tarmac. Whether you're a seasoned marathoner or a casual walker, everyone is welcome! Plus, finishers will receive a charming Highland Cow medal and a goody bag full of delightful treats. Mark your calendars for this fun-filled day of fitness, community, and furry friends—The Highland Hotfoot awaits!Read more

## Event 45: https://findarace.com/events/runthrough-chase-the-moon-olympic-park-5k-10k

- **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/runthrough-chase-the-moon-olympic-park-5k-10k
- **Date**: Chase The Moon QE Olympic Park 5k & 10k4.9·18 reviewsFrom RunThrough EventsWed 18th February 2026Time19:00LocationLondonPrice£32.00Races2 racesDistances5km, 10kmTop ratedThis is a popular eventPopularBooked 11 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventChase The Moon QE Olympic Park 5k & 10kWed 18th February 20264.9·18 reviewsCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready to lace up your running shoes for the exhilarating Chase The Moon Queen Elizabeth Olympic Park 5k & 10k, hosted by RunThrough Events on Wednesday, February 18th, 2026, in the heart of London! This fantastic event invites runners of all levels to experience the stunning grounds of South Park within the iconic Queen Elizabeth Olympic Park. Whether you're aiming for a personal best in the flat and fast 5k or pushing through the 10k challenge, this race promises a thrilling atmosphere and a chance to enjoy the outdoors.Starting at 7:00 PM, participants can expect a chip-timed race featuring accurately marked distance flags, enthusiastic helpers, and a supportive crowd to cheer you on. With both routes set on smooth tarmac and minimal elevation, it’s perfect for those looking to achieve a quick time. Plus, every finisher will be rewarded with a unique themed medal, along with water and goodies to refuel after your run! Join us for a fun-filled evening and make unforgettable memories at the Chase The Moon event – we can't wait to see you there!Read moreChase The Moon QE Olympic Park 5k & 10kWed 18th February 2026Organiser's DescriptionJoin us for the Chase The Moon Queen Elizabeth Olympic Park 5k & 10k on Wednesday 18th February 2026Come and run a chip timed 5k or 10k through the incredible grounds of the South Park in London’s Queen Elizabeth Olympic Park. This course is not only flat and fast, but also inspiring. The RunThrough Queen Elizabeth Olympic Park 5k & 10k event is a great opportunity for a quick time and a fun morning out with a unique themed medal waiting for you at the finish!Included is a themed medal, water and goodies! Each kilometre is accurately marked out with distance flags and we have plenty of helpers to keep you on track and to encourage you on your way to the finish. ## Event 47: https://findarace.com/events/atw-love-welwyn-garden-city-10k

- **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/atw-love-welwyn-garden-city-10k
- **Date**: ATW Love Welwyn Garden City 10KFrom ATWSun 15th February 2026Time09:30LocationStanborough Lakes, North Lake, Welwyn Garden City, Hertfordshire, AL8 6DQPrice£31.80Races1 raceDistances10kmLast chance to enterRace day is fast approachingPopularBooked 14 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventATW Love Welwyn Garden City 10KSun 15th February 2026Copy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready to embrace the love for running at the ATW Love Welwyn Garden City 10K on Sunday, February 15th, 2026! Organized by ATW, this exciting event is celebrating its 11th anniversary in the picturesque setting of Welwyn Garden City, Hertfordshire. Whether you're a seasoned runner or stepping into your first race, this 10K promises an unforgettable experience filled with community spirit, music, and the thrill of competition.Starting at the beautiful Stanborough Lakes, participants will enjoy a well-marked course with enthusiastic marshals cheering you on every step of the way. As you cross the finish line, you'll be rewarded with a custom-designed medal and delightful goodies from the ATW team, plus free event photos to capture your achievement. Join us in making every stride count by supporting our partner charity, Alzheimer's Research UK, and help raise funds for an important cause. Don’t miss out on this chance to challenge yourself, meet fellow running enthusiasts, and spread a little love this February! Mark your calendars for this must-attend event organized by ATW, where every step leads to a greater purpose.Read moreATW Love Welwyn Garden City 10KSun 15th February 2026Organiser's Description2026 will mark the 11th anniversary of the event!JOIN US FOR THE LOVE WELWYN GARDEN CITY 10K ON SUNDAY 15th FEBRUARY 2026!Welcome to the Love Welwyn Garden City 10K — historically held around Valentine's Day this is the event that encourages you to fall in love with running.Whether you're a seasoned runner or a first-timer, the Love Welwyn Garden City 10K offers an ideal opportunity to challenge yourself while enjoying the camaraderie of friends and fellow club members. What's more, your run will earn you a custom medal and finish line goodies from the ATW Team.Lace up, hit the pavement, and experience the perfect blend of athleticism, community and music in this exceptional winter race.You will need to collect your race number on the morning of the event, from the registration stand at the event venue. Registration will open at 08:30am. Please ensure you leave plenty of time to collect your number and warm up before the start time.The ATW Love Welwyn Garden City 10K is a popular and much loved event, starting in the beautiful Stanborough lakes with a lovely run route. The course is well marked and sign posted and there will be supportive marshals along the route, to help you out if needed (and cheering you on) Not only will you receive a custom designed medal and goodies, you will also get FREE event photos. Don't forget to smile when you see the photographer!Come and spread a little love this February by joining us on the start line.The event has received some excellent reviews from previous participant (see below)Read reviews on RacecheckCharity - Join us in making every stride count by raising money for our partner charity, Alzheimers Research UK - your run will not only challenge your limits but also change lives, one step at a time, for a cure. Opportunities to donate and fundraise will appear at checkout.ATW Transfer & Refund PolicyAt ATW, we understand that sometimes plans change. While we do not offer refunds or transfers to another person, we provide a hassle-free event transfer option for those unable to attend their booked event. If you are unable to participate in your scheduled event for any reason—including injury, illness, pregnancy, bereavement, or other unforeseen circumstances—you can transfer your entry to any future ATW event. There is no proof required, we trust you!Even better news, transfers can be made all the way up to 48 hours before your original event date.Event OrganiserATW was established in 2011 by James Shipley. We have now become a national events company in the UK, specialising in Running, Triathlon, Duathlon, Aquathon, Cycling, Swimming and all permutation of swim-bike run events. With over 40,000 entries a year ATW has become a firm favourite amongst people who are determined to Dream, Believe and Achieve.Read moreRaces10kSun 15th February 2026 (09:30)£31.80UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketBook10kSun 15th February 2026 (09:30)£31.80UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketReviewsDone this event?First-hand event reviews from people who have been there, done that and got the breathable T-shirt, really help everyone choose their next event.Add a reviewLocationStanborough Road, Welwyn Garden City, AL8 6, United KingdomWho's InSee who else is doing this eventMaybeBWBen WilliamsViewGWGraham WhiteViewATW Love Welwyn Garden City 10KDescription·Races·Reviews·Who's In↑Back to topTopBook NowWhy book with us?Official Booking PartnerLowest Price GuaranteedSecure PaymentsAdd to your eventsLoadingLoadingPsst… mark an event as Maybe and we’ll remind you when it’s coming up.Event OrganiserATWATW was started in 2011 by James Shipley - St Albans based runner and triathlete.   The company started to promote and sell training plans for multiple endurance sporting disciplines. However, along the way, event organisation became a big part of the business.   Today, we organise 80+ events per year and time 50+ more for other organisers, under the company Event Chip Timing.   Happy racing!More from ATWOfficial PartnerOfficial Booking PartnerWe’ve teamed up with ATW to allow you to enter this event directly on findarace.com.Complete the entry form and secure card payment, we'll then instantly pass on your entry without you having to leave the site.Convenient huh. 👍

Organized by ATW, this exciting event is celebrating its 11th anniversary in the picturesque setting of Welwyn Garden City, Hertfordshire. Whether you're a seasoned runner or stepping into your first race, this 10K promises an unforgettable experience filled with community spirit, music, and the thrill of competition.Starting at the beautiful Stanborough Lakes, participants will enjoy a well-marked course with enthusiastic marshals cheering you on every step of the way. As you cross the finish line, you'll be rewarded with a custom-designed medal and delightful goodies from the ATW team, plus free event photos to capture your achievement. Join us in making every stride count by supporting our partner charity, Alzheimer's Research UK, and help raise funds for an important cause. Don’t miss out on this chance to challenge yourself, meet fellow running enthusiasts, and spread a little love this February! Mark your calendars for this must-attend event organized by ATW, where every step leads to a greater purpose.Read moreATW Love Welwyn Garden City 10KSun 15th February 2026

## Event 48: https://findarace.com/events/midweek-chasing-numbers

- **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/midweek-chasing-numbers
- **Date**: Midweek Chasing Numbers Feb 20265.0·3 reviewsFrom Saturn RunningFri 6th February 2026Time08:30LocationThe Last Hop, Unit 2, Thames Edge Court, Clarence Street, Staines, TW18 4SUPrice£18.00 - £27.00Races8 racesDistances5km, 10km, Half Marathon, Marathon, UltraTop ratedThis is a five star eventLast chance to enterRace day is fast approachingBook NowShareShare this eventMidweek Chasing Numbers Feb 2026Fri 6th February 20265.0·3 reviewsCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an exhilarating running experience at the Midweek Chasing Numbers event, hosted by Saturn Running on Friday, February 6, 2026! This unique midweek run invites participants of all abilities to challenge themselves along the stunning banks of the River Thames. With a flexible start time ranging from 7:30 AM to 3:30 PM, you can choose your perfect running slot. The event features a 7-hour time challenge, allowing you to run as little as 3km or as much as 50km, depending on your personal goals.Whether you're training for a marathon or simply aiming to complete a fun run, the Midweek Chasing Numbers event is designed to be inclusive and affordable. Members can enjoy entry for just £20 and will receive a mini-me medal pin for every event attended. Plus, with a fantastic aid table, goody bags, and special recognition for milestone achievements, this event promises to be a memorable day for all participants. Don't miss out on this opportunity to push your limits and enjoy a vibrant running community!Read moreOrganiser's DescriptionAbout The EventJoin us for a Chasing Numbers midweek run. The aim of our midweek runs is to hold events as cheaply as possible in order for people to be able to build their numbers or simply to help people achieve their own goals at an affordable price.The midweek runs are fully flexible with start times available from 7.30am with time to run all the way to 3.30pm! Now that's a FLEXIBLE FRIDAYAll abilities are welcome at our super-inclusive events and we also welcome walkers too.The £20.00 entry price is available only to members. You can become a member by logging into your profile and purchasing one of the membership schemes. As well as the cheapest entry price, a member is guaranteed entry to the run and gets a mini-me medal pin at every event they attend!Distance & TimeThis is a 7 hour Time Challenge event which allows you full control over the distance you want to run. The minimum distance is 3km, which is part of a lap. A full lap is 4.37 miles or 7km (this is subject to change to a 3.28 mile loop depending on course conditions) and you can run as many, or as few, of these laps as you like subject to UK athletics age restrictions (please see the restrictions below). We will have a 3k turn point and 5k turn point within the lap which is available to allow our younger runners to comply with the UK Athletics distance rules. It is 3 laps for a half marathon, 6 for a marathon and 7 or over for an ultra, we also have a 50km distance available.CourseThe course along a beautiful stretch of the River Thames. It is fully signed so no navigational experience is required. The course is mainly well made paths and tarmac with a very short grass section. Road shoes are suitable all year round.The regular course is subject to some change near the time of the event depending on our health and safety review of the course.Medals & PinsAs long as you complete 3km, you will be an official finisher and you will receive one of our fantastic medals! We also have distance pins available for anyone who runs a half-marathon distance or more. Pins available to collect are our Half-Marathon, Mega-Half, Marathon, 50km and Ultra pins. You will receive one pin on the ribbon of your medal to reflect your achievement.Goody BagAbsolutely! You earned the calories after all, so all our finishers get a goody bag. As well as our standard goody bag, with a selection of various goodies to choose from, we also have a fabulous vegan goody bag for our vegan runners.Aid TableWe have a fantastic aid table and drinks table at the start / finish of each lap. There is a great selection of savoury, sweet, fruit, cheese and vegan items at the aid table.Special EventIf our event is a special event for you, for instance your 50th, 99th, 100th, 200th, 250th etc., half or full marathon then please let us know. You will receive a special running number with your name on it and the date of the event.Venue FacilitiesToilets are available at the start / finish area inside the venue HQ. There are changing facilities available at the venue. These facilities will be open from 8am and will be available for use throughout the entire event.Car ParkingThere is parking available in Bridge Street Car Park in Staines, TW18 4TP.Race Day Instructions & Number Pick UpFinal race day instructions will be sent out via email in the week leading up to the event and will include full race day information. Number pick up is on the day at race registration before the start of the event.Other InformationThere will be somewhere to leave a bag at the start / finish area. However, this is not a secure bag and the items cannot be watched at all times so please do not leave anything valuable.Headphones are allowed.Our running events are held on public footpaths so we would be grateful if all runners were considerate of other path users.Read moreRaces10kmFri 6th February 2026 (08:30)£27.00Trail Running Association (TRA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than
