## Event 49: https://findarace.com/events/phoenix-tiaras-tutus

- **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/phoenix-tiaras-tutus
- **Date**: PHOENIX - Tiaras & Tutus Orange RunFrom Phoenix RunningSat 17th January 2026LocationWalton-on-Thames, SurreyPrice£22.00 - £40.00Races6 racesDistances5km, 10km, Half Marathon, Marathon, UltraLast chance to enterRace day is fast approachingPopularBooked 7 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventPHOENIX - Tiaras & Tutus Orange RunSat 17th January 2026Copy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryJoin us for the vibrant PHOENIX - Tiaras & Tutus Orange Run, hosted by Phoenix Running, on Saturday, January 17th, 2026, in the picturesque Walton-on-Thames, Surrey! This exciting event brings back the beloved Tiaras & Tutus theme, featuring a fabulous new orange medal that will have you sporting your best tutus as you run along the scenic riverside towpath. Choose your adventure with race distances ranging from 5km to ultra marathon, completing laps on a beautiful 5.3km course. Whether you’re a seasoned runner or just starting out, this event welcomes all abilities, allowing you to be a finisher with just one lap or challenge yourself to go the distance within the 7-hour time limit.Experience the unique flexibility of Phoenix Running's 'rock up and run' start times, giving you the freedom to begin your race within a one-hour window. With affordable pricing options, including a new 'no medal' entry for just £20, you can enjoy all the usual Phoenix support without breaking the bank. Don't miss this opportunity to participate in a fun, supportive environment where everyone is a winner! Spaces are limited, so be sure to secure your spot for thisRead moreView allPHOENIX - Tiaras & Tutus Orange RunSat 17th January 2026Organiser's DescriptionOur very popular Tiaras & Tutus Run is back and this time with a brand new orange coloured medal so it's time to don those tutus once again and go for a run - these medals are epic.This is one of our timed events where you choose - from 5km to 10k, half to marathon to ultra, you can complete as many, or as few, laps as you like of a 5.3km, 3.28 mile, out and back, course along the beautiful river Thames.  We welcome all abilities of runner - complete one lap and you're both a finisher and a winner in our eyes, or keep going and see how far you can get in the full 7 hour time limit and everyone gets the fabulous bespoke finishers' medal.We also operate our uniquely flexible 'rock up and run' start times within a 1 hour start window, so no waiting around if you arrive early and no added pressure if you're running a bit late.  If you have any special start time requirements then just get in touch because depending on the distance you are planning to run we can pretty much accommodate all start time requests.Whilst Phoenix Running is very much about the bling and our medals are second to none, we're also acutely conscious that events can be expensive to enter and times are tough right now.  We're now in our 12th year of fixed prices for our standard themed events, but to try and do our bit we're trailing a 'no medal' entry option at a flat £20.  We cover our base costs, you get a run with all the usual Phoenix support, epic aid station, same day results published, athletes profiles, Phoenix World, digital BIBs, digital finishers certificates, etc, etc but without a medal we can keep the price low for this entry option.Not sure?  Well the beauty of this format is that you don't even need to decide in advance - simply come and run a lap and see how you feel on the day and keep on going if it feels right.Places will be strictly limited so don't leave it too late to enter.Minimum distance is 3.28 miles or as far as you can go in 7 hours. Off road trail, the terrain is riverside towpath. The overall course has no elevation gain / loss making this a flat and fast route.Read moreRacesPhoenix - 5k£27.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - 10k£29.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Half Marathon£37.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Marathon£39.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Ultra Marathon / 7-Hour Timed Event£40.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketNo Medal - Phoenix - 7-Hour Timed Event£22.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketBookPhoenix - 5k£27.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - 10k£29.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Half Marathon£37.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Marathon£39.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketPhoenix - Ultra Marathon / 7-Hour Timed Event£40.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketNo Medal - Phoenix - 7-Hour Timed Event£22.00UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketCourse DetailsCourse DetailsPhoenix Running offer two different courses from the Weir in Walton.  The 'Blue Bridge' route takes you west along the Thames, passing over the small 'Blue Bridge' before passing under Walton Bridge before returning.  The 'Hampton Court' route takes you east along the times in the direction of 'Hampton Court', passing Sunbury Locke on the way before returning.  Both courses are along scenic towpaths and have no elevation gain / loss making them both fast and flat routes.  The course will be confirmed one week prior to the event and dependent on factors such as planned local events and the weather.ZoomWhat's IncludedWhat's IncludedPhoenix Running operate welcoming and inclusive events for those for all ability levels.Fabulous Bespoke Finishers’ Medal!Epic Aid Station!Flexible Start Times!Chip Timing with online live resultsResults e-mailed to participants and uploaded to website same dayDigital Finishers certificate available on Phoenix World siteAthlete profile available on Phoenix World siteHow to Get ThereHow to Get ThereThe race is around 6 miles from the closest M25 junction.Walton Station is around a 2.5 mile walk away.ParkingParkingFree parking is available a short 5 minute walk from the start line.  Details are provided in the final event information which is emailed a week prior to the event.LocationLocationThe event will start in the small field just before The Weir Pub on Waterside Drive or on the patio of the Weir Pub depending on the weather.  Just look for the Phoenix Running marquee, you can't miss it!FAQsFAQsWhat do you stock at the Aid Station?Selection of jelly sweets, crisps and pretzels along with Freddos.  There is a good chance of cake and, if the weather is hot, ice lollies as well.  Vegan options are clearly marked.  Water, variety of squash and cola will be available.Are spectators welcome?Spectators are more than welcome!  The Weir Pub is located right next to the start and finish line so offers an extremely convenient cheering on point for spectators.  The Weir offers cold drinks, hot drinks and food from 11am.  There is also a café located at the nearby Xcel Leisure Centre.  The Weir offers hotel rooms for those wishing to stay over but please book early as it always sells out.How many laps for my distance?Complete 1 lap and you’re both a finisher and a winner in our eyes! Complete 4 laps for a half marathon distance. Complete 8 laps for a full marathon distance, recognised by the 100 Marathon Club. Complete 9+ laps for an ultra marathon distance.Flexible Start times?We offer flexible start times, simply come to the start area ready to run so we can check you in and set you off as soon as you’re ready.Toilets and Facilities?There are toilets in the Weir Pub, which will be open for our use from 8:00am.  There are also toilets in the Xcel Leisure Centre that are available from 7:00am.Can I leave my bag at the aid station?We have no ‘official’ baggage check in facility; however you can leave small bags and your own items in the grassy area nearby at your own risk – these will be easily accessible to you during and throughout the event.What is the event cut-off?The cut-off time is 3:30pm by which time you should be finished running.Who are Phoenix Running?Phoenix Running are now in their 11th year of organising events in the South-East of England.  We’re all about being inclusive; it’s about great routes, great medals and above else, great people.  We believe it’s all about encouraging and enabling runners to achieve things that perhaps they once thought were impossible, whether that’s your first 5km, 5 mile or 50 km run, we’ll support you to success.  Small steps lead to bigger things and everyone starts at the beginning. We recommend all entrants to our events join these groups.For any specific event changes we will always EMAIL all entrants as the primary communication channel.However, please do join our Facebook chat groups as we post news / interim updates / weather updates and much more.PHOENIX RUNNING COMMUNITYTREATING CUSTOMERS FAIRLY (TCF) OR IN THIS CASE TREATING RUNNERS FAIRLY (TRF)TREATING CUSTOMERS FAIRLY (TCF) OR IN THIS CASE TREATING RUNNERS FAIRLY (TRF)On entering Phoenix Running Ltd events you are entitled to the following;FREE DEFERALS - You can transfer or defer any race entry (once) to a future event up to 1 week (7 days) prior to the original event date (subject to availability). This is our ‘no questions asked’ commitment to our runners. There are no deferral fees, no online forms to fill out and there is no onus on you to find another runner to take your place (unless, of course, you want to). You can defer an event once, just email us and we’ll do the rest. Simple, easy and fair.FREE TRANSFERS - You can, if you prefer, transfer your entry to another runner up to 1 week (7 days) prior to the event date (subject to availability). Simply email us the new runner’s full contact details prior to the transfer deadline and include any club affiliation and medical conditions.PREGNANT DEFERALS – We allow pregnant & postpartum mums to defer their event for up to three years. This also applies to adopting parents.1 WEEK (7 DAY) DEADLINE - Race numbers are not transferable to another event or another runner within 1 week (7 days) of the race date.DEADLINE EXCEPTIONS – There are no exceptions to shortening the 7 day deadline. From time to time Phoenix Running hold special events or events with a larger than usual capacity. We reserve the right to extend the 1 week deadline for transfers and deferrals for such events. This will be made clear in the specific terms and conditions for those events.REFUNDS - The entry fee is non-refundable.ReviewsDone this event?First-hand event reviews from people who have been there, done that and got the breathable T-shirt, really help everyone choose their next event.Add a reviewLocationWaterside Drive, Walton-on-Thames, KT12 2, United KingdomWho's InSee who else is doing this eventBe the firstNo-one has marked this event as 'Doing' or 'Maybe' yet. - **Price**: PHOENIX - Tiaras & Tutus Orange RunFrom Phoenix RunningDateSat 17th January 2026LocationWalton-on-Thames, Surrey£22.00 - £40.00Races6 racesDistances5km, 10km, Half Marathon, Marathon, UltraLast chance to enterRace day is fast approachingPopularBooked 7 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventPHOENIX - Tiaras & Tutus Orange RunSat 17th January 2026Copy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryJoin us for the vibrant PHOENIX - Tiaras & Tutus Orange Run, hosted by Phoenix Running, on Saturday, January 17th, 2026, in the picturesque Walton-on-Thames, Surrey! This exciting event brings back the beloved Tiaras & Tutus theme, featuring a fabulous new orange medal that will have you sporting your best tutus as you run along the scenic riverside towpath. Choose your adventure with race distances ranging from 5km to ultra marathon, completing laps on a beautiful 5.3km course. Whether you’re a seasoned runner or just starting out, this event welcomes all abilities, allowing you to be a finisher with just one lap or challenge yourself to go the distance within the 7-hour time limit.Experience the unique flexibility of Phoenix Running's 'rock up and run' start times, giving you the freedom to begin your race within a one-hour window. With affordable pricing options, including a new 'no medal' entry for just £20, you can enjoy all the usual Phoenix support without breaking the bank. Don't miss this opportunity to participate in a fun, supportive environment where everyone is a winner! Spaces are limited, so be sure to secure your spot for thisRead moreView allPHOENIX - Tiaras & Tutus Orange RunSat 17th January 2026

## Event 50: https://findarace.com/events/edinburgh-marathon-festival

- **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/edinburgh-marathon-festival
- **Date**: Edinburgh Marathon Festival5.0·3 reviewsFrom GSi Events LtdSat 28th May 2022 - Sun 29th May 2022Time09:00LocationEdinburgh, MidlothianPrice£13.50 - £133.75Races9 racesDistances5km, 10km, 10 Mile, Half Marathon, MarathonShareShare this eventEdinburgh Marathon FestivalSat 28th May 2022 - Sun 29th May 20225.0·3 reviewsCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready to lace up your running shoes for the Edinburgh Marathon Festival, taking place on the stunning streets of Edinburgh, Midlothian, from Saturday, May 28th to Sunday, May 29th, 2022! Organised by GSi Events Ltd, this exciting event promises to be Scotland's biggest running festival, offering a variety of race distances to suit all abilities. Participants can choose from the marathon, half marathon, Team Relay, 10k, 5k, and even a Junior race, making it an inclusive and exhilarating experience for everyone.Join thousands of fellow runners and experience the electric atmosphere as you navigate through breathtaking routes that showcase the beauty of Edinburgh. With its reputation for selling out every year, the Edinburgh Marathon Festival is not just a race; it's a celebration of community and fitness. Don’t miss your chance to be a part of this unforgettable weekend of running and fun!Read moreView allEdinburgh Marathon FestivalSat 28th May 2022 - Sun 29th May 2022Organiser's DescriptionMake 2022 your year to Run in Edinburgh. The Edinburgh Marathon Festival takes place on 28/29 May and has marathon, half marathon, Team Relay, 10k, 5k & Junior distances to choose from. Scotland’s biggest running festival combines an unrivalled atmosphere with spectacular routes, it's no wonder it sells out every year!RacesEdinburgh MarathonSun 29th May 2022 (02:00)£64.00CLOSEDEMF Half MarathonSun 29th May 2022 (08:00)£39.75CLOSEDEMF Hairy Haggis Team RelaySun 29th May 2022 (10:00)£133.75CLOSEDEdinburgh Marathon Festival 10KSat 28th May 2022 (09:00)£32.00CLOSEDEdinburgh Marathon Festival 5KSat 28th May 2022 (11:00)£21.25CLOSEDEdinburgh Marathon Festival Junior 5KSat 28th May 2022 (11:45)£21.25CLOSEDEdinburgh Marathon Festival Junior 2KSat 28th May 2022 (14:40)£13.50CLOSEDEdinburgh Marathon Festival Junior 1.5KSat 28th May 2022 (13:45)£13.50CLOSEDEdinburgh Marathon Festival Kids KilometreSat 28th May 2022 (13:00)£13.50CLOSEDBookEdinburgh MarathonSun 29th May 2022 (02:00)£64.00CLOSEDEMF Half MarathonSun 29th May 2022 (08:00)£39.75CLOSEDEMF Hairy Haggis Team RelaySun 29th May 2022 (10:00)£133.75CLOSEDEdinburgh Marathon Festival 10KSat 28th May 2022 (09:00)£32.00CLOSEDEdinburgh Marathon Festival 5KSat 28th May 2022 (11:00)£21.25CLOSEDEdinburgh Marathon Festival Junior 5KSat 28th May 2022 (11:45)£21.25CLOSEDEdinburgh Marathon Festival Junior 2KSat 28th May 2022 (14:40)£13.50CLOSEDEdinburgh Marathon Festival Junior 1.5KSat 28th May 2022 (13:45)£13.50CLOSEDEdinburgh Marathon Festival Kids KilometreSat 28th May 2022 (13:00)£13.50CLOSEDWhat's IncludedWhat's IncludedWicking finisher’s t-shirtAccurate chip timingTailored training planGreat finisher’s medalRace recovery packFantastic running experienceMInimum AgesMInimum AgesMarathon - 18yrsHalf Marathon -  17yrsHairy Haggis Relay - 15yrs10K - 15yrs5K - 11yrsKids KilometreKids KilometreThe EMF Kids’ Kilometre is for 3-6 year olds. The junior races are a fantastic way to get children active and encourage them to get involved in running. As with the 5K and 10K races, the routes will be run around the beautiful Holyrood Park.EMF KIDS KILOMETRE ROUTEStarting and finishing near the EMF Hub at Dynamic Earth, this beautiful, scenic route takes place in Holyrood Park in the heart of Edinburgh. The first 200m is a slight downhill section towards Queens Drive / Holyrood Gait Roundabout. Runners continue along the side of Dynamic Earth and run along Queens Drive with Holyrood Palace Gardens on their left and the steep slopes of Salisbury Crags on their right. The runners just about reach St. Margaret’s Loch on their right hand side before it doubles back on itself … you’ll have to come back later if you want to feed the ducks! On the return leg, runners can enjoy the views of Holyrood Palace, Calton Hill and Dynamic Earth and the Scottish Parliament Building. Once back through the first roundabout, the runners make a right turn into Holyrood Gait and the final 50m dash to the Finish Line!The Junior 1.5KThe Junior 1.5KThe 1.5K - The EMF Junior 1.5K is for 6-8 year olds. The EMF Junior races are a great way to get the whole family involved in Edinburgh Marathon Festival weekend. There are two Junior 1.5K races to choose from – Race 1 and Race 2. The only difference between these two races is their start time – choose which one suits you best!Race 1 at 1.45pm, Race 2 at 2pmEMF JUNIOR 1.5K ROUTEStarting and finishing near the EMF Hub at Dynamic Earth, this beautiful, scenic route takes place in Holyrood Park in the heart of Edinburgh. The first 200m is a slight downhill section towards Queens Drive / Holyrood Gait Roundabout. Runners continue along the side of Dynamic Earth and run along Queens Drive with Holyrood Palace Gardens on their left and the steep slopes of Salisbury Crags on their right. The runners just about reach St. Margaret’s Loch on their right hand side before it doubles back on itself … you’ll have to come back later if you want to feed the ducks! The route passes the 1 km mark and with the runners now more dispersed on the return leg, they can enjoy the views of Holyrood Palace, Calton Hill and Dynamic Earth and the Scottish Parliament Building. Once back through the first roundabout, the runners make a right turn into Holyrood Gait and the final 50m dash to the Finish Line!The Junior 2KThe Junior 2KThe 2K Junior - The EMF Junior 2K is for 9-12 year olds. The EMF Junior races are a great way to get the whole family involved in Edinburgh Marathon Festival weekend. There are two Junior 2K races to choose from – Race 1 and Race 2. The only difference between these two races is their start time – choose which one suits you best!Race 1 at 2.40pm, Race 2 at 3pmEMF JUNIOR 2K ROUTEStarting and finishing near the EMF Hub at Dynamic Earth, this beautiful, scenic route takes place in Holyrood Park in the heart of Edinburgh. The first 200m is a slight downhill section towards Queens Drive / Holyrood Gait Roundabout. Runners continue along the side of Dynamic Earth and run along Queens Drive with Holyrood Palace Gardens on their left and the steep slopes of Salisbury Crags on their right. The runners just about reach St. Margaret’s Loch on their right hand side before it doubles back on itself … you’ll have to come back later if you want to feed the ducks! The route passes the 1 km mark and with the runners now more dispersed on the return leg, they can enjoy the views of Holyrood Palace, Calton Hill and Dynamic Earth and the Scottish Parliament Building. Once back through the first roundabout, the runners make a right turn into Holyrood Gait and the final 50m dash to the Finish Line!THE JUNIOR 5KTHE JUNIOR 5KThe EMF Junior 5K is for 11-18 year olds and takes place on the Saturday. The event offers a picturesque route around Holyrood Park with stunning panoramic views of the city and Edinburgh Castle. It’s the perfect way for teenagers to get involved in EMF WeekendEMF JUNIOR 5K ROUTEStarting and finishing near EMF Hub at Dynamic Earth, this beautiful scenic route takes you around Holyrood Park in the heart of Edinburgh. The route offers stunning views of Edinburgh Castle and panoramic views of the city.Runners start on Queens Drive and after 0.5km the route passes St. Margaret’s Loch before starting the ascent around the east side of Arthur’s Seat before you pass Dunsapie Loch at around 2k. On the far side of Arthur’s seat and approaching the halfway point, runners can enjoy spectacular views of Duddingston Loch below and across the southside of Edinburgh and beyond towards the Pentland Hills with the knowledge that the rest of the route is flat and downhill! As the route continues down the West Side of this famous old volcano you can enjoy the views of the steep slopes on your right and in the distance the cheering masses at the final turn. The last 250m section runs back through the Start Line and then turns left onto Holyrood Gait and the Finish Line.THe 5KTHe 5KThe EMF 5K takes place on the Saturday and offers a picturesque route around Holyrood Park with stunning panoramic views of the city and Edinburgh CastleThe EMF 5K is a great start to your running journey and a chance to be part of the biggest running festival in Scotland.EMF 5K ROUTEStarting and finishing near EMF Hub at Dynamic Earth, this beautiful scenic route takes you around Holyrood Park in the heart of Edinburgh. The route offers stunning views of Edinburgh Castle and panoramic views of the city. Runners start on Queens Drive and after 0.5km the route passes St. Margaret’s Loch before starting the ascent around the east side of Arthur’s Seat before you pass Dunsapie Loch at around 2k. On the far side of Arthur’s seat and approaching the halfway point, runners can enjoy spectacular views of Duddingston Loch below and across the southside of Edinburgh and beyond towards the Pentland Hills with the knowledge that the rest of the route is flat and downhill! As the route continues down the West Side of this famous old volcano you can enjoy the views of the steep slopes on your right and in the distance the cheering masses at the final turn. The last 250m section runs back through the Start Line and then turns left onto Holyrood Gait and the Finish Line.The 10KThe 10KThe EMF 10K is an increasingly popular race distance, a great step-up for those who have completed a 5K in the past, but aren’t quite ready for the half marathon distance.EMF 10K ROUTEStarting and finishing near the EMF Hub at Dynamic Earth, this beautiful, scenic route takes place in Holyrood Park in the heart of Edinburgh. The route offers stunning views of Edinburgh Castle and panoramic views of the city.Runners start on Queens Drive with a 200m downhill section before heading past the steep sides of Arthurs Seat on your right and Dynamic Earth, The Scottish Parliament and Holyrood Palace on your left. Continuing on towards the 1km mark you will pass St. Margaret’s Loch before starting the ascent around the east side of Arthurs Seat. As you continue the clockwise route around this famous old volcano, you will pass Dunsapie Loch at around 2.5km. Now on the far side of Arthur’s seat at 3km runners can enjoy spectacular views of Duddingston Loch below and across the southside of Edinburgh and beyond towards the Pentland Hills. Approaching the 4km marker you will enjoy a panoramic view of the City. The route then doubles back on itself along Duddingston Low Road at 5km and through Duddingston Village at 6km. The route joins the Innocent Railway Road through 7km and 8km and then heads back up onto Queens Drive. Passing the 9km it’s now downhill all the way, back through the Start Line again and the final 250m dash to the cheering crowds and the finish line on Holyrood Gait.Hairy Haggis Team RelayHairy Haggis Team RelayThe Relay - The EMF Relay is a chance for a team of four to join forces and complete the Edinburgh Marathon! The 26.2 miles is split into 4 legs, each runner will run one leg and with each relay leg posing a different challenge, make sure you pick and order your team wisely; which lucky runner will get The Glory Leg?EMF TEAM RELAY ROUTEThe EMF Hairy Haggis Team Relay is an opportunity for a team of 4 runners to take part in the Edinburgh Marathon Festival. The 26.2 miles is split into 4 legs, each runner will run one leg. The approximate distance of each leg is shown below:Leg 1: The Royal Leg – 8.3 miles: Starting on Potterrow in the grounds of one of the oldest Universities in the world, this incredible city centre start takes you through the very heart of Edinburgh. As you pass Greyfriars Bobby on your left, you also take in the National Museum of Scotland on your right, before heading over the High Street and down The Mound. Don’t be surprised if you see a few shoppers stop and cheer as you then run past the Scottish National Gallery and into Princes Street Gardens, with the iconic Edinburgh Castle as your backdrop.The route then takes in the ‘Gothic Rocket’, otherwise known as the Scott Monument, as it meanders down the historic Royal Mile, heading towards and past the somewhat controversial Scottish Parliament building. Here you can look up and take in the spectacular Arthurs Seat & Salisbury Crags, as well as the Palace of Holyrood, before departing the Royal Park and heading east towards the coast where runners reach the first relay changeover at Fisherrow Links, Musselburgh.Leg 2: The Cock & Pan Leg – 5.5 miles: The route then crosses the River Esk and past Musselburgh Racecourse and then continues beyond Prestongrange Mining Museum, Prestonpans, the site of the 1745 Battle of Prestonpans, Cockenzie and Port Seton where reaching the second relay changeover at Port Seton Links.Leg 3: The Gosford Leg – 8 miles: It then races on to picturesque East Lothian, along Scotland’s golf coast which boasts 22 courses including some of the world’s oldest and most admired like North Berwick, Gullane and Muirfield. The route passes the village of Longniddry then enters the grounds of Gosford House before looping around and heading back to Port Seton and the final relay changeover.Leg 4: The Glory Leg – 4.4 miles: Now in the home straight the route passes through Port Seton, Cockenzie and Prestonpans back past Musselburgh Links, the famous golf course officially recognised by the Guinness Book of Records as the oldest golf course in the world. The marathon finishes in playing fields at Pinkie St Peter’s Primary School. Well done hairies!Half marathonHalf marathonDirectly preceding the Marathon, the Edinburgh Half Marathon follows the same fast and flat marathon course which was voted the fastest marathon in the UK by Runners World, ideal if it’s your first half marathon or you are looking for a PB. The Edinburgh Half Marathon has sold out every year since 2012 and is one of the largest half marathons in the UK.EMF HALF MARATHON ROUTEStarting on Potterrow in the grounds of one of the oldest Universities in the world, this incredible city centre start takes you through the very heart of Edinburgh. As you pass Greyfriars Bobby on your left, you also take in the National Museum of Scotland on your right, before heading over the High Street and down The Mound. Don’t be surprised if you see a few shoppers stop and cheer as you then run past the Scottish National Gallery and into Princes Street Gardens, with the iconic Edinburgh Castle as your backdrop. The route then takes in the ‘Gothic Rocket’, otherwise known as the Scott Monument, as it meanders down the historic Royal Mile, heading towards and past the somewhat controversial Scottish Parliament building. Here you can look up and take in the spectacular Arthurs Seat & Salisbury Crags, as well as the Palace of Holyrood, before departing the Royal Park and heading east towards the coast. As you reach Musselburgh, you’ll run past the oldest golf course in the world, where it’s documented that golf has been played as early as 1672, before heading further East on the flat towards the historic village of Preston Pans before turning and heading back towards the beautiful finish line within the vast playing fields of Pinkie School. With a descent of almost 90 metres to near sea level, we believe this is the fastest half marathon route in the world.Edinburgh MarathonEdinburgh MarathonFast & flat, this course was voted the fastest marathon in the UK by Runners World, ideal if it’s your first marathon or you are looking for a PB. The Edinburgh Marathon has sold out every year since 2008 and is the second largest marathon in the UK, behind only London. Enjoy a fantastic all-encompassing route which takes in some of Edinburgh’s most iconic landmarks as well as offering picturesque views along the East Lothian coastlineEDINBURGH MARATHON ROUTEStarting on Potterrow in the grounds of one of the oldest Universities in the world, this incredible city centre start takes you through the very heart of Edinburgh. As you pass Greyfriars Bobby on your left, you also take in the National Museum of Scotland on your right, before heading over the High Street and down The Mound. Don’t be surprised if you see a few shoppers stop and cheer as you then run past the Scottish National Gallery and into Princes Street Gardens, with the iconic Edinburgh Castle as your backdrop. The route then takes in the ‘Gothic Rocket’, otherwise known as the Scott Monument, as it meanders down the historic Royal Mile, heading towards and past the somewhat controversial Scottish Parliament building. Here you can look up and take in the spectacular Arthurs Seat & Salisbury Crags, as well as the Palace of Holyrood, before departing the Royal Park and heading east towards the coast. As you reach Musselburgh, you’ll run past the oldest golf course in the world, where it’s documented that golf has been played as early as 1672, before heading further East on the flat to Gosford House, an imposing neo-classical mansion which was one of the last great architectural commissions of the celebrated Scots architect, Robert Adam. As you turn and head back along the coast, you’ll see the beautiful finish line ahead, one of the greatest sights for any marathon runner! With a descent of almost 90 metres to near sea level, we believe that this is the fastest marathon route in the world.FAQsFAQsAll your queries answered and more!View the faqs hereTransportTransportRunners and Spectators can purchase an EMF Event Bus ticket to allow easy travel to and from the start and finish areas. On the day tickets will be significantly more expensive to purchase.It is in your best interests to get the earliest bus possible as this will reduce crowding and waiting time. Please note that the EMF Event Bus pick up point is a 25 min walk from the finish area.The start/finish area for the EMF Saturday races and the EMF Hub are located at Dynamic Earth (EH8 8AS) in Holyrood ParkThe start of the Edinburgh Marathon, Half Marathon and Team Relay is on Potterrow (EH8 9AL) in the city centre. Starting and finishing at the Millenium Arena this course laps the park making it easy to follow and great for any spectators to catch you flying around the course.What's IncludedWhat's IncludedUnique Themed MedalFree Race PhotosChip-timed resultsPost Race GoodiesHow to Get ThereHow to Get ThereAddress: Carriage Dr E, London SW11 4NJBY TRAINBattersea Park Railway Station is a 10 minute walk away from the Bandstand and has frequent services from Victoria.BY BUSBus services run frequently from Vauxhall Railway Station to Battersea Park.BY CARThere is a paid car park open to the public at the park.ParkingParkingThere will be paid parking available at Battersea Park as well as the surrounding area.on the dayon the dayBaggageWe will have a secure area where you can leave your baggage. We recommend you bring as little baggage as possible. Your baggage tag is attached to your race number, you simply tear it off and attach to your bag. To collect, show your number to the baggage tent staff and they will help you find your baggage.ToiletsBattersea Park itself has two sets of toilets, they are a little walk from the Start/Finish so ensure you take this into account. If you see a toilet on the way walking in, we recommend you use that.RefreshmentsThere will be water provided at the 5k point of the race, and there will also be water available at the finish. We have also ordered some flapjacks and bananas for when you finish 🙂During the raceThere will be marshals all around the course as well as km signs and arrows. The course is two laps for the 5k and 4 laps for the 10k on the roads/paths in the park. Please be aware that there will be spectators and members of the public using the park.SpectatorsYou are free to wander around the park so you can see where the competitors will be running. It is lapped so you will see them on numerous occasions.ResultsResults will be available on www.runthrough.co.uk after the race.PicturesWe will have a photographer taking a few snaps of the race and they will be on our Facebook page after the race so be sure to check it out and see if you can spot yourself.There WILL be pedestrians on the paths. We recommend you do not use earphones and you need to pay close attention to everything around you. Set to take place on Sunday, May 10th, 2026, in the picturesque town of Nantwich, Cheshire East, this exciting event has been a favorite among athletes for over 30 years. With race distances suitable for everyone—from complete novices to seasoned triathletes—it's the perfect opportunity to challenge yourself, support a charity, or simply have fun with friends and family.The Cheshire Triathlon is designed to create a welcoming and vibrant atmosphere for all participants and spectators alike. The course is spectator-friendly, allowing your loved ones to cheer you on as you navigate the swim, bike, and run segments. Plus, the event offers a range of attractions and facilities, including delicious food options, refreshments, and a play area for kids. Mark your calendars for this fantastic day out on May 10th, and join us in celebrating fitness, community, and fun!Read moreCheshire Triathlon 2026Sun 10th May 2026Organiser's DescriptionThe Cheshire Triathlon is set in Nantwich, Cheshire, and has been enjoyed by thousands of competitors for over 30 years now.The event is aimed at all abilities and fitness levels, from complete novice to club and pro triathlete, as well as charity and fun competitors.The course is spectator friendly for supporting family or friends and a fun and relaxed atmosphere is guaranteed.There will be attractions and facilities available at the park for the triathlon including hot and cold food, refreshments, toilets and a play area with swings and slides that all make for a great day out for the family!RacesCheshire Sprint 10th May£89.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketCheshire Super Sprint 10th May£89.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketCheshire Fun 10th May£89.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketCheshire Relay 10th May£99.00 perTeam3BookCheshire Sprint 10th May£89.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketCheshire Super Sprint 10th May£89.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketCheshire Fun 10th May£89.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketCheshire Relay 10th May£99.00 perTeam3Course DetailsCourse DetailsThe event starts with the swim in one of the only heated outdoor brine swimming pools in Great Britain, followed by a one-lap 21km cycle around the South Cheshire countryside until returning to the Barony Park in Nantwich for the final run, in and around the park itself. So helpful when you are wearing sunglasses and going through shadow and bright sunshine. Great course, not too challenging for a beginner but beautiful setting. Cycle route needs to be improved as it is on very busy roads on an awful road surface. I got a puncture 5km into the cycle which ruined the race for me.   Organisers were excellent thoughABAlex·2 years ago·VerifiedWell organised, good fun, massage tent, nice medal, good weather, perfect.Add a reviewLocationBarony Park, Barony Rd, Nantwich CW5 5QY, UKWho's InSee who else is doing this eventDoingHLHeath ListViewMaybeOmer DoganciViewCheshire Triathlon 2026Description·Races·Reviews·Who's In↑Back to topTopBook NowWhy book with us?Official Booking PartnerLowest Price GuaranteedSecure PaymentsAdd to your eventsLoadingLoadingPsst… mark an event as Maybe and we’ll remind you when it’s coming up.Event OrganiserUK TriathlonUK Triathlon has been organising events for 30 years and offers professionally run, fun & friendly nationwide events for all ages & abilities. We have fun duathlons & triathlons for kids, run events from 1/2 marathon to 5k for all the family and triathlons ranging from Fun & Sprint through to Long Distance...there really is something for everyone!  *Choose from indoor pool, outdoor pool, lake and sea swims. *Several distances to choose from. *Option to enter as a Relay Team and share the swim, bike & run with one or two friends.More from UK TriathlonOfficial PartnerOfficial Booking PartnerWe’ve teamed up with UK Triathlon to allow you to enter this event directly on findarace.com.Complete the entry form and secure card payment, we'll then instantly pass on your entry without you having to leave the site.Convenient huh. 👍

- **Price**: Cheshire Triathlon 20264.5·4 reviewsFrom UK TriathlonDateSun 10th May 2026Time08:00LocationNantwich, Cheshire East£89.00 - £99.00Races4 racesDistancesSuper Sprint, SprintTop ratedThis is a popular eventPopularBooked 21 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventCheshire Triathlon 2026Sun 10th May 20264.5·4 reviewsCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready to embrace the thrill of competition at the Cheshire Triathlon 2026, organised by UK Triathlon! Set to take place on Sunday, May 10th, 2026, in the picturesque town of Nantwich, Cheshire East, this exciting event has been a favorite among athletes for over 30 years. With race distances suitable for everyone—from complete novices to seasoned triathletes—it's the perfect opportunity to challenge yourself, support a charity, or simply have fun with friends and family.The Cheshire Triathlon is designed to create a welcoming and vibrant atmosphere for all participants and spectators alike. The course is spectator-friendly, allowing your loved ones to cheer you on as you navigate the swim, bike, and run segments. Plus, the event offers a range of attractions and facilities, including delicious food options, refreshments, and a play area for kids. Mark your calendars for this fantastic day out on May 10th, and join us in celebrating fitness, community, and fun!Read moreCheshire Triathlon 2026Sun 10th May 2026

## Event 53: https://findarace.com/events/ride-london

- **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/ride-london
- **Date**: Ford RideLondon4.5·1 reviewFrom London Marathon EventsSun 28th May 2023LocationLondonShareShare this eventFord RideLondonSun 28th May 20234.5·1 reviewCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an unforgettable day of cycling as the Ford RideLondon returns to the streets of London on Sunday, 28th May 2023! Organised by London Marathon Events, this year's event marks the 10th anniversary of the world's greatest festival of cycling, which was established as a legacy of the 2012 London Games. Participants can look forward to a vibrant atmosphere filled with enthusiastic cyclists of all levels, as the event features the Ford RideLondon FreeCycle, allowing families and friends to enjoy the iconic streets of central London, and the Ford RideLondon-Essex routes, offering challenging distances of 100, 60, and 30 miles.For those seeking adventure, the route will take cyclists through picturesque Essex before culminating in a grand finish back in the heart of London. Whether you're a seasoned rider or a casual cyclist, the Ford RideLondon promises a day of fun, fitness, and festivity. Don’t miss out on being part of this milestone event that celebrates cycling and community spirit!Read moreFord RideLondonSun 28th May 2023Organiser's DescriptionFord RideLondon, the world’s greatest festival of cycling, returns on Sunday 28 May 2023.It will be the 10th anniversary of the hugely popular event, which was established by the Mayor of London in 2013 as an Olympic legacy event from the 2012 London Games. It will be the second year the event visits Essex after a successful first edition in 2022.The 2023 edition will once again include the mass participation events Ford RideLondon FreeCycle in central London and the Ford RideLondon-Essex 100, 60 and 30, which all start in central London and head out into Essex before returning for a finish in the capital.RacesNo RacesThis event has no races available at this time.View similar eventsCourse DetailsCourse DetailsThe RideLondon-Essex 100 is a unique opportunity to ride 100 miles on traffic-free roads through the heart of London and neighbouring Essex. It will start and finish in the centre of the capital, with 60 miles of rolling roads through some of Essex’s most beautiful countryside and villages in between. The full route will be announced in early 2022.ReviewsVerified PurchaseA 'verified' purchase review means that we've verified that the person writing the review purchased their entry to this race on findarace.com.543214.51 reviewTDThomas·3 years agoThis event was really good, we got to ride on closed off motorways and nice country roads all the way to the Essex/Suffolk boarder and back. It was great cycling in central London without the traffic and there were plenty of rest stops provided which gave out refreshments and saw to mechanical problems. The only issues I had were with pedestrians crossing parts of the course inappropriately and when we got the the main rest stop in Felsted there were only two coffee vans operational when there should have been 6, this meant that we were waiting in a very long queue for 20 minutes or more for coffee when we could have been back on the road.Read moreAdd a reviewLocationLondonWho's InSee who else did this eventSteliyan EnchevViewFord RideLondonDescription·Races·Reviews·Who's In↑Back to topTopThis event has taken placeCongratulations to everyone who took part. ## Event 54: https://findarace.com/events/oxford-half-marathon

- **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/oxford-half-marathon
- **Date**: OXFORD HALF MARATHON4.2·3 reviewsFrom Limelight SportsSun 12th October 2025LocationOxford, OxfordshireShareShare this eventOXFORD HALF MARATHONSun 12th October 20254.2·3 reviewsCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready to lace up your running shoes for the OXFORD HALF MARATHON, taking place on Sunday, 12th October 2025, in the enchanting city of Oxford, Oxfordshire! Organised by Limelight Sports, this event promises an unforgettable experience as participants navigate a traffic-free course that showcases the stunning scenery and rich history of Oxford.Runners will enjoy a superb half marathon route that meanders through the city's iconic landmarks and the breathtaking countryside, all set against the picturesque backdrop of the River Thames. With live entertainment at every mile, the OXFORD HALF MARATHON is not just a race; it’s a celebration of community and athletic spirit! Whether you're a seasoned runner or a first-time participant, join us for this exhilarating event and make lasting memories in one of the UK's most beautiful settings.Read moreOXFORD HALF MARATHONSun 12th October 2025Organiser's DescriptionA fantastic half marathon through the City of Oxford and surrounding countryside on a traffic-free course.A superb route taking in the iconic Oxfordshire scenery, the wonderful landmarks and the 'dreaming spires' of this beautiful City, against the the spectacular backdrop of the River Thames with live entertainment at every mile.RacesNo RacesThis event has no races available at this time.View similar eventsReviewsVerified PurchaseA 'verified' purchase review means that we've verified that the person writing the review purchased their entry to this race on findarace.com.543214.23 reviewsSLSamuel·3 years ago·VerifiedFantastic event and very well organised. Big shoutout to Find a Race. Couldn’t recommend them more for anyone looking to get booked on to their first half marathon.Vladimir·6 years agoReliably good, scenic and fast Half. The weather this time was something completely special and unique, so let's take it away from the equation. Generally, I’m happy how the race was organised. With about 7000 participants running, Virgin Sports did a good job keeping the race safe and providing water & electrolytes on the course. The course was quite flat, maybe a bit too much packed into the same streets, with dual-way running. I think my only concern is, since the race village was in the park and it was raining cats & dogs, the entire territory around tents was wet and muddy. Even with gravel covered pathways, it was barely possible to get in or out without collecting the mud on your feet. Thinking out loud, some recycled plastic/rubber mats could to the job, if properly placed on the pathways… But I get it, maybe it was hard to predict & plan such things in advance. Same as with the transportation: when you put thousands of people into a small historical city, you'd obviously expect some heavy traffic & delays.Read moreKate·6 years agoGreat run, course could be better?. First off, the weather REALLY affected the run this year. It was terribly wet, and had been wet for a while before the run, so at some points water lay ankle-deep across the road. I would have liked to have seen the grass protected at University Parks, with those underfoot grids you see used. There was a wait at the start if you were right at the back, but with 12k runners, that’s acceptable. The first few miles wind around the town but then you have the first of two out-and-back drags, up to Summertown and back. Then there’s another long drag out to the village of Marston, which made for quite boring running. Luckily, after about mile 9/10 you head back into town to finish up. The t shirt and medal were great, decent goody bag too, and despite the awful wet, people were so upbeat, both runners and supporters. This race would score five stars if only the course was better! Those out and back drags are there to make up distance and they’re not fun to run. I know councils really affect road closures and in oxford of course you have colleges too but PLEASE, could this race change to have a more imaginative and central route? Even just knocking out the Marston drag would be great.Read moreAdd a reviewLocationOxford United Football Club, Grenoble Road, Littlemore, Oxford, UKWho's InSee who else did this eventCHCaitlin HolroydViewmmzViewOllie RodriguezViewOXFORD HALF MARATHONDescription·Races·Reviews·Who's In↑Back to topTopThis event has taken placeCongratulations to everyone who took part. Hosted by the renowned RunThrough Events, this lively race invites participants to shake off the winter blues with a refreshing jog through one of London’s most beautiful parks. The flat course in Hyde Park is ideal for both seasoned runners looking to chase a personal best and those who just want to enjoy a scenic run with friends in the crisp February air.With races starting at 9:30 AM, each kilometre is clearly marked, and friendly marshals will be there to guide and cheer you on every step of the way. After crossing the finish line, enjoy a vibrant event village filled with post-race refreshments and exciting freebies from our partners to celebrate your achievement. Each participant will receive a unique themed medal, free race photos, and chip-timed results, making this an event not to be missed. Join us for a fantastic day of fitness and fun at the HYDE PARK 5K & 10K!Read moreHYDE PARK 5K & 10K February 2026Sat 21st February 2026Organiser's DescriptionJoin us for Hyde Park 5k & 10k on Saturday 21st February 2026!Shake off the winter blues with a refreshing run through one of London’s most picturesque parks. Hosted by the renowned RunThrough Events, this lively race invites participants to shake off the winter blues with a refreshing jog through one of London’s most beautiful parks. The flat course in Hyde Park is ideal for both seasoned runners looking to chase a personal best and those who just want to enjoy a scenic run with friends in the crisp February air.With races starting at 9:30 AM, each kilometre is clearly marked, and friendly marshals will be there to guide and cheer you on every step of the way. After crossing the finish line, enjoy a vibrant event village filled with post-race refreshments and exciting freebies from our partners to celebrate your achievement. Each participant will receive a unique themed medal, free race photos, and chip-timed results, making this an event not to be missed. Join us for a fantastic day of fitness and fun at the HYDE PARK 5K & 10K!Read moreHYDE PARK 5K & 10K February 2026Sat 21st February 2026

## Event 57: https://findarace.com/events/run-battersea-park-10k

- **URL**: https://findarace.com/events/run-battersea-park-10k
- **Date**: RUN Battersea Park 10K Winter5.0·1 reviewFrom Run UKSun 8th February 2026Time10:30LocationBattersea Park (next to the Bandstand)- Post Code SW11 4NGPrice£27.80Races1 raceDistances10kmTop ratedThis is a five star eventLast chance to enterRace day is fast approachingPopularBooked 216 times recentlyBook NowShareShare this eventRUN Battersea Park 10K WinterSun 8th February 20265.0·1 reviewCopy linkEmailMessagesWhatsappEvent summaryGet ready for an exhilarating day of running at the RUN Battersea Park 10K Winter, hosted by Run UK on Sunday, February 8th, 2026! This scenic race will take place in the stunning Battersea Park, known for its pancake flat, well-paved paths that make it perfect for both beginners and seasoned runners alike. As you navigate the beautiful loop around the park, you'll be surrounded by lush greenery, picturesque gardens, and breathtaking views of the River Thames, creating a peaceful yet motivating atmosphere.Participants can expect a fantastic race experience, complete with the latest chip timing technology to ensure accurate results that are posted immediately after you cross the finish line. Every finisher will be rewarded with a well-deserved medal and a selection of goodies including water, juices, fresh fruit, and a chocolate bar. With a supportive community of fellow runners and spectators cheering you on, this event promises to blend nature, city vibes, and a strong sense of camaraderie. Plus, enjoy the convenience of a free baggage drop, public toilets, and even a pre-race warm-up session to get you ready for the challenge ahead. Mark your calendars and prepare to make unforgettable memories at the RUN Battersea Park 10K Winter!Read moreOrganiser's DescriptionLace up your trainers and RUN Battersea Park 10k which will be a scenic and energizing experience. The park offers pancake flat, well-paved paths, making it ideal for both beginners and experienced runners. Soak in the beautiful views, surrounded by trees, gardens, and views of the River Thames, the route is both peaceful and motivating. The race will loop around the park, creating a supportive atmosphere with spectators and fellow runners nearby. It’s a great blend of nature, city vibes, and community spirit—perfect for chasing a personal best or simply enjoying the run.All runners will receive a medal for finishing as well as loads of goodies at the finish including water, juices, fruit and a chocolate bar! We use the latest Chip Timing technology so you will get an accurate finishing time. The results are posted as soon as you cross the finish line!We will have a free baggage drop facility for you to use. This area will be cordoned off but we do advise against storing any valuables as we can’t be held responsible. Public Toilets will also be available near the start/finish area. An official race photographer will be in attendance around the course so make sure they catch you when you are looking your very best!A Medical team will also be situated at the start area and around the course. We will also have a pre-race warm up around 10 minutes before the race start to warm up those tight muscles so you will be ready for the run. All in all we are ready – the question is; are you ready to take on the challenge?﻿Read moreRacesRUN Battersea Park 10K February 2026Sun 8th February 2026 (10:30)£27.80UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketBookRUN Battersea Park 10K February 2026Sun 8th February 2026 (10:30)£27.80UK Athletics (UKA) save £2.00Quick BookBooking for more than 1?Add to your basketReviewsVerified PurchaseA 'verified' purchase review means that we've verified that the person writing the review purchased their entry to this race on findarace.com.543215.01 reviewDVDavid·a month ago·VerifiedWell organised race, very easy to find and beautiful course. Every finisher will be rewarded with a well-deserved medal and a selection of goodies including water, juices, fresh fruit, and a chocolate bar. With a supportive community of fellow runners and spectators cheering you on, this event promises to blend nature, city vibes, and a strong sense of camaraderie. Plus, enjoy the convenience of a free baggage drop, public toilets, and even a pre-race warm-up session to get you ready for the challenge ahead. Mark your calendars and prepare to make unforgettable memories at the RUN Battersea Park 10K Winter!Read more
